### [1] [RoboHarness: A Simple Harness Outperforms VLA and World Actions Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14018)
*Bince Qu,Wei Chen,Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboHarness，一种为LLM智能体提供视觉-几何控制面板的机器人外挂框架，使编码智能体无需大规模动作数据训练即可直接理解并执行具身操作任务。在BEHAVIOR Challenge 2025任务上，Qwen3.8-Flash-Next配合RoboHarness超越当前最先进的VLA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型兴起推动了通用具身智能的发展，核心问题在于LLM如何在物理世界中行动。主流做法是训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型：收集机器人动作数据与视频演示、改造LLM架构加入动作头并进行大规模训练。然而该路线成本高昂且依赖大量动作标注数据。作者主张编码智能体（coding agents）本身具备实现通用具身操作的潜力，只需为其配备一个简单而有效的机器人外挂即可。

Method: 提出RoboHarness：为LLM智能体提供一个视觉-几何控制面板（visual-geometric control panel），使其能够直接理解并调用具身任务，而无需对模型进行动作头改造或大规模动作数据训练。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR Challenge 2025任务上，配备RoboHarness的Qwen3.8-Flash-Next性能超过当前最先进的VLA模型。实现将开源。

Conclusion: 编码智能体有望成为实现通用具身操作的可行路径，关键在于提供合适的机器人交互接口（harness），而非依赖端到端VLA训练。该工作为通用具身智能提供了区别于VLA范式的新思路。

Abstract: The rise of large language models (LLMs) has opened a path toward generalist embodied AI, raising the question of how LLMs can act in the physical world. One straightforward approach is training the model to generate actions: collect robot-action data and video demonstration, refine LLM architecture with an action head, and train a vision-language-action (VLA) model at scale. However, we argue that coding agents have the potential to realize generalist embodied manipulation. All they need is a simple yet effective robot harness. We present RoboHarness: a robotic harness that gives LLM agents a visual-geometric control panel so that they can directly understand and invoke embodied tasks. With RoboHarness, Qwen3.8-Flash-Next outperforms state-of-the-art VLA models on BEHAVIOR Challenge 2025 tasks. The implementation will be released as open source.

</details>


### [2] [LabInstruct: Benchmarking Situated Instructional Video Generation for Lab Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14020)
*Yuming Fu,Weijia Wu,Jing Chen,Jiahao Tang,Feifei Chen,Hongyu Zhu,Xin Jin,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LabInstruct基准，用于评估真实实验室场景下的情境化教学视频生成，涵盖5个学科204个任务，发现8个前沿图生视频模型虽视觉逼真但流程常出错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室（SDLs）理想上可由机器人直接执行AI生成的实验方案，但在复杂开放世界的实验室环境中，涉及细粒度操作、长时序流程及跨任务/装置的巨大差异，可靠自动化仍很困难。因此人类仍是AI方案与物理实验之间的重要执行接口，亟需清晰有效的人向实验指导。实验流程本质上是视觉、空间且动态的，视频特别适合传达装置配置、操作动作、时序依赖和状态变化。视频生成技术的进步使从初始工作区图像与自然语言指令直接合成实验演示成为可能，但这些模型能否可靠传达真实实验流程尚未被系统研究。

Method: 引入LabInstruct基准，用于真实实验室中情境化教学视频生成。该基准包含跨5个科学学科的204个任务，配有真实参考执行以及对象、动作、接触和状态转换的结构化标注。评估了8个前沿图像到视频（image-to-video）模型。

Result: 评估8个前沿图生视频模型后发现，视觉上看似合理的生成结果在流程上往往不正确，揭示了视觉真实性与实验指导所需可靠性之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 视频生成模型在真实实验室教学场景中尚不可靠；LabInstruct为系统评估该能力提供了基准，并暴露出视觉逼真与流程正确之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) aim to automate the full experimental loop, from scientific decision-making to physical execution. Ideally, AI-generated plans could be carried out directly by robotic systems, but reliable automation remains difficult in complex, open-world laboratory environments, where experiments often involve fine-grained manipulation, long-horizon procedures, and substantial variation across tasks and setups. Humans therefore remain an important execution interface between AI-generated plans and physical experiments, creating a need for clear and effective human-facing experimental guidance. Because laboratory procedures are inherently visual, spatial, and dynamic, video is particularly well suited to communicating apparatus configurations, manipulation actions, temporal dependencies, and state changes. Recent advances in video generation now make it possible to synthesize experimental demonstrations directly from an initial workspace image and a natural-language instruction. However, whether such models can reliably communicate real laboratory procedures has not been systematically studied. We introduce LabInstruct, a benchmark for situated instructional video generation in real laboratories. LabInstruct contains 204 tasks across 5 scientific disciplines, with real reference executions and structured annotations of objects, actions, contacts, and state transitions. Evaluating 8 frontier image-to-video models, we find that visually plausible generations frequently remain procedurally incorrect, revealing a substantial gap between visual realism and the reliability required for experimental instruction.

</details>


### [3] [OIL: An Object Interaction Layer for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14033)
*Pranay Dugar,Mohitvishnu S. Gadde,Rajesh Mangannavar,Aayam Kumar Shrestha,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出OIL（Object Interaction Layer），将全身控制器的运动学指令接口从机器人本体扩展到包含物体运动与接触，使单一学习策略无需任务特定重训练即可完成多种非抓取式物体交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式全身控制器虽能通过跟踪关节或末端执行器轨迹实现鲁棒自由空间运动，并为上游系统提供有用抽象，但该抽象主要局限于机器人自身运动。涉及物体交互时仍需任务特定控制器或重新训练，缺乏统一的物体级指令接口。

Method: OIL在标准全身控制器基础上增加以物体为中心的指令，将期望的物体运动与接触目标翻译为协调的全身行为，同时保持运动与平衡。该设计使单一学习策略能够直接从物体级运动学目标执行推动、拉动、双臂抓放及与运动耦合的搬运等非抓取式交互。

Result: 仿真中，物体级指令相较以机器人为中心的控制器提升了性能，并在物体质量、尺寸等属性变化下达到与任务特定策略相当的表现；进一步实现了对新行为组合的零样本泛化，并在Digit人形机器人上完成sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 将物体级运动学与接触指令纳入全身控制接口，可在不进行任务特定重训练的情况下统一多种物体交互任务，并具备良好的泛化性与实机迁移能力。

Abstract: Recent progress in learned whole-body controllers for humanoid robots has enabled robust free-space motion by tracking kinematic targets such as joint or end-effector trajectories. This interface provides a useful abstraction for upstream systems, including motion capture, teleoperation, or generative models, which can specify desired motion without reasoning about forces or balance. However, this abstraction has largely been limited to robot motion, requiring task-specific controllers or retraining for object interaction. We introduce OIL, an Object Interaction Layer that extends the kinematic command interface of whole-body control to include desired object motion and contact. OIL augments a standard controller with object-centric commands and translates them into coordinated whole-body behavior while preserving locomotion and balance. This design enables a single learned policy to execute non-prehensile interactions, including pushing, pulling, two-arm pick-and-place, and locomotion-coupled transport, directly from object-level kinematic targets without task-specific retraining. In simulation, we demonstrate that object-level commands improve performance over robot-centric controllers while matching task-specific policies across variations in object properties such as mass and size. We further demonstrate zero-shot generalization to novel behavior combinations and sim-to-real transfer on the Digit humanoid robot.

</details>


### [4] [Building Trustworthy Household Robots through Open-World Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14034)
*Yu-Lin Tsai,Muxi Lyu,Roei Herzig,Koushik Sen,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这是一篇立场论文，提出家庭机器人应追求"开放世界安全性"（open-world safety），即在未见情境下仍能维持既定安全要求；文章分析了现有安全方法失效的原因，并提出涵盖安全要求指定、学习、落地、可靠恢复与泛化评估的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型扩展了机器人的能力，使其能处理多样物体、复杂指令与复杂环境，家庭机器人正走向日常生活。但杂乱、动态的家庭环境会让机器人遭遇陌生物体、任务与交互，可靠的零样本任务完成仍具挑战。作者因此主张从安全视角出发，机器人不仅要完成任务，还要在未见过的情况下（包括无法完成任务时）保留既定安全要求。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）形式：先论证开放世界安全性的必要性，再剖析现有安全方法为何无法提供此类保护，指出三类失效途径——上下文被误解、忽视过往交互、以及在不熟悉条件下变得不安全的停止与恢复策略。随后提出研究议程：指定（specify）、学习（learn）、落地/锚定（ground）安全要求，并以可靠恢复强制执行，同时评估这些保护的泛化能力。

Result: 作为立场论文，本文不报告实验结果，而是给出诊断性结论：现有安全方法在上下文理解、跨动作/任务的历史交互建模、以及陌生条件下的停止与恢复策略方面存在缺口，因而无法保证开放世界安全性。

Conclusion: 呼吁家庭机器人的安全保护应超越训练与开发所依赖的情境、上下文与历史，在其之外仍保持可靠，从而实现开放世界安全性（open-world safety）。

Abstract: Foundation models are expanding robot capabilities, enabling robots to handle more diverse objects, follow sophisticated instructions, and operate in complex environments. These advances bring us to a future in which robots become part of everyday household life. However, cluttered, dynamic household environments also expose robots to unfamiliar objects, tasks, and interactions, making reliable zero-shot task completion an ongoing challenge for robotics research.
  From a security perspective, we argue for open-world safety: household robots should preserve specified safety requirements in unseen situations, including when they cannot complete the requested task. Safety protections must account for context and interactions that persist across actions and tasks. In this position paper, we examine why existing safety methods can fail to provide this protection. Protection can fail through misinterpreted context, overlooked past interactions, or stopping and recovery strategies that become unsafe under unfamiliar conditions. We therefore propose a research agenda to specify, learn, and ground safety requirements, enforce them with reliable recovery, and evaluate the generalization of such protections. We call for household robots whose safety protections remain reliable beyond the situations, context, and history used to train and develop them, to achieve open-world safety.

</details>


### [5] [Residual Compliant Policy: Learning Compliant Policies from Few Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14035)
*Chunyu Jiang,Yichen Cai,Shuai Li,Wenbin Sun,João Carvalho,Kostas Daniilidis,Oleg Arenz,Cristiana de Farias,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个使柔顺性在各阶段显式化的框架，结合数字孪生示教、Laplacian编辑与仿真轨迹优化，以及残差强化学习，在翻转和推动任务上取得40/40的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作要求机器人既要在自由空间精确以建立预期接触，又要在接触后保持柔顺以避免过大作用力。模仿学习在较简单任务上有效，但演示中的柔顺性不可直接观测，难以从演示数据中推断。

Method: 框架使柔顺性在每个阶段显式化：（1）数据采集时，操作者遥操作真实机器人，同步数字孪生可视化指令参考与实际位姿的误差，使当前柔顺性可见，并在两种离散柔顺模式间选择；（2）为泛化到不同物体位姿，结合Laplacian编辑（高效重定向轨迹但接触时非物理一致）与基于仿真的轨迹优化（保证物理合理性但缺乏人工引导）；（3）残差强化学习策略通过调整底层导纳控制器的参考位姿来精炼交互力。

Result: 在Franka FR3上评估三项翻转与推动任务，基于跨任务共7次演示。方法在全部40次分布外试验中成功，两个基线分别为33/40和24/40，仅基础策略达到35/40。

Conclusion: 将柔顺性在数据采集、轨迹泛化与策略精炼各阶段显式化，可显著提升接触丰富操作在分布外场景下的成功率。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires a robot to be precise in free space to establish the intended contacts, and compliant once in contact to avoid excessive forces. Imitation learning has proven effective for simpler manipulation tasks, but the compliance underlying a demonstration is not directly observable and is therefore difficult to infer from demonstration data alone. We present a framework that makes compliance explicit at every stage. During data collection, the operator teleoperates the real robot while a synchronized digital twin visualizes the error between the commanded reference and the achieved pose, making the current compliance visible, and selects between two discrete compliance modes. To generalize across object poses, we combine Laplacian editing, which retargets trajectories efficiently but is not physics-consistent during contact, with simulation-based trajectory optimization, which enforces physical plausibility but offers no manual guidance. Finally, a residual reinforcement learning policy refines the interaction forces by adjusting the reference pose of the underlying admittance controller. We evaluate on three flipping and pushing tasks on a Franka FR3 based on a total of 7 demonstrations across tasks. Our method is successful on all 40 out-of-distribution trials, compared to 33/40 and 24/40 for two baselines, with our base policy alone reaching 35/40.

</details>


### [6] [From bits to atoms: A survey of cross-layer safety in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14038)
*Tuo Feng,Yue Zhang,Shiji Zhou,Zhaoxin Fan,Wenguan Wang,Xiaowei Chi,Tianyu Shen,Xiangtai Li,Xuesong Bai,Zheng Zhu,Sirui Han,Boyu Zhou,Zhaosong Wang,Shizhe Chen,Zilong Zheng,Chenjia Bai,Jiangmiao Pang,Yaodong Yang,Hao Zhao,Di Hu,Li Yi,Yan Huang,Qi Wu,Hanwang Zhang,Fei Gao,Shanghang Zhang,Hongsheng Li,Jianye Hao,Yuan Zhang,Yingjie Xia,Jian Shen,Cewu Lu,Hui Zhang,Kai Chen,Wenwu Yu,Badong Chen,Jungong Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述以四层参考架构（物理交互层、系统与中间件层、算法与决策层、云边与数据层）组织具身AI安全研究，串联系统栈中的漏洞、传播路径与防护措施，并倡导软硬件协同对齐以构建可信具身系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身人工智能（Embodied AI）打通数字与物理世界，数字故障与攻击可直接造成物理伤害，产生信息物理（cyber-physical）风险，因此需要一套跨系统栈的统一安全框架来系统梳理风险来源、传播机制与防护手段。

Method: 采用综述（survey）方法，提出并以此贯穿全文的四层参考架构：物理交互层、系统与中间件层、算法与决策层、云边与数据层；据此（1）追溯安全范式演进，（2）分析故障与攻击如何跨越架构边界导致物理后果，（3）系统化梳理复合攻击链，（4）归纳多层防御策略，（5）评述代表性具身安全基准（benchmarks）。

Result: 形成了跨层贯通的具身AI安全知识体系：明确各层漏洞与跨层传播路径，归纳出复合攻击链与多层防御策略框架，并整理出可用于评测的代表性具身安全基准，填补了单一层面研究难以刻画跨层风险传导的空白。

Conclusion: 作者主张将软件层面的对齐（software alignment）与严格的硬件约束（hardware constraints）统一起来，以构建能够在以人为中心的环境中安全运行的可信具身系统。

Abstract: Embodied Artificial Intelligence (AI) introduces cyber-physical risks because digital failures and attacks can cause physical harm. This survey organizes embodied AI safety through a four-layer reference architecture -- comprising the physical interaction, system and middleware, algorithm and decision, and cloud-edge and data layers -- and uses it to connect vulnerabilities, propagation paths, and safeguards across the system stack. It traces the evolution of safety paradigms and examines how faults and attacks cross architectural boundaries to produce physical consequences. Furthermore, the survey systematizes composite attack chains, outlines multi-layered defense strategies, and reviews representative embodied safety benchmarks. Ultimately, we advocate for unifying software alignment with rigorous hardware constraints to build trustworthy systems capable of operating safely within human-centric environments.

</details>


### [7] [RAIN: Robotic Task Generalization with Region-Aware Interaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14039)
*Seunghun Lee,Kyuhwan Shim,Yeogyeong Song,Seung Hwan Kim,Sihaeng Lee,Byoung-Tak Zhang,Sunghoon Im,Gunhee Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Region-Aware Interaction Networks (RAIN)，将任务建模为目标条件子任务序列，实现技能复用与跨配置泛化，并引入参考轨迹重定向策略RRS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽受益于大规模视觉-语言预训练，但倾向于记忆训练轨迹，难以将学到的交互泛化到新任务与场景配置。

Method: 将任务表示为一系列目标条件子任务，每个子任务由动作类型与目标区域掩码指定；Target-adaptive Cross-View Encoder (TCE)以目标掩码为条件融合多视角特征并跨视角交换信息；PlanDiT预测目标导向轨迹与动作；Transition Head估计子任务完成度以实现自动切换；Reference-based Retargeting Strategy (RRS)利用已录轨迹合成对新目标区域的平滑接近动作，无需额外采集数据。

Result: 在新基准LIBERO-Analogy及真实桌面任务上，RAIN的泛化能力优于现有VLA基线方法。

Conclusion: 目标条件子任务分解与区域感知建模可有效缓解轨迹记忆问题，提升机器人任务与场景配置的泛化性。

Abstract: Existing vision-language-action (VLA) models benefit from large-scale vision-language pretraining, but tend to memorize training trajectories, limiting their ability to adapt learned interactions to new task and scene configurations. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{Region-Aware Interaction Networks (RAIN)}, which formulates a task as a sequence of goal-conditioned subtasks, each specified by an action type and a target-region mask. This formulation allows learned skills to be reused across different objects and scene layouts and composed into task sequences not seen during training. Its Target-adaptive Cross-View Encoder (TCE) conditions multi-view features on target masks and exchanges information across views, while PlanDiT predicts target-directed trajectories and actions. A Transition Head estimates subtask completion to enable automatic transitions. We also introduce a \textbf{Reference-based Retargeting Strategy (RRS)}, which uses recorded trajectories to synthesize smooth approaches to reference interactions with alternative target regions, without collecting additional data. Experiments on \textbf{LIBERO-Analogy}, our new benchmark for robotic task generalization, and real-world tabletop tasks demonstrate that RAIN generalizes more robustly than existing VLA baselines.

</details>


### [8] [Reinforcement Learning from Real-Robot Interaction without High-Quality State Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14042)
*Ryan Gao,Ashvin Anilkumar,Nicholas Corrado,William Cong,Josiah Hanna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究针对机器人在无外部仪器环境下直接从物理经验学习时状态不可观测的问题，在双足机器人踢足球任务中结合无迹卡尔曼滤波与循环状态空间模型（RSSM），提出RSSM-RLPD方法，实现仅用机载传感、30分钟真机交互即达100%进球成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人强化学习的长期目标是让机器人像人和动物一样从自身物理经验中学习。核心障碍在于：在非仪器化的真实环境中，世界状态对学习器通常不可观测，必须依靠机载传感估计；而估计误差会同时污染策略输入和奖励信号，导致学习困难。

Method: 以双足机器人学习射门这一接触密集任务为研究对象，用无迹卡尔曼滤波（UKF）在无机载外仪器（无动捕、无顶置摄像头）条件下估计关键状态变量。由于UKF在球未被观测时估计可能出错，作者用循环状态空间模型（RSSM）学习新的状态表示，且仅在被观测到球时才修正RSSM的隐状态。在RLPD基础上提出RSSM-RLPD，应用于NAO机器人带球射门任务，并辅以仿真对比实验。

Result: 机器人经过30分钟真实世界交互后，能够从多个起始位置学会进球，成功率达100%，而相同训练预算下的前馈策略成功率为0%。仿真研究进一步表明，RSSM-RLPD所学策略的成功率高于行为克隆、前馈和GRU基线。

Conclusion: 在现有真机学习方法中加入可学习的状态表示，使强化学习能够仅凭真实机器人数据、即便在高度部分可观测的情况下也能有效进行。

Abstract: A longstanding goal for robot reinforcement learning (RL) is that robots learn from their own physical experience, as humans and animals do. A key obstacle to learning directly from physical experience, especially outside instrumented lab environments, is that the state of the world is usually unobservable to the learner and must be estimated from on-board sensing. Errors in that estimate corrupt both the policy input and potentially the reward signal. We study this challenge using the contact-rich task of a bipedal robot learning to score soccer goals. We estimate all key state variables on-board with an unscented Kalman filter (UKF) and no external instrumentation (e.g., motion capture or overhead cameras). Because the UKF may incorrectly estimate state when the ball is unobserved, we learn a new state representation with a recurrent state-space model (RSSM) that, critically, only corrects the RSSM's latent state when the ball is observed. Building on RLPD, we introduce RSSM-RLPD and apply it to the task of a NAO robot learning to dribble a soccer ball into a goal. We show that the robot learns to score from multiple starting positions after 30 minutes of real-world interaction, reaching 100% success against 0% for a feed-forward policy under the same training budget. We complement our physical robot experiments with a simulation study and show that RSSM-RLPD learns policies that achieve a higher success rate than behavior cloning, a feed-forward, and GRU baseline. Taken together, our results show that the addition of a learned state representation to existing on-robot learning methods enables RL from real-robot data even with a high degree of partial observability.

</details>

*Thanh Ly,Chien Le,Truong-Duy Dang,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Duy-Dung Le,Vien Anh Ngo,An T. Le*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需运动捕捉重定向、生成式运动先验与蒸馏流程的完整人形机器人运动系统：用感知高程网格上的地形分类器路由五个专家，并用基于模型的落足点规划器在插值地形代价场上经六步闭式梯度细化落足点。在部署级仿真器中，系统以90%（18/20）成功率完成85米五段航路，四个单段达20/20；去掉专家混合或细化后，楼梯与跨栏段成功率为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的感知式人形运动系统普遍依赖重定向动作捕捉、生成式运动先验与蒸馏阶段，流程复杂且依赖大规模参考运动数据。作者希望验证：不依赖该流水线，仅靠感知驱动的地形分类、专家混合策略与模型化落足点优化，能否让机器人在多样地形上稳定行走。

Method: 系统由三部分构成：(1) 五专家混合策略，由基于感知高程网格的地形分类器进行路由；(2) 基于模型的落足点规划器，为每个落足点指定目标；(3) 在插值地形代价场上进行六步闭式梯度细化，取代从固定网格中选取落足点。整套系统运行于与实机一致的感知—执行软件栈，并在第二个部署级仿真器中进行评测。作者还进行了匹配的组件消融研究。

Result: 在85米五段航路端到端测试中成功18/20次（90%，Wilson 95% CI 70–97%），其中四段单独评测达20/20。移除专家混合与细化的消融配置在楼梯段和跨栏段成功率均为0%。连续细化使72.4%的落足点跨越至少一个网格边界，实现了真实的亚网格修正。组件研究显示：单一专家在训练分布上与五专家持平；四种无标签路由器均退化为近似均匀激活；自监督地形嵌入劣于特权标签。

Conclusion: 不依赖动作捕捉重定向、运动先验和蒸馏流程，仅通过地形感知专家混合与模型化落足点梯度细化，即可实现稳健的感知式人形运动。性能增益主要来自连续细化带来的亚网格落足点修正，而非专家数量、路由器复杂度或自监督地形表示。

Abstract: State-of-the-art perceptive humanoid locomotion is mostly built on retargeted motion capture, a generative motion prior, and a distillation stage. We report a complete system that does not require this pipeline for a humanoid to cross varied terrain. In its place, we provide a five-expert mixture routed by a terrain classifier over a sensed elevation grid, and target every foothold with a model-based planner, refined by six closed-form-gradient steps on an interpolated terrain-cost field instead of picked from a fixed grid. Driven through the same perception-to-actuation software a robot would run, inside a second, deployment-fidelity simulator, it crosses an $85$\,m five-segment waypoint course $18$ of $20$ times end-to-end ($90\%$, Wilson $95\%$ CI $70$--$97$) and succeeds $20/20$ on four of five segments scored alone, where an ablated configuration without the mixture and without refinement reaches $0\%$ on both the stair and the hurdle segment. The continuous refinement moves $72.4\%$ of foot placements across at least one grid-cell boundary, which produces real sub-grid correction. We also ran matched component studies to report what does not account for it: one expert equals five on the training distribution, four label-free routers collapse to near-uniform activation, and a self-supervised terrain embedding loses to the privileged label.

</details>


### [10] [Do My Samples Help? Compute Budget Allocation for Real-World Sampling-Based MPC in Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14064)
*Magnus Dierking,João Carvalho,Vince Kurtz,Jan Peters,Nils Dengler,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过BAMPC框架系统研究了接触丰富操作中采样型模型预测控制（SMPC）在有限算力预算下的分配权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMPC借助GPU物理仿真器可实时评估大量不可微的接触轨迹，但其算力预算有限，可用于更密集的控制探索、表征动力学或状态不确定性、更频繁重规划或改善仿真与物理状态对齐等多个竞争方向。这些权衡在真实世界的sim-to-real控制中尚未被充分理解。

Method: 提出基于GPU的Budget-Allocating Model Predictive Control（BAMPC）框架，运行于MuJoCo Warp，将动力学不确定性、状态不确定性与时间对齐作为算力预算的三个独立维度，并在仿真与真实机器人上使用相同的控制栈以支持受控比较。

Result: 在三个接触丰富的操作任务中，最优预算分配取决于主导的sim-to-real误差来源；动力学随机化（dynamics randomization）并非普遍最有效的策略。

Conclusion: SMPC的算力预算分配需针对具体任务中主导的sim-to-real误差进行针对性调整，不存在通用最优的分配方案。

Abstract: Sampling-based model predictive control (SMPC) is increasingly practical for contact-rich manipulation because GPU-based physics simulators enable real-time evaluation of large numbers of non-differentiable contact rollouts. However, this computation budget is finite and can be spent in competing ways, including denser control exploration, representing dynamics or state uncertainty, replanning more frequently, or aligning the simulation better with the physical state. Yet, the resulting trade-offs are not well understood in real-world sim-to-real control. In this work, we address this question through a systematic study of how to allocate a fixed computation budget for SMPC in contact-rich manipulation. To enable controlled comparisons, we develop a GPU-based framework named Budget-Allocating Model Predictive Control (BAMPC), which runs on MuJoCo Warp and exposes dynamics uncertainty, state uncertainty, and temporal alignment as independent dimensions of the computation budget while using the same control stack in simulation and on a real robot. Across three contact-rich manipulation tasks, we find that the best budget allocation depends on the dominant sim-to-real error, with dynamics randomization not universally the most effective one.

</details>


### [11] [Safety-Oriented Trajectory Ranking from BEV Perception Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14066)
*Leon Pohl,Martin Frauendorfer,Corinna Schmitt,Mirko Maehlisch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化轨迹排序流水线，基于BEVFusion的BEV检测与地图分割结果，按安全优先规则对候选自车轨迹排序，并与21名参与者的人类偏好对比，一致率达84.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量、可解释且直接作用于BEV输出的候选轨迹评估方法，对车辆安全工作流、快速原型开发和人在环路验证仍有价值，而现有方法在这方面尚不充分。

Method: 构建模块化轨迹排序流水线，输入为nuScenes数据经BEVFusion得到的物体检测与地图分割；对人工设定的候选轨迹按安全优先准则排序：优先避免碰撞与人行道（步道）侵占，其次最小化到目标的末端距离，最后考虑路径效率。另设计7个BEV交通场景，通过21名参与者的问卷调查获取成对偏好，与规则排序结果进行对比。

Result: 在146次成对投票中，124次与评估器排名第一的轨迹一致，总体一致率为84.9%。

Conclusion: 简单的BEV中心安全启发式可在受限轨迹选择任务中近似人类偏好，同时保持足够的透明度，适用于分析、数据集筛选与自动驾驶研究。

Abstract: Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) representations are increasingly used as compact inputs for autonomous driving perception and planning. However, lightweight and interpretable methods for evaluating candidate ego trajectories directly on BEV outputs remain useful for vehicular safety workflows, rapid prototyping, and human-in-the-loop validation. This paper presents a modular trajectory ranking pipeline that consumes BEVFusion object detections and map segmentations derived from nuScenes sensor data and ranks manually specified candidate trajectories according to safety-first criteria. The ranking prioritizes avoiding collisions and walkway violations, then smaller end distance to the target, and finally path efficiency. To study whether these rule-based rankings resemble human judgment, we created seven BEV traffic scenarios and compared the script output with preferences from a survey of 21 participants. Across 146 pairwise votes, 124 matched the trajectory ranked first by the evaluator, corresponding to an overall agreement of 84.9 %. The results indicate that simple BEV-centric safety heuristics can approximate human preferences in constrained trajectory choice tasks while remaining transparent enough for analysis, dataset curation, and autonomous driving studies.

</details>


### [12] [From Hitter to Player: Separating Motor Execution and Strategy in Competitive Humanoid Table Tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14067)
*Fanrong Dong,Ziyi Sun,Ziqi Han,Junhan Sun,Lei Ye,Zetong Jing,Hao Zhao,Shaoxun Liu,Rongrong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将乒乓球任务解耦为"击球执行"与"落点决策"两个子问题，以落点目标为接口；通过运动自博弈提升执行能力，再冻结运动层、用策略自博弈学习落点选择，最终在真机上完成11分完整对局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞技类人形乒乓球需要两种不同能力：按意图完成回球（运动控制）与决定回球落点（策略）。现有工作往往将二者混在一起训练，难以厘清执行能力与战术决策各自的贡献，因此作者希望显式解耦，并验证策略层单独变化对比赛结果的影响。

Method: 采用三阶段分解式训练框架，以"球的落点目标"作为运动控制与策略之间的接口：(1) 训练以期望落点为条件的全身运动策略，通过基于模型的击球规划器与学习到的控制器将落点映射为可执行的人形挥拍动作；(2) 在闭环回合中进行运动自博弈，使机器人应对由另一名"对手"产生的来球而非仅限规定来球，此阶段落点仍随机采样、不考虑对手，只提升执行质量；(3) 冻结整个运动栈，通过竞争性自博弈训练击球级别的策略网络来选择落点。

Result: 运动自博弈同时提升了落点精度与合法回球率。更关键的是，在运动栈完全冻结的情况下，仅更换落点选择策略即造成显著比赛结果差异：所学策略对中心、随机、远离对手三种基线分别赢得89%、82%、73%的分数。该策略还会根据对手位置与上一拍的落点改变回球落点，说明自博弈学到的并非简单地打向固定的"好位置"。在硬件上，同一套冻结的执行栈与策略接口支持与业余人类对手完成完整的11分对局，包含正反手多拍回合。

Conclusion: 以落点为接口解耦运动与策略是可行且有效的：分层训练既能提升执行可靠性，又能学到超越固定瞄准点的战术行为；冻结执行栈后的策略替换实验证明战术层是决定比赛胜负的关键变量，并成功迁移到真人对局。

Abstract: Competitive humanoid table tennis requires two distinct abilities: executing a desired return and deciding where to return the ball. We separate these two problems, with the ball landing target serving as an interface between motor control and strategy. (1) We first train a whole-body motor policy conditioned on the desired landing target, which maps the target to an executable humanoid stroke through a model-based strike planner and a learned controller. (2) We then improve the motor policy through self-play in closed-loop rallies, where the robot learns to handle incoming balls generated by another player rather than only prescribed incoming balls. At this stage, the landing targets are still sampled without considering the opponent, so the policy improves its execution without learning game strategy. (3) Finally, we freeze the entire motor stack and train a stroke-level strategic policy to choose landing targets through competitive self-play.
  Motor self-play improves both landing accuracy and legal-return rate during rallies. More importantly, with the entire motor stack fixed, changing only the target-selection policy leads to large differences in game outcomes: the learned strategy wins 89%, 82%, and 73% of points against center, random, and away-from-opponent baselines, respectively. The learned strategy also changes where it sends the ball according to the opponent's position and the previous target, showing that self-play learns more than simply aiming at fixed "good" locations. On hardware, the same frozen execution stack and strategic interface support complete 11-point games against a recreational human opponent, including multi-stroke rallies with both forehand and backhand returns.

</details>


### [13] [Assessing the Marginal Utility of Exocentric Head Views in UMI-Style Large-FoV Wrist-Camera Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14075)
*Yuhui Duan,Shi Jin,Yuntian Wang,Jin Wang,Siao Liu,Wenchang Gao,Zhaxizhuoma,Yan Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在真实双臂桌面操作平台上通过控制变量实验重新审视"多视角必然更好"的假设，发现当腕部相机已能观测物体、夹爪与目标区域时，增加头部视角并不能稳定提升任务完成度；只有提供集中且任务相关的信息（如ROI裁剪）时，头部视角才带来更稳定的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角感知常被默认为机器人操作的安全设计选择，但"相机越多越好"这一假设缺乏在真实双臂平台上的受控验证。作者希望厘清视角数量与视角信息质量对策略性能的真实影响。

Method: 在真实双臂桌面操作平台上采用控制变量实验，对比仅用两个大视场角（FoV）腕部相机训练的策略，与额外引入头部视角的策略；覆盖三个桌面任务，并进一步操纵腕部相机FoV、对头部图像进行ROI裁剪，以及开展数据规模缩放实验。

Result: 增加头部视角并未稳定提升任务完成度或执行质量；当腕部相机已观测到物体、夹爪和目标区域时，仅腕部策略即为强基线。限制腕部FoV会提升头部视角的潜在价值，但ROI裁剪带来的增益比未裁剪图像更稳定（后者受背景和无关区域主导）。数据缩放显示FoV与演示数量存在交互：宽视角覆盖更广但需要更多数据才能稳定，数据充足时可能达到更高性能上限。

Conclusion: 相机设计应被视为任务相关性与信息密度问题，而非单纯输入视角数量的堆叠。

Abstract: Multi-view perception is often treated as a safe design choice for robot manipulation: if one camera is useful, more cameras should be better. We revisit that assumption in tabletop bimanual manipulation by comparing policies trained with two large-field-of-view (FoV) wrist cameras against policies that additionally receive a head view, using controlled-variable experiments on a real bimanual platform. Across three tabletop tasks, adding a head view does not consistently improve task completion or execution quality: when the wrist cameras already observe the objects, grippers, and goal regions, wrist-only policies form a strong baseline. Restricting the wrist-camera FoV increases the potential value of a head view, but performance improves most reliably when the added view supplies concentrated, task-relevant information: region-of-interest (ROI) cropping yields more consistent head-view gains than the uncropped image, which is dominated by background and task-irrelevant regions. The data-scaling results further suggest an interaction between FoV and demonstration count: wide observations improve coverage but may require more data to stabilize, and they may reach a higher performance ceiling once enough demonstrations are available. These results suggest that camera design should be framed as a task-relevance and information-density problem rather than as a simple count of input views.

</details>


### [14] [SenseWeather: Multimodal Sensing for Robust Robotic Perception under Extreme Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14082)
*Fucai Ke,Sukai Huang,Junyi Hao,Kal Backman,Naim Rastgoo,Gholamreza Haffari,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SenseWeather多模态基准与Fusion-to-RGB方法，用RGB+D+IR融合在上游恢复任务兼容的RGB观测，在极端天气下无需改动下游模型即可保持机器人感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端环境（大雨、雪、尘、雾、黑暗）严重退化RGB观测，但机器人平台虽常搭载深度与红外等互补传感器，现代感知栈仍以RGB为中心，导致多模态机载传感与预训练下游模型之间存在错配。研究目标是在不修改或重训练下游感知栈的前提下，用互补传感在上游恢复任务兼容的RGB观测。

Method: 1) 构建SenseWeather：受控多模态机器人感知基准，覆盖雨、雪、尘、雾、黑暗，提供配准的RGB、深度、红外观测、干净参考及带标注的高层感知任务。2) 提出Fusion-to-RGB，两种参数高效的传感器融合实现：面向扩散Transformer的token空间融合，以及面向inpainting UNet的残差潜变量条件化。

Result: 在冻结的开放词汇定位模型与视觉语言模型上，RGB+D+IR融合在所有成对下游对比中均优于对应的RGB-only恢复方法，包括全部十组检测器对比中的AP50提升。感知收益与常规图像保真度指标并不一致，且天气严重程度增加不会导致下游性能单调退化。

Conclusion: 互补传感在上游融合可保留极端条件下可复用的机器人感知能力；同时表明任务级感知鲁棒性是独立于视觉恢复质量的优化目标。

Abstract: Robotic perception must remain reliable when extreme environmental conditions severely degrade the primary RGB observation. Although robotic platforms commonly carry complementary sensors such as depth and infrared, much of the modern perception stack remains RGB-centric, creating a mismatch between multimodal onboard sensing and pretrained downstream models. We study whether complementary sensing can be used upstream to recover a task-compatible RGB observation that preserves existing perception capabilities without modifying or retraining the downstream stack. To this end, we introduce SenseWeather, a controlled multimodal benchmark for robotic perception under severe rain, snow, dust, fog, and darkness, with registered RGB, depth, and infrared observations, clean references, and grounded high-level perception tasks. We further develop Fusion-to-RGB, with two parameter-efficient sensor-fusion realizations based on token-space fusion for a diffusion transformer and residual latent conditioning for an inpainting UNet. Across frozen open-vocabulary grounding and vision-language models, RGB+D+IR fusion improves all paired downstream comparisons over the corresponding RGB-only restorers, including AP50 gains across all ten detector comparisons. Importantly, these perception gains do not consistently follow conventional image-fidelity metrics, and increasing weather severity does not induce monotonic downstream degradation. These results show that complementary sensing can preserve reusable robotic perception under extreme conditions, while highlighting task-level perception robustness as an objective distinct from visual restoration quality.

</details>


### [15] [When pose graphs are not enough: a failure analysis of pose-graph-based camera estimation for Gaussian splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14090)
*Senthil Palanisamy,Azwad Sabik,Alan Dolhasz,Mira Slavcheva*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对房地产航拍高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）采集的特定轨迹结构，尝试仅用稠密位姿图优化（PGO，未知量仅为相机位姿）替代联合位姿-点优化的光束法平差（BA），构建了专用SfM系统。结果：速度优于COLMAP，但精度始终不及；作者以坦诚的"事后复盘"形式剖析失败原因，指出需引入Sim(3)约束、GPS绝对先验、保留结构与不确定性加权才能可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍高斯泼溅数据采集遵循固定配方：无人机单机环绕、多高度重叠航带、偶有垂直爬升，影像密集、时序化、带GPS与时间戳，且每面立面被多角度重复观测。这种高度结构化的采集方式蕴含大量回环，使得"仅优化位姿"的PGO（未知量远少于BA的位姿-点联合问题）在理论上极具吸引力。然而PGO在机器人领域多用于后处理（在已有较优轨迹上用回环重分布漂移），而泼溅采集把"每帧与数十帧闭环"推到极限。作者由此提出核心问题：纯位姿优化能否达到BA精度？

Method: 1) 构建专门适配该采集结构的SfM系统（而非通用SfM工具），以验证假设。2) 采用稠密位姿图优化，未知量仅为相机位姿，边由两视图相对位姿及GPS约束构成，配合贪心合并与手工设定权重。3) 与COLMAP进行速度与质量对比。4) 对失败原因进行系统性剖析，识别实现层面（手工权重、贪心合并、噪声GPS标尺）与公式固有层面（SE(3)边缺乏尺度自由度、两视图边无法表征单个3D点对所有观测帧的耦合）两类问题。

Result: 该系统在速度上优于COLMAP，但在重建质量上始终未能超越。精度不足源于多重交互原因：手工设定的权重、贪心式合并策略、带噪的GPS标尺，以及两条固有的公式局限——SE(3)边不携带尺度自由度，导致独立缩放的子图在合并时发生漂移；两视图边无法表达单个3D点对每个观测帧所施加的耦合约束。

Conclusion: 纯位姿优化在该类高回环泼溅采集场景下尚不能达到BA精度。可行的设计必须补回四项要素：Sim(3)约束、GPS绝对先验、保留显式结构，以及不确定性感知加权。本文以"坦诚事后复盘"的形式，为后续工作提供了明确的改进方向。

Abstract: Exterior Gaussian-splat twins for real-estate listings are captured to a fixed recipe: a consumer drone orbits a single property in several overlapping passes at stepped altitudes, with occasional vertical ascents, so the imagery is dense, sequential, GPS- and time-tagged, and revisits each facade from many angles. This structure is tantalizing: it suggests camera poses could be recovered by a dense pose-graph optimization (PGO) whose only unknowns are the poses, far fewer than the joint pose-and-point problem bundle adjustment (BA) solves. PGO is mature in robotics, but almost always as post-processing: it redistributes the drift of an already-decent trajectory once loop closures tie it together. Splatting captures push that regime to its limit, every frame closing loops with dozens of others, raising a clean question: does pose-only optimization reach BA accuracy? We built a system specialized to this capture structure, not a general-purpose SfM tool, to test it. It beat COLMAP on speed but never on quality; this paper is the candid post-mortem. The shortfall arises from several interacting causes, hand-set weights, greedy merging and a noisy GPS ruler, together with two limits inherent to the formulation: SE(3) edges carry no scale degree of freedom, so independently scaled sub-maps drift when merged, and a two-view edge stops short of the coupling one 3D point imposes on every frame that sees it. A viable design must add back Sim(3) constraints, absolute GPS priors, retained structure, and uncertainty-aware weighting

</details>


### [16] [Embodied Zero-Shot Task Planning via Hardware-Aware LLM-VLM Integration for Grounded Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14106)
*Lonnie Tankersley,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Soroush Korivand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向Franka Research 3机器人的硬件感知LLM-VLM操作框架，将符号推理与物理落地执行分离，在Isaac Sim中通过数字孪生实验验证多类操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽能将自然语言指令转化为看似合理的操作计划，但机器人实际执行需要物理落地的决策，必须考虑机器人本体构型、工作空间几何、可达性、碰撞约束与抓取可行性，而LLM本身无法可靠提供这类度量信息。

Method: 框架将符号推理与度量执行解耦：API托管的LLM规划器接收结构化场景摘要，仅生成符号任务信息（操作对象、目标关系、接近偏好、避让对象）；本地Qwen3-VL 8B VLM验证器用于歧义对象与开口容器替代物的语义确认。LLM与VLM均被禁止输出机器人坐标、抓取位姿、禁入区尺寸、夹爪指令、轨迹路点或成功标签，这些由确定性落地与执行模块负责，依赖机器人模型、场景几何、MoveIt 2碰撞检测、逆运动学、抓取验证、释放验证与最终视觉评估；执行反馈仅以符号上下文形式回传用于重规划。

Result: 通过数字孪生仿真试验评估，覆盖抓取、遮挡源访问、禁入区推理、关系放置与顺序操作等任务。

Conclusion: 该硬件感知LLM-VLM框架通过严格的符号-度量职责划分，使LLM/VLM不直接产生度量执行细节，从而提升操作计划的物理可行性与可靠性，并在仿真多任务中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Large language models can translate natural-language instructions into plausible manipulation plans, but robotic execution requires physically grounded decisions that respect robot embodiment, workspace geometry, reachability, collision constraints, and grasp feasibility. This paper presents a hardware-aware large language model-vision-language model (LLM-VLM) manipulation framework for a simulated Franka Research 3 robot in NVIDIA Isaac Sim. The framework separates symbolic reasoning from metric execution: an API-hosted LLM planner receives a structured scene summary and generates only symbolic task information, including the object to manipulate, target relation, approach preference, and objects to avoid. A local Qwen3-VL 8B VLM verifier supports semantic confirmation of ambiguous objects and open-container surrogates. Both the LLM planner and the VLM verifier are prohibited from generating robot coordinates, grasp poses, keepout dimensions, gripper commands, trajectory waypoints, or success labels. Instead, these decisions are handled by deterministic grounding and execution modules that use the robot model, scene geometry, MoveIt 2 collision checking, inverse kinematics, grasp validation, release verification, and final visual assessment. Execution feedback is returned only as symbolic context for replanning. The framework is evaluated through simulated digital-twin trials involving pickup, occluded-source access, keepout reasoning, relation placement, and sequential manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [17] [Make π0.5 Learn to Call Astra for Reliable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14117)
*Jianli Sun,Chengxiang Li,Yonglin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CALL轻量级头，让冻结的π0.5策略学会预测何时需要外部GPT-6 Astra协助，仅在关键恢复事件时激活，兼顾成功率与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT-6 Astra具备强具身推理能力，但频繁外部查询会显著增加推理时延与token开销，需要一种选择性调用机制来平衡性能与成本。

Method: 为冻结的π0.5策略配备轻量级CALL头，复用策略前向传播特征，在本地判断是否需要外部协助；Astra仅在关键恢复事件被激活，稳定后交还控制权给π0.5。

Result: 在40个LIBERO任务（200回合）上达到97.5%成功率，优于纯π0.5的95.5%基线；在Object、Goal、Spatial套件中，Astra仅在5.49%的环境步中执行，每个测试回合约消耗5,027个token。

Conclusion: 选择性干预在保留π0.5快速常规执行的同时按需集成Astra推理能力，实现了成功率与推理成本的更优权衡。

Abstract: GPT-6 Astra provides robust embodied reasoning, but frequent external queries increase inference time and token costs. We equip a frozen $π_{0.5}$ policy with a lightweight CALL head that learns to predict when assistance is necessary. CALL reuses features from the standard policy forward pass and operates locally without consulting external models. Astra is activated solely for critical recovery events and returns control to $π_{0.5}$ upon stabilization. Evaluated across 40 LIBERO tasks (200 episodes), our system achieves a 97.5% success rate, outperforming the 95.5% baseline of pure $π_{0.5}$. In the Object, Goal, and Spatial suites, Astra executes only 5.49% of environment steps, consuming approximately 5,027 tokens per test episode. This selective intervention preserves the fast ordinary execution of $π_{0.5}$ while seamlessly integrating Astra's reasoning on demand. Our project page is available at https://kykgeek.github.io/Pi05_CALL_Astra/.

</details>


### [18] [Refine Where It Matters: Contact-Adaptive Meshing for Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14142)
*Gabriel Johnson,Tom Olesch,Fei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出接触自适应网格细分框架，仅在接触处增加分辨率，在提升物理保真度的同时保持高效仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体操作需要精确建模高度局部化的接触，同时保持适合交互使用的仿真速度。均匀细四面体网格能解析这些交互，但在整个物体上引入冗余自由度；粗网格更高效，却可能无法充分解析接触引起的变形。

Method: 利用可变形体求解器已有的几何量、弹性量和接触量对候选边进行打分；采用全GPU并行流程选择无冲突子集，并在仿真循环内直接更新网格拓扑与物理状态。

Result: 在真实可变形物体操作序列上评估，自适应细分降低了sim-to-real误差，提升与细网格参考解的一致性，且效率显著优于均匀细网格仿真。

Conclusion: 局部细分能够在不付出全局细离散化代价的前提下提升物理保真度。该框架为交互式操作中的接触密集仿真、sim-to-real建模以及在线物理数字孪生提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Deformable-object manipulation requires accurate modeling of highly localized contact while maintaining simulation speeds suitable for interactive use. Uniformly fine tetrahedral meshes can resolve these interactions, but they introduce unnecessary degrees of freedom throughout the object. Coarse meshes are more efficient, yet they may under-resolve contact-induced deformation. We present a contact-adaptive mesh refinement framework that introduces spatial resolution only where it is most useful. The method uses geometric, elastic, and contact quantities already available from the deformable-body solver to score candidate edges. A fully GPU-parallel procedure then selects a conflict-free subset and updates both mesh topology and physical state directly within the simulation loop. We evaluate the approach on real deformable-object manipulation sequences. Adaptive refinement reduces simulation-to-real error, improves agreement with a fine-mesh reference, and remains substantially more efficient than uniformly fine simulation. These results show that local refinement can improve physical fidelity without incurring the cost of a globally fine discretization. The framework provides a practical basis for contact-rich simulation in interactive manipulation, sim-to-real modeling, and online physical digital twins.

</details>


### [19] [Axiom-0: Hierarchical Self-Refinement for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14155)
*Renbin Shu,Jing Yang,Xiangyuan You,Guolin Qiu,Yufei Xia,Yiming Zhu,Nanjun Dai,Fei Zhang,Feng Wu,Keke Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Axiom-0，一种通过分层自精炼提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型泛化能力的策略，在 RoboCasa365 上取得 62.32% 的整体成功率，超越已公布最高榜单分数 4.22 个百分点，且推理延迟仅增加 1.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的操作需要策略能够跨任务、布局和物体交互进行泛化。现有 VLA 模型虽结合了预训练多模态理解与学习控制，但在陌生任务组合上表现仍受限，其瓶颈既在于表征质量，也在于表征在动作生成中的使用方式。

Method: Axiom-0 采用分层自精炼框架，包含两个组件：IVRR（Intra-VLM Representation Refinement）在训练中通过一致性监督与学习到的特征投影强化选定的中间特征；VAIR（VLM-Action Interface Refinement）在动作生成前递归更新条件表征。二者与动作生成器联合优化，将表征质量与动作生成连接起来。

Result: 在 RoboCasa365 上，Axiom-0 整体成功率为 62.32%，超过最高已公布榜单分数（Paimon-0，58.1%）4.22 个百分点，超过 Xiaomi-Robotics-1 参考基线 4.92 个百分点。在所评估的性能剖析设置下，平均动作块生成延迟相对 Xiaomi-Robotics-1 仅增加 1.7%。

Conclusion: Axiom-0 在仅带来适度推理开销的前提下显著提升了任务完成率，表明分层自精炼是改善 VLA 策略泛化与性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Reliable manipulation requires policies that generalize across tasks, layouts, and object interactions. Vision-language-action (VLA) models combine pretrained multimodal understanding with learned control, but their performance remains limited on unfamiliar task compositions. We present Axiom-0, a VLA policy that addresses both the quality of its representations and their use in action generation through hierarchical self-refinement. Intra-VLM Representation Refinement (IVRR) strengthens selected intermediate features through consistency supervision with learned feature projections during training. VLM-Action Interface Refinement (VAIR) recursively updates the resulting conditioning before action generation. Joint optimization of the supervised VLM layers, VAIR, and the action generator connects the two components. On RoboCasa365, Axiom-0 achieves 62.32% overall success in our evaluation, exceeding the highest published leaderboard score (Paimon-0, 58.1%) by 4.22 percentage points and the Xiaomi-Robotics-1 reference by 4.92 points. Under the evaluated profiling setup, mean action-chunk generation latency increases by 1.7% over Xiaomi-Robotics-1. These results demonstrate improved task completion with modest inference overhead.

</details>


### [20] [Disentangling Task-Relevant Latent Dynamics for Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14159)
*Dayou Li,Jionghao Wang,Peihao Wang,Jiuzhou Lei,Hao Wang,Jichen Chai,Hezhen Hu,Minghui Zheng,Zhangyang Wang,Xin Li,Wenping Wang,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiTaR，一种从异构视频中学习稠密奖励的框架，通过在解耦的任务相关潜在空间中建模任务动态来生成更可靠的奖励，显著提升机器人操作策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中的强化学习奖励设计是一大瓶颈：手工稠密奖励需大量任务特定工程；而现有基于视频的奖励在纠缠的视觉空间中计算，任务相关状态变化与无关视觉变化混杂，导致奖励可能响应视觉干扰而非真实任务执行。

Method: DiTaR 通过非对称两阶段程序从多帧视频潜变量中学习连续的任务相关表示，抑制任务无关的视觉变化；在任务相关潜在空间中，训练一个以历史与语言为条件的整流流（rectified-flow）模型来捕捉任务动态，并基于动态兼容性的时间变化推导出方向性稠密奖励，从而直接建模任务相关动态而非通用视觉变化。

Result: 在 MetaWorld-v3 和 ManiSkill 上，DiTaR 相较基于视频的奖励基线大幅提升策略学习效果，并通过奖励重标注迁移到真实世界离线强化学习；奖励分析进一步表明 DiTaR 提供了更可靠的奖励。

Conclusion: DiTaR 表明在解耦的任务相关潜在动态空间中建模奖励，能够有效克服视频奖励对视觉干扰敏感的问题，为可扩展、数据驱动的奖励设计提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Reward design remains a major bottleneck for reinforcement learning in robotic manipulation. Handcrafted dense rewards can compensate for the limited guidance provided by sparse rewards, but require substantial task-specific reward engineering. Learning rewards from videos provides a scalable, data-driven alternative to manual reward design. However, existing video-based rewards are often computed in entangled visual spaces where task-relevant state changes are confounded with extraneous visual variation. As a result, the learned reward may respond to visual distractors instead of actual task execution. To address this limitation, we propose DiTaR (Disentangling Task-Relevant Latent Dynamics for Reward Modeling), a framework for learning dense rewards from heterogeneous videos. DiTaR learns a continuous task-relevant representation from multi-frame video latents through an asymmetric two-stage procedure that suppresses task-irrelevant visual variation. In the task-relevant latent space, we train a history- and language-conditioned rectified-flow model to capture task dynamics and derive a directional dense reward from temporal changes in dynamics compatibility. Together, these components enable reward modeling directly over task-relevant dynamics rather than generic visual variation. DiTaR substantially improves policy learning on MetaWorld-v3 and ManiSkill over video-based reward baselines, and transfers to real-world offline RL through reward relabeling. Reward analyses further show that DiTaR provides more reliable rewards.

</details>


### [21] [Observation-Referenced Tissue Deformation Forecasting with Tool-Driven and Residual Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14191)
*Tom Olesch,Nan Xiao,Xiongye Xiao,Jonathan C. DeLong,Fei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出观测参照（observation-referenced）的可微表面模型，用未来器械轨迹预测组织形变，显著优于绝对位置预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从未来器械运动预测组织形变可为预测性规划与日益自主的手术系统提供支持。直接预测绝对模拟位置对重建、初始化及持续仿真偏移敏感，需降低这种敏感性。

Method: 基于场景特定的可微表面模型，拟合到一段观测到的器械-组织交互窗口；采用观测参照公式，将每个组织点锚定到其最近一次有效观测，仅叠加由已知预规划未来器械轨迹诱导的形变；物理模型采用可微、无网格的基于位置的动力学（PBD），含场景特定刚性、附着、区域尺度与抓取耦合；可选引入观测参照残差模型，结合图谱空间模态与周期谐波动力学以建模器械输入未解释的结构化运动。

Result: 在三个数据集共12段手术操作视频上回溯评估。观测参照带来最大增益，相对对应绝对仿真预测的片段内误差比中位数为0.598；当残余运动具有周期结构时，可选周期残差进一步改善预测。

Conclusion: 观测参照形变预测可作为未来手术世界模型、数字孪生与生成式仿真器的实用基础模块，用于预测组织在器械作用下的演化。

Abstract: Forecasting tissue deformation from future instrument motion could support predictive planning and increasingly autonomous surgical systems. We study this problem using a scene-specific differentiable surface model fitted to an observed tool-tissue interaction window. Rather than forecasting absolute simulated positions, we introduce an \textit{observation-referenced} formulation that anchors each tissue point to its last valid observation and adds only the deformation induced by the known, preplanned future tool trajectory. This reduces sensitivity to reconstruction, initialization, and persistent simulation offsets while preserving the predicted deformation response. The physical model uses differentiable, mesh-free position-based dynamics with scene-specific rigidity, attachment, region scale, and grasp coupling. We further introduce an optional \textit{observation-referenced residual model} combining graph-spectral spatial modes with periodic harmonic dynamics for structured motion not explained by the tool input. We evaluate the framework retrospectively on 12 surgical manipulation clips from three datasets. Observation referencing provides the largest gain, with a median within-clip error ratio of $0.598$ relative to the corresponding absolute simulation forecast. The optional periodic residual further improves forecasting when the remaining motion exhibits periodic structure. These results support observation-referenced deformation prediction as a practical building block for future surgical world models, digital twins, and generative simulators that must predict how tissue evolves under instrument actions.

</details>


### [22] [ClothCode: Generative Modeling for Garment State Estimation and Dynamics Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14200)
*Yingdong Ru,Lipeng Zhuang,Shiyu Fan,Zhuo He,Florent P. Audonnet,Gerardo Aragon Camarasa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的生成式框架ClothCode，在共享离散配置空间中同时完成衣物状态估计与动作条件动力学预测，兼顾精度与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 衣物状态估计与动力学预测在机器人操作中很关键，但现有方法精度不足且推理速度慢（如扩散模型耗时长），难以支撑实时控制。作者希望用统一框架在共享离散表示空间中同时处理这两类任务。

Method: 采用材料对齐的tokenizer，将衣物配置编码为残差向量量化（RVQ）的patch token；随后使用任务专用的掩码生成Transformer（Masked Generative Transformer），通过并行的置信度引导细化过程预测这些token。两类任务共享同一离散配置空间。

Result: 在三种衣物模板上，状态估计的平均位移误差降低26.6%，动力学预测降低37.0%（相对最强基线）；动力学预测每窗口仅需16.7 ms，而扩散模型需556.2 ms。集成到同一模型预测控制器后，真实世界折叠与铺平成功率从56.7%提升至78.9%。

Conclusion: 统一的离散生成式表示能有效兼顾衣物状态估计与动力学预测的精度和效率，并在真实机器人操作任务中显著提升成功率，具备实时控制可用性。

Abstract: We introduce ClothCode, a unified generative framework for garment state estimation and action-conditioned dynamics prediction in a shared discrete configuration space. A material-aligned tokenizer encodes garment configuration as residual vector-quantized patch tokens, while task-specific Masked Generative Transformers predict these tokens through parallel confidence-guided refinement. Across three garment templates, ClothCode reduces mean displacement error by 26.6% for state estimation and 37.0% for dynamics prediction relative to the strongest baselines. Dynamics prediction requires only 16.7 ms per window, compared with 556.2 ms for diffusion. Integrated into the same model-predictive controller, ClothCode improves real-world folding and flattening success from 56.7% to 78.9%. Further results and videos are available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ClothCode-DE32

</details>


### [23] [Mapping Tire-Road Friction Coefficients Along Driven Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14235)
*Risto Ojala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FrictionMap——一种离线后处理方法，利用车载传感器记录的行驶数据，沿行驶轨迹映射轮胎-路面摩擦系数；通过EKF+RTS平滑估计车辆动力学、基于轮胎滑移筛选可观测数据点，并用高斯过程拟合得到连续摩擦估计；仿真验证表明其性能优于常用在线摩擦估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩擦系数决定车辆可控的操纵极限，对智能车辆系统至关重要。现有在线摩擦估计文献丰富，但尚无研究基于记录传感器数据对摩擦系数进行后处理并沿行驶轨迹映射。若能将摩擦信息地图化，联网车辆可共享该信息，从而在低摩擦区域采取主动预防措施。

Method: FrictionMap是一种离线后处理方法。输入为常见车载传感器信息，先验知识仅需基本车辆参数集与一个滑移阈值。方法流程为：使用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）结合Rauch-Tung-Striebel（RTS）平滑器估计车辆动力学；通过基于轮胎滑移的筛选识别摩擦极限可观测的数据点；再对这些点拟合高斯过程，获得沿轨迹的连续摩擦系数估计。

Result: 通过仿真研究对FrictionMap进行验证，结果表明其性能优于常用的在线摩擦估计技术。代码实现与仿真基准将在论文录用后开源，以便后续研究。

Conclusion: 该工作首次探索了基于记录驾驶数据的摩擦系数后处理与轨迹映射，仅依赖常见车载传感器和少量先验参数即可实现连续摩擦估计，为联网车辆共享摩擦信息、在低摩擦区域实施主动安全措施提供了可行途径；后续将开源代码与基准以促进研究。

Abstract: Information of the tire-road friction coefficient is critical for intelligent vehicle systems, as friction establishes the limits at which the vehicle can be manoeuvred without loss of control. While there is extensive literature on online friction estimation, no previous studies have explored post-processing and mapping friction coefficients along a driven trajectory based on recorded sensor data. Mapped friction information could be shared by connected vehicles, enabling proactive measures in low-friction areas. This paper presents an offline post-processing method, FrictionMap, for mapping friction coefficients from recorded driving data. The method operates on commonly available on-board vehicle sensor information, and requires only a basic vehicle parameter set and a slip threshold as prior knowledge. FrictionMap runs an extended Kalman filter combined with a Rauch-Tung-Striebel smoother to estimate vehicle dynamics. Data points where the friction limit is observable are identified by filtering points based on tire slip. A Gaussian process is fitted to these points to acquire a continuous estimate of the friction coefficient along the trajectory. FrictionMap is validated via a simulation study, and shown to outperform commonly used online friction estimation techniques. The code implementation and the simulation benchmark are released as open-source after paper acceptance to enable further research.

</details>


### [24] [Composable Dynamics for Cooperative Humanoids: A RoboCompiler Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14237)
*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文扩展了 RoboCompiler，提出成员级动力学汇编方法，用于物理连接的多足/人形机器人系统，实现高效且一致的动力学编译，并支持多种协同控制任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理连接的人形机器人需要在内部执行器回路、机器人间作用力及变化的地面支撑间保持一致的动力学，这要求一个可复用、高效且精确的动力学编译框架。

Method: 提出成员级动力学汇编，将刚性体、运动执行器部件和反射转子惯量编译为约简动力学；编译体雅可比矩阵和解析约束曲率给出满足浮动基准速度功率恒等式的显式科里奥利因子；采用两级约简保留可复用机器人模型，同时组合其支撑与手部连接；局部惯性更新秩至多为六，规定加速度约束力旋量对执行器力矩呈仿射关系。

Result: 在八种人形配置的264个可行状态中，归一化加速度差异对硬约束参考保持在1.6×10⁻¹⁰以内，对独立于编译器的参考保持在1.4×10⁻¹⁰以内；MuJoCo仿真中环形舞蹈的骨盆均方根误差为0.73 mm，并完成20次支撑模式切换的侧步。

Conclusion: 该编译框架可支持执行器感知跟踪、耦合力分配、负载更新与支撑模式切换等任务，适用于五个手拉手连接的人形机器人系统，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Physically connected humanoids need consistent dynamics for internal actuator loops, inter-robot forces, and changing ground supports. We extend RoboCompiler with member-wise dynamics assembly, compiling reduced dynamics from rigid bodies, moving actuator parts, and reflected rotor inertias. Compiled body Jacobians and analytic constraint curvature yield an explicit Coriolis factor satisfying the power identity for floating-base quasi-velocities. A two-level reduction retains reusable robot models while composing their supports and hand connections; local inertial updates have rank at most six, and prescribed-acceleration constraint wrenches are affine in actuator efforts. These interfaces support actuator-aware tracking, coupled force allocation, payload updates, and support-mode switching for five hand-linked humanoids. Across 264 feasible states in eight humanoid configurations, normalised acceleration discrepancies remain within $1.6\!\times\!10^{-10}$ against a hard-constraint reference and $1.4\!\times\!10^{-10}$ against a compiler-independent reference; MuJoCo simulations achieve 0.73~mm root-mean-square (RMS) pelvis error in the ring dance and complete side steps through 20 support-mode changes.

</details>


### [25] [From LLM Plans to Authorized Motion: Execution-Bound Authorization for Excavators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14245)
*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Seihun Kim,Soyi Jung,Soohyun Park,Jouni Mattila,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出"执行绑定授权"框架，用于约束LLM调度挖掘机在"批准—派发—执行"区间内的权限，通过一次性租约、原子派发和周期监督实现运动身份与派发完整性、负载感知中断的绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM调度的挖掘机可能执行机械上可行、但目标或权限已不再符合操作员批准的运动，即批准与执行之间存在权限漂移窗口，需要在此区间内施加执行层面的授权约束。

Method: 提出执行绑定授权框架：一次性租约绑定动作、生成运动、任务契约与准入容差；原子派发重新校验当前权限与实测关节漂移；周期监督检测撤销并请求带carry-pitch端点的五次多项式停止，通过采样的关节、行程、流量与参考加速度检查进行选择。

Result: 网关接纳全部120个良性接口用例，攻击套件中零未授权派发；负载派发窗口测试零未授权卸载；模拟倾倒阶段撤销时最大封闭场地卸载量由1.23 m³降至0.33 m³；负载回转停止使用低于75%的额定回转扭矩。评估包含672用例接口套件、四个负载挖掘案例的同步仿真、空载MuJoCo/Pinocchio迁移及两个命令级LLM规划器。

Conclusion: 结果验证了运动绑定授权的有效性，并量化了受监督挖掘技能在权限撤销下的保持与停止行为。

Abstract: An LLM-directed excavator can execute mechanically feasible motion whose destination or authority no longer matches the operator's approval. We propose execution-bound authorization for the approval--dispatch--execution interval. A one-use lease binds the action, generated motion, mission contract and admission tolerance; atomic dispatch rechecks current authority and measured joint drift. Periodic supervision detects revocation and requests a quintic stop with a carry-pitch endpoint, selected through sampled joint, stroke, flow and reference-acceleration checks. The framework connects exact motion identity to dispatch integrity and load-aware interruption. Evaluation combines a 672-case interface suite, four loaded digging cases in synchronous simulation, unloaded MuJoCo/Pinocchio transfers and two command-level LLM planners. The gateway admitted all 120 benign interface cases and produced zero unauthorized dispatches in the attack suite. Loaded dispatch-window tests produced zero unauthorized deposition. During simulated dump-phase revocation, maximum closed-site deposition decreased from 1.23 to 0.33\,m$^3$; loaded-slew stops used less than 75\% of nominal swing torque. These results demonstrate motion-bound authorization and quantify the retention and stopping behavior of supervised excavation skills.

</details>


### [26] [Akule: Fast and Scalable Multi-Robot Motion Planning via Sparse Interaction Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14248)
*Manav Vora,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Aditya Penumarti,Jane Shin,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Akule：一种稀疏交互扩散框架，用冻结的单机器人先验加三个学习模块（成对修正、上下文选择器、组合器）实现大规模多机器人运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型能为多机器人运动规划提供表达力强的轨迹先验，但扩展到大规模机器人团队时，需要在避免昂贵的联合计算的前提下协调各机器人运动。

Method: 提出Akule，通过冻结的单机器人先验增强三个学习模块：可复用的成对修正模型R_φ、决定评估哪些修正的上下文选择器U_ψ、以及通过上下文相关带符号权重组合其效果以形成协调去噪更新的组合器G_ω。R_φ用匹配的成对轨迹优化训练，G_ω与U_ψ从物理优化后的联合轨迹中学习，无需给定的真值交互图；生成的联合提案交给下游基于搜索的规划器验证与修复。

Result: 在多个MRMP基准上，Akule取得有竞争力的规划成功率、完整规划器最高25倍加速、零样本种群迁移，并可成功规划多达256个机器人。消融显示学习到的选择与组合相比启发式选择和均匀求和可降低修复前冲突及下游修复代价。

Conclusion: 通过将“哪些交互值得计算”与“如何组合其效果”分离，Akule在无需昂贵联合计算的情况下实现了可扩展的多机器人扩散规划，并可迁移至大规模团队。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide expressive trajectory priors for multi-robot motion planning (MRMP), but scaling them to large teams requires coordinating individual motions without expensive joint computation. We introduce Akule, a sparse interaction diffusion framework that augments a frozen single-robot prior with three learned modules: a reusable pairwise correction model $R_φ$, a contextual selector $U_ψ$ that determines which corrections to evaluate, and a composer $G_ω$ that combines their effects through context-dependent signed weights. This decomposition separates which interactions merit computation from how their effects should be combined into coordinated denoising updates. We train $R_φ$ using matched pairwise trajectory optimizations, then learn $G_ω$ and $U_ψ$ from physically optimized joint trajectories without requiring a prescribed ground-truth interaction graph. The resulting joint proposals are passed to a downstream search-based planner for validation and repair. Across diverse MRMP benchmarks, Akule achieves competitive planning success, complete-planner speedups of up to $25\times$, zero-shot population transfer, and successful planning with up to $256$ robots. Ablations further demonstrate that learned selection and composition reduce pre-repair conflicts and downstream repair effort relative to heuristic selection and uniform summation.

</details>


### [27] [PILOT: Guiding Long-Horizon World-Model Planning for Robot Manipulation with Visual Subgoals and Policy Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14335)
*Yang Liu,Hanchen Cui,Karthik Desingh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PILOT框架，用视频模型生成中间视觉子目标，并用学习策略初始化CEM，在世界模型中滚动优化动作序列，提升长时域操作任务的成功率与规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型规划在目标条件机器人操作中有效，但长时域规划面临两大问题：远景视觉目标对预备动作缺乏指导，且无信息动作采样导致局部优化低效。

Method: PILOT由三部分组成：(1)利用视频模型生成中间视觉子目标以提供时序引导；(2)用学习到的策略初始化CEM的候选动作分布；(3)在滚动时域控制回路中，以动作条件世界模型为代价评估，将候选动作序列逐步精化至各子目标。

Result: 真实UR5e四任务评估：PILOT成功率68.8%，无策略初始化55.0%，仅策略执行13.8%，直接最终目标规划0.0%。仿真中长期任务增益持续：长Push-T较直接执行策略高30个百分点，LIBERO-10高22个百分点。Push-T上CEM仅需4次迭代即达到通用初始化10次迭代的代价，相同采样预算下最终规划代价降低24.1%。

Conclusion: 将时序子目标与策略引导的动作搜索相结合，可同时提升长时域任务执行能力与局部规划效率。

Abstract: Latent world-model planning has shown promising results in goal-conditioned robotic manipulation, but remains challenging for long-horizon planning due to limited temporal guidance and inefficient action search. A distant visual goal provides little guidance for preparatory actions, while uninformed action sampling makes local optimization inefficient. We propose PILOT (Policy-Initialized Local Optimization with Temporal subgoals), which uses a video model to generate intermediate visual subgoals and a learned policy to initialize the cross-entropy method (CEM). CEM refines candidate action sequences toward each subgoal using an action-conditioned world model in a receding-horizon control loop. In our primary real-robot evaluation across four UR5e manipulation tasks, PILOT achieves 68.8% success, compared with 55.0% without policy initialization, 13.8% for policy-only execution, and 0.0% for direct final-goal planning. Simulation results show that these gains persist over longer horizons: PILOT exceeds direct execution of the same policy by 30 percentage points on long Push-T tasks and by 22 percentage points on LIBERO-10. Policy initialization also accelerates local optimization: on Push-T, CEM matches the cost reached by generic initialization after 10 iterations in only 4 iterations and reduces the final planning cost by 24.1% under the same sampling budget. These results show that combining temporal subgoals with policy-guided action search improves both long-horizon task execution and local planning efficiency. Videos are available on the webpage: https://pilot-planner.github.io/.

</details>


### [28] [CoPlanVLM: Coordinated Path Planning for a Robot Team using Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14341)
*David R. Martin,Jared Paquet,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Pramod Khargonekar,Kyle Volle,J. Humberto Ramos,Scott Nivison*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoPlanVLM 利用视觉语言模型将单条自然语言指令与图像转化为两台机器人的协同路径，通过图像叠加、任务分类和任务特定思维链推理增强空间推理，在仿真与真机上平均成功率达 84.4%，优于最佳基线 50.0%，成功试验中路径平均缩短 27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 为机器人团队提供灵活的自然语言交互界面，但其空间推理能力薄弱，严重限制了基于 VLM 的路径规划。

Method: 提出 CoPlanVLM，包含三个组件：(1) 标记自由空间与障碍物的图像叠加；(2) 任务分类，为每条指令选择最合适的底层规划器与推理提示；(3) 任务特定的思维链推理，引导 VLM 识别关键物体、分配角色并在确定计划前验证路径无碰撞。

Result: 在 Gazebo 仿真与物理 Turtlebot 4 硬件上验证。平均成功率达 84.4%，最佳基线为 50.0%；在两者均成功的试验中，CoPlanVLM 路径平均缩短 27%。

Conclusion: CoPlanVLM 支持需要双机器人协作的复杂协同机动、可拆分大任务以提升效率，并支持涉及特定路线、环路、大范围覆盖以及方向推理（如南北或左右）的精细机动。

Abstract: We present CoPlanVLM, which uses a Vision Language Model (VLM) to translate a single natural-language instruction and image into coordinated paths for a team of two robots. While VLMs offer a flexible, natural-language interface for commanding robot teams, their weak spatial reasoning has greatly limited VLM-based path planning. CoPlanVLM improves the VLM's spatial reasoning and path planning abilities through three components: (1) an image overlay marking both free space and obstacles, (2) task classification that selects the most appropriate low-level planner and reasoning prompt for each instruction, and (3) task-specific chain-of-thought reasoning that prompts the VLM to identify key objects, allocate roles, and verify routes are collision-free before committing to a plan. Together, these components enable the team to complete complex coordinated maneuvers requiring both robots and divide large tasks between robots to increase efficiency. CoPlanVLM also supports detailed maneuvers involving specific routes, loops, and coverage of large areas, as well as tasks requiring directional reasoning such as north/south or left/right. We validate CoPlanVLM in both Gazebo simulation and on physical Turtlebot 4 hardware. CoPlanVLM attains an average success rate of 84.4% compared to 50.0% for the closest baseline. On trials where both methods succeed, CoPlanVLM's paths are 27% shorter on average. Project code is available at https://github.com/AICPS/CoPlanVLM.

</details>


### [29] [DexFLEX: Contact-Aware Foundation Controller for Command-Guided Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14372)
*Weikang Wan,Zeyu Lai,Jianyu Hou,Yuchen Zhou,Jiawei Fu,Henrik I Christensen,Hao Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DexFLEX，一种接触感知的基础控制器，将上游指尖运动草稿转化为接触一致的关节命令，通过"草稿-想象-选择"流程在推理时筛选候选动作，无需重训练即可提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手可从遥操作或学习策略中获得运动意图，但成功执行还依赖这些命令通常未指定的接触决策。直接复制上游草稿轨迹会忽略接触后果，导致执行不稳定或失败。

Method: 将上游指尖运动草稿视为关于意图的证据而非待复制轨迹。从当前触觉-本体感知状态出发，提出可行的短时域运动片段，预测其接触后果，并选择既遵循命令又保持未来接触稳定性的候选。推理时采用 Draft-Dream-Select 过程，结合纯先验提议（用于恢复）与草稿播种提议（用于响应性），再将所选运动解码为可执行关节空间目标。

Result: 在退化命令仿真、真实世界共享控制与视觉运动策略学习中，同一训练好的控制器无需重训练即可提升鲁棒性：真实遥操作成功率由 29.2% 提升至 78.3%；策略学习成功率达 46.7%，为直接关节动作预测的 3.5 倍、纯先验修正的 1.56 倍。

Conclusion: DexFLEX 作为一种接触感知的基础控制器，可有效弥合上游运动意图与接触执行之间的鸿沟，在不重训练的情况下跨多种任务显著提升鲁棒性与成功率。

Abstract: Dexterous robot hands can receive useful motion intent from teleoperation or learned policies, but successful execution also depends on contact decisions that these commands rarely specify. We introduce DexFLEX, a contact-aware foundation controller that turns upstream fingertip-motion drafts into contact-consistent joint commands. Instead of treating a draft as a trajectory to copy, DexFLEX treats it as evidence about intent: from the current tactile-proprioceptive state, it proposes feasible short-horizon motion chunks, predicts their contact consequences, and selects the candidate that best follows the command while preserving future contact stability. At inference time, a simple Draft-Dream-Select procedure combines pure-prior proposals for recovery with draft-seeded proposals for responsiveness, then decodes the selected motion into executable joint-space targets. Across degraded-command simulation, real-world shared control, and visuomotor policy learning, the same trained controller improves robustness without retraining, increasing real-world teleoperation success from 29.2% to 78.3% and reaching 46.7% policy-learning success, 3.5 times direct joint-action prediction, and 1.56 times prior-only correction. Videos are available at dex-flex.github.io.

</details>


### [30] [OATH: Object-Agnostic Tracking for Dexterous Hands, Enabling Rapid Physics-based Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14379)
*Solvin Sigurdson,Prasanna Sriganesh,Juan Borbon,Joel Burdick,Dingqi Zhang,Jiuguang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将物理重定向重构为残差学习问题，通过结构化观测（局部接触误差信号）实现跨物体的零样本泛化，提出OATH方法，速度比实时快2.4倍，算力降低60-350倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示重定向到灵巧手轨迹需解决复杂接触物理问题，但现有方法要么忽略物理约束，要么依赖仿真中昂贵的采样优化；强化学习虽可处理接触动力学，但标准策略需针对特定物体训练，无法泛化到任意几何形状。

Method: 将基于物理的重定向重构为残差学习问题，在观测空间中引入局部接触误差信号，引导各机器人连杆到达物体表面目标坐标，从而用单一统一的残差策略实现零样本泛化；在运动学参考轨迹上仿真rollout该策略，得到基于物理的重定向器OATH。

Result: OATH每参考秒仅需0.4秒计算，达到比实时快2.4倍的性能，相比采样方法算力降低60-350倍；在DexYCB数据集上恢复58%的留出轨迹和54%的未见物体轨迹，跟踪误差低于2厘米。

Conclusion: 结构化状态表征（局部接触误差信号）是残差策略实现跨物体零样本泛化的关键，OATH在保持高精度跟踪的同时显著提升计算效率，为物理接触下的灵巧手重定向提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Retargeting human demonstrations into dynamically feasible dexterous hand trajectories requires resolving complex contact physics, yet current methods either omit physical constraints or rely on computationally expensive sampling-based optimization in simulation. Reinforcement learning has previously been used to achieve planning and control through contact dynamics; however, standard policies require object-specific training and fail to generalize across arbitrary geometries. This work reframes physics-based retargeting as a residual learning problem, where we demonstrate that zero-shot generalization across objects relies on structured state representations. Specifically, formulating the observation space to include a localized contact-error signal guiding each robot link to its target coordinate on the object surface enables a single, unified residual policy to successfully grasp novel, unseen object geometries. Rolling out this policy in simulation on a kinematic reference trajectory yields a physics-based retargeter, OATH. Operating at 0.4 seconds of compute per second of reference, OATH achieves 2.4x faster-than-real-time performance and a 60-350x reduction in computational cost compared to sampling-based methods. The system recovers 58% of held-out and 54% of unseen-object DexYCB trajectories while maintaining a sub-2 cm tracking error.

</details>


### [31] [Memory Without Curation: Learning Multi-Stream Latent Memory for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14406)
*Yilin Wu,Rutav Shah,Shuaiyi Huang,Sumedh A. Sontakke,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Andrea Bajcsy,Max Simchowitz,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMPRINT：一种策略无关的潜在记忆框架，通过多流潜在记忆压缩与两阶段训练增强VLA模型，避免捷径学习并保持低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略需要长期记忆以完成复杂任务，但直接以全部历史观测为条件易导致捷径学习（spurious shortcuts），并在部署时引入高延迟。现有记忆增强策略通过筛选记忆内容（仅保留近期帧、关键帧、文本摘要或仅学习视觉流的潜在表示）来缓解，但显式约束了应记住什么。

Method: IMPRINT将不同信息流（观测、动作、本体感受）压缩为潜在记忆，并在推理时增量更新以实现低延迟。采用两阶段训练：第一阶段监督记忆重建过去的多流信息并估计当前任务进度；第二阶段与策略联合端到端微调，进行动作预测。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界的记忆需求任务中，IMPRINT相比基础VLA平均成功率提升至少26%，优于主流替代方案，并在所有记忆增强VLA中保持最低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 通过消融实验验证了将多样信息流纳入机器人记忆的有效性，证明了放弃历史筛选（foregoing history curation）的优势。

Abstract: It is widely appreciated that robotic policies require long-term memory to perform complex tasks. However, directly conditioning policies on all past observations leaves policy learning susceptible to learning spurious shortcuts, and introduces large latency at policy deployment. As such, today's memory-augmented policies curate what to remember, either by retaining only recent frames, selected keyframes, or textual summaries, or by learning latent representations of the visual stream alone. In this work, we propose an alternative approach IMPRINT, a policy-agnostic latent memory framework that augments vision-language-action (VLA) models with multi-stream memory without explicitly constraining what the policy should remember. IMPRINT compresses different streams into a latent memory that is updated incrementally at inference, enabling low latency. To avoid shortcut learning, IMPRINT adopts a two-stage training procedure, first supervising memory to reconstruct multiple streams (observations, actions, proprioception) of past information, as well as estimate current task progress, followed by end-to-end finetuning, jointly with the policy, on action prediction. Across both simulation and real-world tasks demanding memory, IMPRINT improves average success over the base VLA by at least 26%, outperforming popular alternatives, and maintaining lowest inference latency among memory-augmented VLAs. Through careful ablations, we demonstrate the utility of incorporating diverse information streams into robotic memory, validating the advantages of foregoing history curation. Project website: https://yilin-wu98.github.io/imprint_website/.

</details>


### [32] [Red-Teaming of Vision-Language-Action Models via Quality Diversity Environment Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14415)
*Shihan Zhao,Henry Chen,Ya-Chuan Hsu,Minjune Hwang,Siddharth Srikanth,Freddie Liang,Akanksha Saran,Aaquib Tabrez,Stefanos Nikolaidis,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-DEG方法，通过质量多样性优化生成对抗环境，并利用VLA自身成功轨迹进行自模仿微调，以提升VLA模型的鲁棒性并促进仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型在训练分布内表现良好，但在测试时出现微小偏移（如物体位置、光照、纹理、相机位姿变化）时往往失败，亟需提升其鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Q-DEG（Quality Diversity for Diverse Environment Generation）。该方法利用质量多样性（QD）优化搜索一组高失败率的对抗环境，并借助从VLA提取的策略嵌入来促进环境多样性；随后在这些暴露脆弱性的环境中，用VLA自身成功 rollout 进行自模仿微调。

Result: 在仿真中针对三种代表性VLA模型进行实验，Q-DEG相比基础VLA以及使用两种red-teaming基线数据微调的VLA，在分布外（OOD）对抗环境下的鲁棒性均有提升；同时证明该鲁棒性提升有助于sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 通过对抗环境生成与自模仿微调相结合的Q-DEG，可有效增强VLA的鲁棒性，并支持从仿真到现实的迁移。

Abstract: A long-standing goal in robotics is to develop generalist robots that can perform tasks across a wide range of settings, flexibly specified with images and text. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising path toward this goal by training on large collections of robot demonstrations. However, while VLAs perform well in-distribution, they often fail under minor test-time shifts, such as changes in object positions, lighting, textures, and camera poses. To address this, we propose Quality Diversity for Diverse Environment Generation (Q-DEG), a method for improving VLA robustness by generating adversarial environments and fine-tuning the VLA on its own successful rollouts. Q-DEG uses Quality Diversity (QD) optimization to find a set of high-failure environments, while promoting diversity using policy embeddings extracted from the VLA. These environments expose vulnerabilities that can be mitigated through self-imitation fine-tuning, thereby improving the VLA's robustness. In simulation, we conduct experiments across three representative VLA models, and our results indicate that Q-DEG improves robustness to out-of-distribution (OOD) adversarial environments compared to the base VLA and VLAs fine-tuned using data collected from two red-teaming baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate that improving VLA robustness through Q-DEG facilitates sim-to-real transfer. Our anonymous project website is at: https://qd-env.github.io/qdeg/.

</details>


### [33] [Shared-Control Teleoperation of Omnidirectional Aerial Robots for Continuous Valve Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14478)
*Jinjie Li,Yicheng Chen,Haokun Liu,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究针对全向飞行机器人遥操作中因人体前臂运动范围受限而无法连续旋转阀门的问题，提出当人体前臂角度超过阈值时由人类控制切换为机器人自主控制的共享控制方法，以实现阀门的持续旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全向飞行机器人具备6自由度独立位姿控制能力，适用于空中操作任务。但复杂空中环境下仍需人类操作。此前的遥操作系统虽能将人手的旋转灵活性映射到无边界空中工作空间，但受限于前臂运动范围，机器人无法连续旋转阀门，制约了工业应用。

Method: 提出一种控制权限切换机制：当人体前臂角度超过预设阈值时，触发机器人的连续旋转；该机制体现了人机交互中的共享控制（shared control）理念。

Result: 通过控制权限的切换实现了阀门的连续旋转，使遥操作系统能够完成此前无法实现的连续操作任务。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了空中遥操作的发展，是人机共享控制在空中操作场景中的进一步探索，为工业应用提供了可能。

Abstract: Omnidirectional aerial robots offer full 6-DoF independent control over position and orientation, making them popular for aerial manipulation. Although there have been advancements in robotic autonomy, human operation remains essential in complex aerial environments. Previously, we proposed an aerial teleoperation system that brings the rotational flexibility of human hands into the unbounded aerial workspace, which was evaluated through a vertically mounted valve-turning task. However, due to the limited range of human forearm's motion, the robot could not continuously rotate the valve, hindering its application in industry. In this work, we propose an approach to initiate continuous rotation when the forearm angle exceeds a threshold. This switch of control authority exemplifies the concept shared control from human-robot interaction, marking a further step in aerial teleoperation.

</details>


### [34] [From Field to Planning: Learning Temporal Polar Gradient Fields for Vision-based AAV Trajectory Planning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14482)
*Haowen Gao,Zhan Chen,Yinuo Xu,Yifeng Niu,Mengyun Wang,Chao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段 Field-to-Planning（F2P）学习框架，为动态环境下的自主飞行器轨迹规划提供准确高效的安全梯度，并在真实场景中验证其实时性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹规划方法依赖静态地图与动态障碍物跟踪来构建梯度场，计算负担重且轨迹偏保守，难以在动态环境中实现实时、准确的安全梯度引导。

Method: 第一阶段：提出以自车为中心的极坐标占用表示（EPO），契合机载相机视锥几何并支持非结构化场景建模；通过多核可分离高斯卷积构建时序极坐标梯度场（TPGF），统一生成安全梯度，无需显式区分动静障碍物；针对极坐标网格卷积带来的空间歧义，引入 ESDF 引导的离线学习策略，将几何先验注入 TPGF。第二阶段：以标定后的 TPGF 通过自监督学习方式引导轨迹规划策略。

Result: 与其他方法对比显示所提方法具有优越性；在多种杂乱动态环境中的大量真实实验验证了其在资源受限机载系统上的实时性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: F2P 框架通过 EPO 表示与 TPGF 梯度场，结合 ESDF 引导的离线学习与自监督规划，实现了高效准确的安全梯度引导，适用于资源受限平台上的动态环境轨迹规划。

Abstract: Autonomous Aerial Vehicle (AAV) trajectory planning requires real-time and accurate safety gradient guidance, especially in dynamic environments. Existing methods typically rely on static maps and dynamic obstacle tracking to construct gradient fields, imposing heavy computational burdens and yielding conservative trajectories. To address these limitations, we propose a two-stage Field-to-Planning (F2P) learning framework to provide accurate and efficient safety gradients for trajectory planning in dynamic environments. In the first stage, we introduce an Ego-centric Polar Occupancy (EPO) representation that naturally matches the geometry of the onboard camera frustum, while supporting unstructured scene modeling. Subsequently, we formulate a Temporal Polar Gradient Field (TPGF) via multi-kernel separable Gaussian convolution to provide unified safety gradients, without explicitly distinguishing between static and dynamic obstacles. To mitigate the inherent spatial ambiguity introduced by applying convolutions within the polar grid, we propose an ESDF-guided offline learning strategy that incorporates geometric priors into TPGF. In the second stage, we employ the calibrated TPGF to guide the trajectory planning policy in a self-supervised learning manner. Comparative results with other approaches demonstrate the superiority of our method. Extensive real-world experiments within various cluttered dynamic environments validate the real-time performance and robustness on resource-constrained onboard systems.

</details>


### [35] [Multi-modal Representation Fusion for Long-term Microsurgical Anastomosis Trajectory Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14492)
*Yuxuan Liu,Yating Luo,Yunfei Luan,Xinyao Zhou,Yuyang Zhuge,Yao Guo,Guang-Zhong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨模态特征融合框架，用于长期2D与3D显微外科吻合术轨迹预测，通过语义识别与运动估计两类视觉线索结合粗到细渐进融合策略，在自建数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微外科吻合术是手术机器人中极具挑战性的操作，需在有限空间内精准操控器械。准确的轨迹预测对手术场景理解、术中规划、机器人操控和行为分析至关重要。然而现有方法多依赖手术视频特征与历史轨迹，忽视了可提供更具判别性多模态表示的语义与运动信息，难以刻画复杂的器械-组织交互。

Method: 提出跨模态特征融合框架，包含多模态表示提取器与跨模态特征融合网络。将手术对象的语义识别与连续运动估计作为多模态视觉线索，提供互补的时空引导以刻画器械-组织交互。引入带长短期记忆机制的粗到细渐进融合策略，逐步捕捉时序依赖。在自建机器人平台与涵盖模型（phantom）和离体（ex-vivo）场景的综合数据集上进行训练与评估。

Result: 大量定量与定性实验表明，该框架在性能上持续优于现有方法，在不同手术场景中表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架在智能机器人辅助及自主手术系统方面具有应用潜力，为长期2D/3D显微外科吻合术轨迹预测提供了有效的多模态融合方案。

Abstract: Microsurgical anastomosis is one of the most challenging and sophisticated procedures for surgical robotics, requiring accurate manipulation of surgical instruments within a limited space to achieve precise operations. Accurate surgical trajectory anticipation is essential for surgical scene understanding, intraoperative planning, robotic manipulation, and reliable behaviour analysis. However, existing approaches mainly focus on features extracted from surgical videos and historical trajectories, while overlooking rich semantic and motion information that can provide more discriminative multi-modal representations for complex surgical instrument-tissue interactions. In this work, we propose a cross-modal feature fusion framework for long-term 2D and 3D microsurgical anastomosis trajectory anticipation. The proposed framework consists of a multi-modal representation extractor and a cross-modal feature fusion network. Semantic recognition of surgical objects and consecutive motion estimation are incorporated as multi-modal visual cues, providing complementary spatial and temporal guidance for instrument-tissue interaction characterization. A coarse-to-fine progressive fusion strategy with a long-short-term memory mechanism is introduced to capture temporal dependencies step by step. The training and evaluation are conducted on our established robotic platform and a comprehensive dataset containing both phantom and ex-vivo surgical scenarios is constructed. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms existing methods and exhibits improved robustness across different surgical scenes, highlighting its potential for both intelligent robot-assisted and autonomous surgical systems.

</details>


### [36] [Safety-Critical Quadrotor Navigation Using Fourth-Order Control Barrier Functions with Full Nonlinear Quadrotor Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14522)
*Yanze Liu,Yaşar İdikut,Dhruv Madhusudan Agrawal,Wei Xiao,Guanrui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于四阶控制障碍函数(CBF)的安全控制器，直接纳入四旋翼完整非线性动力学(含姿态动力学与力矩级驱动)，并采用积分高阶CBF技术解决混合相对度问题，实现力矩级安全约束优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分层架构中，高层安全滤波器仅修改位置参考，再由独立姿态控制器跟踪，导致平动安全目标与姿态演化之间存在不匹配（脱节）。

Method: 构建基于四阶CBF的安全控制器，显式包含完整非线性四旋翼动力学（姿态动力学与力矩级驱动）；应用积分高阶CBF技术处理约束推导中的混合相对度问题；在避障约束下优化控制力矩，使安全层直接作用于姿态行为。

Result: 在高保真仿真与真实飞行实验中验证：约束满足可靠，避障成功。

Conclusion: 力矩级高阶CBF为完整刚体动力学下可证明更安全的四旋翼导航提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Safety-critical quadrotor navigation in cluttered environments typically relies on hierarchical architectures in which a high-level safety filter modifies position references that are then tracked by a separate attitude controller.
