Result: 在225条留出试验上，轨迹级分类准确率达93.78%，分别较单独TCN与FGFN提升4.00与2.22个百分点；TCN停止路径F1为95.25%。数据集含5个物体实例、1500条自动采集轨迹。Flash部署中混合加速器执行保持93.78%分类准确率与94.84%停止F1，平均推理时间2.890 ms，平均采样到停止延迟9.172 ms。50次实物试验中识别率96.00%、抓取成功率98.00%，整块嵌入式板功耗约0.80 W。

Conclusion: 该边缘原生管线以极低硬件与功耗代价，用无稠密触觉的反馈信号实现了高精度的物体识别与逐指停止预测，并在嵌入式平台上验证了实时性与实物抓取有效性，可作为多指手自适应抓取的低成本方案。

Abstract: Multi-finger hands contact objects at different times, so a global close command can overload early-contact fingers while others are still moving. Dense tactile arrays address this problem but add hardware, calibration, bandwidth, and computation. We present an edge-native pipeline that uses only built-in joint-angle and pre-use-calibrated actuator-load feedback to recognize the grasped object and predict an independent Stop event for each finger. Four samples and their first differences form a 4-by-20 contact-evolution window. At its core is a heterogeneous dual-expert architecture, termed DualExpert, that assigns complementary tasks to a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) and a Finger Graph Filter Network (FGFN). The TCN models short-term contact evolution and supplies all five Stop probabilities, while the FGFN encodes finger topology and contributes only to object classification. Fixed 0.7/0.3 class-probability fusion reaches 93.78\% trajectory-level classification accuracy on 225 held-out trials, improving the standalone TCN and FGFN by 4.00 and 2.22 percentage points, respectively. The TCN Stop path attains 95.25\% F1. The dataset contains 1,500 automatically collected trajectories from five object instances. In Flash deployment, mixed accelerator execution retains 93.78\% classification accuracy and 94.84\% Stop F1 with 2.890~ms mean inference time and 9.172~ms mean sample-to-stop latency. Across 50 physical trials, the system achieves 96.00\% recognition and 98.00\% grasp success while the complete embedded board draws approximately 0.80~W.

</details>


### [72] [CFG-Policy: Unified Diffusion Policy Learning Framework via Classifier-free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14936)
*Shutong Ding,Zhaoyang Liu,Yimiao Zhou,Jingya Wang,Jiayuan Gu,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CFG-Policy，一种即插即用框架，将探索与利用分离为两个生成策略，通过无分类器引导在采样阶段融合其得分/速度场，从而在提升任务性能的同时保持行为多样性与可迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在机器人控制、视觉-语言-动作模型和世界模型智能体中应用日益广泛，其多模态动作分布有利于强化学习中的探索。然而，用 RL 优化扩散策略会降低行为多样性并削弱探索能力；在线后训练中直接微调预训练策略可能破坏已有能力，而动作空间残差又缺乏多模态探索的表达力。这些在线与离线到在线扩散 RL 场景共同面临一个挑战：在提升任务性能的同时保持持续适应所需的行为多样性与能力。

Method: CFG-Policy 将探索与利用解耦为两个生成策略，并在采样时通过无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）组合二者的得分场或速度场。在线 RL 中，冻结的探索分支维持行为多样性，利用分支从任务奖励中学习；离线到在线 RL 中，预训练的利用分支保持冻结，由可训练分支引导组合后的行为。

Result: CFG-Policy 保留了底层 RL 目标，并在 5 个 MuJoCo 任务上配合 QSM 与 QVPO、在 4 个 RoboTwin 操作任务上配合 Flow-SDE+PPO 均提升了性能。

Conclusion: CFG-Policy 是一个统一、即插即用的框架，可在在线与离线到在线扩散 RL 设置下兼顾任务性能提升与行为多样性、能力的保持，为扩散策略的持续适应提供通用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion policies are increasingly used in robotic control and manipulation, vision-language-action models, and world-model-based agents. Their ability to represent multimodal action distributions offers rich opportunities for exploration in reinforcement learning (RL). However, optimizing diffusion policies with RL can reduce the behavioral diversity and further limit their exploration capability. In RL post-training, directly fine-tuning a pretrained policy may also disrupt its acquired capabilities, while action-space residuals may lack the expressive power for multimodal exploration. Together, these issues reveal a common challenge across online and offline-to-online diffusion RL: improving task performance while retaining the behavioral diversity and capabilities needed for continued adaptation. We introduce CFG-Policy, a unified, plug-and-play framework that separates exploration and exploitation into two generative policies and combines their score or velocity fields during sampling through classifier-free guidance. For online RL, a frozen exploration branch sustains behavioral diversity while an exploitation branch learns from task rewards; for offline-to-online RL, a pretrained exploitation branch remains frozen while a trainable branch steers the composed behavior. CFG-Policy preserves the underlying RL objectives and improves performance with QSM and QVPO on 5 MuJoCo tasks and with Flow-SDE+PPO on 4 RoboTwin manipulation tasks. Project page is at https://zhy-liu.github.io/cfg-policy-page.

</details>


### [73] [FoundationVIO: Enforcing Metric Scale in Feed-forward Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14951)
*Jin Rhee,Yuhao Zhang,Frank Dellaert,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoundationVIO将惯性传感与几何基础模型（GFM）预测通过因子图融合，恢复度量尺度，实现与经典VIO相当的里程计精度和优于GFM-SLAM的稠密重建，并加入基于GFM的闭环机制构建完整视觉惯性SLAM系统，在消费级GPU上近实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFM直接预测稠密几何与相机位姿，但独立预测仅局部一致、缺乏物理尺度约束，训练中已知尺度也只提升一致性而非直接测量尺度；因此GFM-enabled SLAM的轨迹与重建继承尺度歧义问题。作者受经典视觉惯性里程计启发，尝试用惯性传感来恢复度量尺度。

Method: 提出FoundationVIO，将惯性测量与GFM预测在因子图框架下融合，以恢复度量尺度的重建；并加入一个基于GFM的最小闭环机制，构成完整的视觉惯性SLAM系统，可产出全局一致的位姿与重建。

Result: 里程计估计与经典SOTA VIO方法相当；稠密重建精度优于GFM-enabled SLAM方法；在三个具有知名传感系统的数据集上验证，兼容常见相机与IMU，在消费级笔记本GPU上约实时运行。

Conclusion: 惯性传感与GFM预测的因子图融合可有效解决GFM尺度歧义，构建全局准确的视觉惯性SLAM系统，且具备良好的传感器兼容性与实时性。

Abstract: Feed-forward geometric foundation models (GFMs) directly predict dense geometry and camera poses from batches of images. Emerging visual SLAM methods chain together consecutive GFM batches to form dense point cloud maps. A core challenge is how to address the ambiguous geometric scale of GFM predictions: independent predictions are locally consistent up-to-scale but not constrained by any direct physical measurements. Models trained on data with known scale improve the consistency of GFM reconstructions but again do not directly measure it. Consequently, trajectories and 3D reconstructions from GFM-enabled SLAM methods inherit this issue. Motivated by parallels with classical visual-inertial odometry, we introduce FoundationVIO which integrates inertial sensing with GFM predictions to recover metric scale reconstruction using a factor-graph based approach. We demonstrate odometry estimates competitive with classical state-of-the-art VIO methods and accurate dense reconstructions that outperform GFM-enabled SLAM methods. We also add a minimal GFM-based loop-closure mechanism to create a full Visual-Inertial SLAM system capable of creating globally accurate reconstructions and pose estimates. We evaluate our method on three datasets with well known sensor systems and demonstrate its compatibility on ubiquitous camera and IMU sensors. The method runs at approximately real-time on a consumer-grade laptop GPU. Code: https://github.com/ori-drs/FoundationVIO.

</details>


### [74] [EnactOpt: Diagnosis-Guided Harness Evolution for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14977)
*ZhiYuan Feng,Zhuoshi Pan,Zhaolu Kang,Zhongxia Chen,Shuo Yang,Sen Ye,Heng Wang,Han Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EnactOpt，通过有限执行反馈联合演化可复用文本技能与上下文构建代码，在冻结模型与运行时前提下提升具身智能体任务成功率约10-27个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体依赖execution harness组织观测、引导动作并整合反馈；失败不仅源于模型能力，也源于harness如何呈现证据、引导使用。harness作为模型外部机制是无需更新参数的适配目标，但任务级结果无法区分"证据缺失"与"证据存在但未被恰当使用"，且二者耦合：有效动作引导可能需要当前上下文缺失的证据，而仅暴露证据又不保证恰当响应；同时任务特定交互经验采集成本高。

Method: EnactOpt利用有限执行反馈，在保持底层模型与运行时固定的条件下，联合演化可复用文本技能与上下文构建代码。方法通过将动作反馈与实际模型输入及后续动作关联来诊断相邻决策；使用结构化episode摘要，通过prompt-level编辑算子引导联合修订；候选harness需经过兼容性检查与基于验证的保留筛选。

Result: 在VLABench、ALFWorld和Embodied Agent Interface (EAI)上，EnactOpt相比初始harness提升任务成功率约10-27个百分点，并优于所评估的GEPA、TextGrad与Trace2Skill基线；增益可迁移到未用于harness演化或候选选择的任务条件。

Conclusion: 结果支持一条互补的具身适配路径：通过联合优化证据呈现方式与其被采纳的方式，将有限执行经验转化为可复用的行为改进。

Abstract: Embodied agents built on foundation models rely on an execution harness to organize observations, guide actions, and incorporate execution feedback into subsequent decisions. Failures can arise not only from limited model capabilities, but also from how the harness presents evidence and guides its use. These model-external mechanisms provide an adaptation target without updating model parameters, but task-specific interaction experience can be costly to collect. Task-level outcomes alone do not reveal whether relevant evidence was missing from the model input or was available but inadequately used. Moreover, these two aspects are coupled: effective action guidance may require evidence that the current context omits, while exposing that evidence alone does not ensure an appropriate response. We introduce EnactOpt, which uses limited execution feedback to jointly evolve reusable textual skills and context-construction code while keeping the underlying models and runtime fixed. It diagnoses adjacent decisions by relating action feedback to the actual model input and subsequent action. Structured episode summaries guide joint revisions through prompt-level edit operators, and candidate harnesses undergo compatibility checks and validation-based retention. Across VLABench, ALFWorld, and Embodied Agent Interface (EAI), EnactOpt improves task success by approximately 10-27 percentage points over the initial harnesses and outperforms the evaluated GEPA, TextGrad, and Trace2Skill baselines. These gains extend to task conditions not used for harness evolution or candidate selection. The results support a complementary route to embodied adaptation: turning limited execution experience into reusable behavioral improvements by jointly refining how evidence is presented and acted upon.

</details>


### [75] [Barrier-Constrained Guidance For Real-time Asynchronous VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14997)
*Wenbo Yu,Jifan Li,Wenke Xia,Yuchen Li,Yizhuo Zhang,Pei Ren,Zhengping Che,Di hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FreeBar，一种免训练实时推理插件，将基于流匹配的VLA模型中的动作块对齐建模为屏障引导的动作空间约束，在闭式求解下实现鲁棒异步执行，几乎不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步执行让VLA模型能在执行期间预测下一动作块，从而实现实时操作，但会与已提交的动作流产生边界错位。现有延迟感知方法需大量重训练、架构改动或固定前缀修补，在低延迟场景下计算代价过高。

Method: FreeBar将动作块对齐转化为屏障引导的动作空间约束：引入受控制屏障函数（CBF）启发的轻量一致性约束，作用于相邻动作块之间，并以闭式解求解修正量，避免对VLA动作头进行反向传播；同时在生成过程中逐步收紧一致性界，以兼顾早期生成自由度与块内未来动作灵活性。

Result: 在Kinetix、RoboCasa及多种真实机器人平台、多种VLA主干上，相比朴素异步执行平均提升成功率10%–15%，高动态场景增益更大；每动作块推理延迟较RTC降低45%以上，并保持实时计算效率。

Conclusion: FreeBar作为免训练通用插件，可在不同延迟环境、基座策略与超参数设置下保持鲁棒，兼顾异步执行的实时性与动作块一致性，且仅有边际额外推理成本。

Abstract: While asynchronous execution enables VLA models to operate in real-time environments by predicting the next chunk during execution, it inevitably introduces boundary misalignment with the committed action stream. Existing delay-aware solutions address this at the prohibitive cost of extensive retraining, architectural modifications, or fixed-prefix inpainting that inflates computation when low latency is vital. To address these problems, we propose FreeBar, a training-free real-time inference plugin that enables robust asynchronous execution with only marginal additional inference cost by casting chunk alignment in flow-based VLAs as barrier-guided action-space constraint. Specifically, FreeBar introduces a lightweight Control Barrier Function (CBF)-inspired consistency constraint between consecutive action chunks and solves the resulting correction in closed form, avoiding costly backpropagation through the VLA action head. By progressively tightening the consistency bound during generation, FreeBar aligns generated chunks with the committed execution stream while preserving early-stage generative and within-chunk future action flexibility,. Across Kinetix, RoboCasa, and diverse real-world robotic platforms with multiple VLA backbones, FreeBar improves success rates over naive asynchronous execution by 10%--15% on average, with larger gains in highly dynamic scenarios. It also preserves real-time computational efficiency, decreasing inference latency per action chunk over 45% than RTC. Additionally, as a universal plugin, FreeBar remains robust across distinct latency environments, base policies, and hyperparameter settings.

</details>


### [76] [Design and Evaluation of a Collaborative Teleoperation Interface for Multi-Operator Multi-Robot Systems in Challenging Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15001)
*Alperen Kenan,Juan José García Cárdenas,Adriana Tapus,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究设计并评估了一种共享可视化协作遥操作界面，通过16人对比实验表明：同地共享界面在多操作员多机器人任务中性能最优、认知负荷最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统整合互补平台能力，对复杂、难以进入环境中的任务至关重要。然而现有部署通常为每台机器人分配一名操作员或团队，异构平台接口不兼容，协作依赖操作员之间的手动沟通，导致认知负荷升高、跨厂商机器人的可扩展性受限。因此需要改善多操作员、多机器人系统中的操作员协调、态势感知与任务绩效。

Method: 设计并评估一种共享可视化协作遥操作界面。开展16名参与者的对比用户研究，比较三种遥操作条件：同地操作员+共享界面、远程操作员+共享界面、同地操作员+独立界面。参与者控制两台仿真机器人在核电站数字孪生中完成灾害响应任务，采集绩效指标、生理数据与行为指标，以评估任务绩效、认知负荷与操作员态势感知。

Result: 同地共享界面条件任务完成最快（292.8 s），优于远程操作（351.6 s）和独立界面（385.1 s）；碰撞最少（每次试验0.9次），态势感知准确率最高（91.1%）。物理同地伴随认知负荷降低，NASA-TLX分数从同地的38.2升至远程操作的57.7，生理指标结果一致。

Conclusion: 物理同地与共享界面提供互补收益：同地支持工作负荷调节，共享界面支持相互态势感知，二者共同提升多操作员、多机器人遥操作的整体表现。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems that combine the complementary capabilities of specialised platforms are critical for complex missions in challenging, inaccessible environments. Current deployments assign an operator or operator team to each robot, with collaboration relying on operator-to-operator communication to manually coordinate heterogeneous platforms with incompatible interfaces. This operator-dependent approach increases cognitive load and limits scalability across robots from different manufacturers. This study designs and evaluates a shared-visualisation collaborative teleoperation interface to improve operator coordination, situational awareness, and the task performance in multi-operator, multi-robot systems.
  A comparative user study with 16 participants compared three teleoperation conditions: co-located operators with a shared interface, remote operators with a shared interface, and co-located operators with individual interfaces. Participants controlled two simulated robots in a nuclear power plant digital twin to complete disaster response tasks. Performance metrics, physiological data, and behavioural measures were recorded to assess task performance, cognitive load, and operator awareness across conditions. The co-located shared-interface condition achieved the fastest mission completion (292.8 s), outperforming remote operation (351.6 s) and individual interfaces (385.1 s), with the fewest collisions (0.9 per trial) and highest situational awareness accuracy (91.1 %). Physical co-presence was associated with reduced cognitive load, with NASA-TLX scores increasing from 38.2 in co-located to 57.7 in remote operation, supported by consistent physiological indicators. These results demonstrate that co-presence and shared interfaces provide complementary benefits, supporting workload regulation and mutual awareness respectively in multi-operator, multi-robot teleoperation.

</details>


### [77] [Training-Free Multi-Agent Cooperative Embodied Search from Uncertain Vision-Language Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15038)
*Fan Pu,Keshu Wu,Jiwan Jiang,Shiyu Shen,Zhiwen Fan,Zhengzhong Tu,Jiachen Li,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量、免训练、不确定性感知的协作多智能体多目标搜索框架，将MLLM作为假设生成器与图优化解耦，在Matterport3D上提升覆盖率与搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实VLN任务处于部分可观测环境，地图结构、语义线索与目标位置常不完整或被基础模型错误推断；端到端导航策略缺乏可扩展性与跨环境泛化能力。

Method: 将高层语义推理与低层执行分离，以可扩展图优化为骨干。MLLM仅作假设生成器，输出语义区域、目标位置线索与可能可通行连接，作为不确定先验，再用在线观测与图上下文证据精化。协作导航建模为滚动时域多智能体混合整数线性规划（MILP），采用字典序目标：先最大化目标导向奖励，再最小化行驶距离、不确定边使用与重复访问。

Result: 基于100个Matterport3D测试用例的实验表明，相比代表性基线方法，目标覆盖率与搜索效率均提升；共享信息与联合规划使智能体负载更均衡。

Conclusion: 框架在抽象图上运行、不依赖具身相关策略参数，可随智能体与目标数量扩展，泛化到未见环境，并兼容不同感知模块与低层控制器而无需任务特定重训练。

Abstract: Real-world Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to act under partial observability, where map structure, semantic cues, and target locations may be incomplete or incorrectly inferred by foundation models. This paper presents a lightweight, training-free, and uncertainty-aware framework for cooperative multi-agent multi-target search in mapless environments. Instead of learning an end-to-end navigation policy, the framework separates high-level semantic reasoning from low-level execution through a scalable graph-optimization backbone. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) serve only as hypothesis generators, proposing semantic zones, target-location cues, and possible navigable connections. These outputs are treated as uncertain priors and further refined using online observations and graph-context evidence. Cooperative navigation is then formulated as a rolling-horizon multi-agent mixed-integer linear program with a lexicographic objective that first maximizes target-directed reward and then reduces travel distance, uncertain-edge use, and repeated visits. Experiments derived from 100 Matterport3D test cases show that the proposed framework improves target coverage and search efficiency over representative baseline methods, while shared information and joint planning enable more balanced workloads across agents. By operating on an abstract graph without relying on embodiment-specific policy parameters, the framework scales across varying numbers of agents and targets, generalizes to unseen environments, and accommodates different perception modules and low-level controllers without task-specific retraining.

</details>


### [78] [Modeling and Control of a Rigid-soft Hybrid-link Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15073)
*Zewen He,Taiki Ishigaki,Ko Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出针对刚性-柔性混合连杆类人机器人系统的逆运动学与柔顺性优化控制框架，采用分段常应变（PCS）模型描述柔性结构连续变形，并通过仿真与实物实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人引入被动柔性元件（构成刚性-柔性混合连杆系统）可存储能量、缓冲动态运动冲击，但该类系统的运动设计与控制难度大，文献中讨论有限。

Method: 提出逆运动学与柔顺性优化相结合的控制框架，利用分段常应变（PCS）模型表征柔性结构的连续变形；通过前向动力学仿真验证站立、跳跃、行走动作；并搭建采用板簧式假肢腿的双足类人机器人实验平台，用运动捕捉系统测量假肢变形。

Result: 前向动力学仿真验证了站立、跳跃与行走运动；实验平台在双支撑与单支撑两种工况下均验证了所提方法的站立平衡控制效果。

Conclusion: 所提逆运动学与柔顺性优化控制框架对刚性-柔性混合连杆类人系统有效，仿真与实验（双/单支撑站立平衡）均验证了其可行性。

Abstract: Physical flexibility plays a significant role not only in human motions but also in robot motions. For example, a humanoid robot with passive and flexible elements, which can be regarded as a rigid--soft hybrid-link system, has the advantages of storing energy and mitigating impact in a dynamic motion. However, the motion design and control of a hybrid-link system are challenging to achieve and have only been discussed in the literature. In this study, we propose the framework of inverse kinematics and compliance optimization-based control for a hybrid-link humanoid system that represents the continuous deformation of a flexible structure using the piece-wise constant strain (PCS) model. The proposed method was validated using forward dynamics simulations for standing, jumping, and walking motions. Then, we built an experimental setup using a biped humanoid robot with leaf-spring type prosthesis leg, in which the prosthesis deformation was measured by a motion capture system. Using this setup, we experimentally validated the proposed method for standing balance control in both double- and single-support cases.

</details>


### [79] [Targeting the Right Situations: Mutation Testing of Fuzzy-Powered Situation Hyperspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15122)
*Nawshin Mannan Proma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在SCALOFT仿真框架中通过变异测试验证：基于模糊逻辑的风险估计与真实系统故障有效相关，模糊引导的风险优先情境生成相比均匀随机生成具有更高的变异得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下矿井中自主无人机运行的安全保障面临运行设计域（ODD）庞大且不确定的挑战。随机测试等传统验证方法情境覆盖率低，常将计算资源分配给低风险场景，无法暴露关键边缘案例。作者基于前期风险优先情境覆盖工作，探究模糊逻辑风险估计能否有效关联真实系统故障。

Method: 在SCALOFT仿真框架内开展变异测试研究：系统性注入固定的软件变异体集合，并在两种测试策略下进行评估——(i) 均匀随机情境生成；(ii) 模糊引导的风险优先情境生成。

Result: 实验结果显示，模糊引导方法相较随机测试取得了更高的变异得分，且在暴露难检测故障方面更为有效。

Conclusion: 模糊逻辑风险估计与真实系统故障具有有效相关性，模糊引导的风险优先情境生成可提升无人机地下矿井作业安全验证的故障暴露效率。

Abstract: Ensuring the safety of autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating in underground mines is challenging due to the vast and uncertain Operational Design Domain (ODD). Traditional validation approaches, such as random testing, often result in inefficient situation coverage, allocating computational resources to low-risk scenarios while failing to expose critical edge cases. Building on prior work in risk-prioritized Situation Coverage, this paper investigates whether Fuzzy Logic-based risk estimation effectively correlates with real system failures. To this end, we conduct a mutation testing study within our SCALOFT simulation framework. A fixed set of software mutants is systematically injected and evaluated under two testing strategies: (i) uniformly random situation generation and (ii) Fuzzy-Guided risk-prioritized situation generation. Experimental results show that the Fuzzy-Guided approach achieves a higher mutation score and is more effective at exposing difficult-to-detect faults compared to random testing.

</details>


### [80] [RootSLAM: Square Root Bundle Adjustment for Visual-Inertial SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15157)
*Tomás Santucci,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers,Taihú Pire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RootSLAM，一个基于Basalt的多相机视觉惯性SLAM系统。通过将平方根束调整引入多相机VI-SLAM并利用位姿图重表述，使其能在单精度下运行，实现与SOTA相当的精度且速度最快提升20倍，并将开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型低功耗设备（机器人、混合现实、自动驾驶、无人机）依赖SLAM估计运动，但学习型SLAM精度高却计算开销大，经典几何方法效率高但需兼顾精度。作者希望在资源受限环境下同时获得高精度与高效率。

Method: 构建名为RootSLAM的全功能多相机视觉惯性SLAM系统，基于Basalt。核心是将structure from motion中的平方根束调整（square-root bundle adjustment）适配到多相机VI-SLAM场景，提出一种利用系统位姿图（pose-graph）的新重表述，使全局优化器可在单精度浮点下运行，从而将性能翻倍。系统包含地图管理、回环检测、位姿图优化器和全局建图组件。

Result: 精度与当前最优方法（SOTA）相当，同时运行速度最多快20倍。作者提供了与SOTA的详尽基准测试，以及对所提贡献的消融研究。

Conclusion: RootSLAM证明了几何方法在保持高精度的同时可大幅提升效率，利于感知系统在资源受限环境中的部署；其实现（含地图管理、回环检测、位姿图优化、全局建图）将开源。

Abstract: Efficient perception is a prerequisite for the small, low-power devices targeted by robotics, mixed reality, autonomous driving, and drone applications, all of which rely on Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) to estimate their motion within their surroundings. Learning-based SLAM systems achieve state-of-the-art accuracy but are demanding in compute, whereas classical geometry-based methods retain a strong efficiency advantage with little to no loss in accuracy. In this work we present RootSLAM, a fully-featured visual-inertial SLAM system for multi-camera setups built on Basalt. RootSLAM achieves accuracy competitive with the state of the art while running up to 20 times faster, facilitating the deployment of perception systems in resource constrained environments. We adapt the square-root bundle adjustment formulation from structure from motion into the multi-camera visual-inertial SLAM setting through a novel reformulation that leverages the system's pose-graph, letting the global optimizer run in single-precision floating point, thus doubling its performance. We provide a thorough benchmark against the state of the art together with an ablation study of our contributions. Our new implementation, including its map management and loop detection module, pose-graph optimizer, and global mapping components, will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [81] [nanoflann: A Header-Only KD-Tree Library for Exact Nearest-Neighbor Search on Compile-Time Product Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15215)
*José Luis Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种header-only、无依赖的C++ KD-tree库，支持在编译期声明的用户自定义乘积流形（R^n、SO(2)、SO(3)、S^n及其乘积SE(2)、SE(3)、环面）上做精确最近邻搜索，并提供支持高churn场景的单树增量自平衡索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人算法中大量使用最近邻搜索，但许多状态空间并非欧氏空间：姿态位于SO(2)/SO(3)、位姿位于SE(2)/SE(3)。在这些流形的原始坐标上用欧氏KD-tree会返回错误的邻居，缺少一种通用、精确且高效的流形最近邻方案。

Method: 设计header-only、零依赖的C++ KD-tree库，空间由基础流形（R^n、SO(2)、SO(3)、S^n及乘积SE(2)、SE(3)、环面）经单一type alias在编译期声明。库自动合成度量，其剪枝可证明是admissible的，对圆形坐标做wrap-aware处理、对单位四元数做double-cover-aware处理。另提供一个针对高churn（如滑窗LiDAR地图）或SE(3)轨迹的增量、自平衡单树索引，具有有界内存和可选的异步尾延迟约束模式。

Result: 实验证实精确性：在相同乘积度量下召回率为1.0（对比穷举扫描）；而"在原始坐标上直接用欧氏"的捷径在m个旋转块时召回率退化约0.5^m；相比暴力搜索最高有三个数量级的加速。增量索引相比先前工作在更新速度和内存上更优。

Conclusion: 该库为机器人领域的流形最近邻搜索提供了精确、高效且通用的解决方案，并开源（https://github.com/jlblancoc/nanoflann）。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor (NN) search underlies countless robotics algorithms, but many state spaces are not Euclidean: orientations live on $SO(2)$ or $SO(3)$ and poses on $SE(2)$ or $SE(3)$, and a Euclidean KD-tree on their raw coordinates returns wrong neighbors. We present a header-only, dependency-free C++ KD-tree library for exact NN search on user-defined product manifolds declared at compile time: the space is composed from base manifolds ($\mathbb{R}^n$, $SO(2)$, $SO(3)$, $S^n$; products $SE(2)$, $SE(3)$, tori) with a single type alias, and the library synthesizes a metric whose pruning is provably admissible, wrap-aware for circular coordinates and double-cover-aware for unit quaternions. Experiments confirm exactness (recall $1.0$ vs an exhaustive scan under the same product metric), where the Euclidean-on-raw shortcut collapses to recall $\approx 0.5^{m}$ for $m$ rotation blocks, with up to three orders of magnitude speedup over brute force. We further contribute an incremental, self-balancing single-tree index for points under heavy churn (e.g. sliding-window LiDAR maps) or $SE(3)$ trajectories: bounded memory and faster updates than prior work, with an optional asynchronous tail-latency-bounding mode. The implementation is open source: https://github.com/jlblancoc/nanoflann

</details>


### [82] [Reinforcement Learning Based Heterogeneous Task Allocation Under Spatio-Temporal Environmental Uncertainty for Multi-Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15237)
*Xi Lin,Yaxuan Li,Paul Szenher,Brendan Englot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究海上自主水面航行器（ASV）在多类型任务、未知时空扰动下的多机器人任务分配问题，提出一种去中心化强化学习策略网络，兼顾异构任务需求与不确定性鲁棒性，并在仿真中取得最低makespan，可扩展至100机器人、400任务规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ASV在海上任务中的部署增多，需要可靠的任务分配策略，能够适应异构任务及海上扰动的干扰；现有策略难以同时应对任务类型/出现时间的异构性与未知随机时空不确定性。

Method: 将问题建模为多机器人任务分配问题：一组能力异构的ASV需完成不同类型、不同出现时间的任务，并面临海上扰动带来的未知随机时空不确定性。提出去中心化RL策略网络，训练使其对不确定性引起的观测偏差具有鲁棒性，并支持个体决策以产生面向异构任务需求的高效执行行为。

Result: 在与四种基线方法的仿真对比中，改变任务类型比例、任务数量、机器人种类及每类机器人数量的设置下，所提策略均获得最低makespan，并可部署于100个机器人、400个任务的场景。

Conclusion: 所提去中心化RL策略能有效应对异构任务与不确定性扰动，在任务分配效率与可扩展性上优于基线方法。

Abstract: Autonomous Surface Vehicles (ASVs) have been increasingly deployed to perform different kinds of maritime tasks in recent years, which motivates the need for a reliable task allocation policy that can adapt to heterogeneous tasks and maritime disturbances. In this work, we formulate a multi-robot task allocation problem in which a team of ASVs with heterogeneous capabilities needs to complete tasks of different types and appearance times under unknown stochastic spatio-temporal uncertainties from maritime disturbances. We propose a decentralized Reinforcement Learning (RL) based policy network that is trained to be robust towards uncertainty-induced observation deviations, and is able to perform individual decision making that leads to efficient execution behaviors toward heterogeneous task requirements. We evaluate the proposed policy and four baseline methods in simulated experiments with varied proportions of different tasks types, numbers of tasks, robot species and robots per species. Experiment results show that our proposed policy achieves the lowest makespan and can be deployed to address scenarios with 100 robots and 400 tasks.

</details>


### [83] [Embodiment-Aware Inference-Time Search for Human Reaction Selection in Humanoid Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15274)
*Yongpeng Cao,Masahiro Hirano,Yuji Yamakawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出推理时反应搜索框架，用生成模型随机采样并结合交互质量与机器人可行性筛选，冻结生成器即可跨人形本体适配，人形重定向可行性提升1.61%，人空间与G1最优选择不一致率达78.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人学习人类行为的数据采集成本高昂，人-机器人交互数据更难获取；为每个本体训练专用反应模型耗时长、资源密集。人-人形运动重定向可复用人类-人类反应数据作为通用运动表示，但因人与机器人在形态与运动学上存在差异，人运动空间中偏好的反应未必适用于特定本体执行，需要一种能兼顾反应质量与本体可行性的适配方法。

Method: 提出推理时反应搜索框架：给定观测到的交互，利用生成式反应模型进行随机采样，生成多个合理的反应候选；将每个候选重定向到目标人形本体，并用互补的交互质量准则与本体特定可行性准则进行评估，从而在目标本体物理约束下完成候选选择。由于反应生成器保持冻结，框架可在推理阶段适配不同人形本体而无需重新训练。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在保持整体人类反应质量的同时提升了不同本体上重定向人形反应的可行性，人形重定向可行性提升1.61%；人空间与G1的最优候选选择在78.98%的样本上不一致，说明存在显著的本体依赖性。

Conclusion: 通过推理时搜索与本体可行性评估，可在不重训练生成器的前提下实现跨本体的反应重定向适应性，且本体差异对最优反应选择影响显著，作者将开源代码。

Abstract: Training humanoid robots to learn human behaviors requires expensive data collection, and human-robot interaction data can be even more difficult and costly to collect. Training a specialized reaction model for each humanoid embodiment can also be time-consuming and resource-intensive. Human-to-humanoid motion retargeting provides a more scalable alternative, allowing human-human reaction data to serve as a common motion representation for different humanoid embodiments. However, due to morphological and kinematic differences between humans and humanoid robots, a reaction preferred in human motion space may not be the most suitable one for execution on a particular robot embodiment.
  In this work, we propose an inference-time reaction search framework that leverages stochastic sampling from a generative reaction model and jointly considers reaction quality and humanoid embodiment feasibility. Given the observed interaction, our framework generates multiple plausible reaction candidates. Each candidate is retargeted to the target humanoid and evaluated using complementary interaction and embodiment-specific feasibility criteria, allowing candidate selection under the physical constraints of the target embodiment. Since the reaction generator remains frozen, the framework can adapt to different humanoid embodiments at inference time without retraining. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework maintains overall human reaction quality while improving the feasibility of retargeted humanoid reactions across different embodiments. The proposed pipeline improves humanoid retargeting feasibility by 1.61%. Moreover, human-space and G1 best-candidate selections disagree in 78.98% of samples, demonstrating substantial embodiment dependence. Code will be released.

</details>


### [84] [ProWorld: Multi-View World Model with Proprioceptive Feedback for Closed-Loop Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15294)
*Zijun Wang,Tianyi Yan,Haokun Lin,Panwen Hu,Bohang He,Siyu Ren,Xiaoyu Guo,Weijia Liufu,Shuoshuo Xue,Fengjiao Chen,Zipeng Ye,Xuezhi Cao,Zutao Jiang,Hanhui Li,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ProWorld 世界模型，联合预测多视角视频与机器人状态并闭环反馈给冻结策略，用失败交互增强训练，在 TriWorldBench 上取得最高分，生成与真实成功率相关性达 Pearson 0.95、Spearman 0.94。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过真实物理试验评估机器人操作策略成本高昂。作者研究动作条件世界模型能否作为闭环测试环境，使策略成功率与真机一致。除真实感视频外，模型还须返回策略下一步动作所需的相机视角与本体状态，并保留失败动作结果以避免高估策略能力。

Method: 提出 ProWorld，联合预测多视角视频和机器人状态，并将二者反馈给冻结策略进行重复交互。模型包含共享 transformer 用于联合视频-状态流预测，以及 Shared-Time View Encoding 以区分相机同时保持时间同步。为减少将漏抓变为成功举起的交互幻觉，用高保真仿真采集的失败交互增强训练。

Result: ProWorld 在对比方法中取得最高的 TriWorldBench 总分。在三种策略、四个本地操作任务上，生成成功率与真实成功率之间的 Pearson 相关系数为 0.95，Spearman 相关系数为 0.94。

Conclusion: 结果支持将联合视觉-本体反馈与失败感知训练作为基于世界模型的策略评估基础。

Abstract: Evaluating robot manipulation policies through repeated physical trials is costly. We study whether an action-conditioned world model can serve as a closed-loop test environment whose policy success rates track those on real robots. Beyond realistic video, the model must return the camera views and proprioceptive state needed for the policy's next action and preserve failed-action outcomes to avoid overestimating policy capability. We introduce ProWorld, a world model that jointly predicts multi-view video and robot state, then feeds both back to a frozen policy for repeated interaction. ProWorld comprises a shared transformer for joint video-state flow prediction and Shared-Time View Encoding to distinguish cameras while preserving temporal synchronization. To reduce interaction hallucinations that turn missed grasps into successful lifts, we augment training with failed interactions collected in high-fidelity simulation, reducing inflated success rates in the generated environment. ProWorld achieves the highest overall TriWorldBench score among the compared methods. Across three policies and four local manipulation tasks, it attains a Pearson correlation of 0.95 and a Spearman correlation of 0.94 between generated and real success rates. These results support joint visual-proprioceptive feedback and failure-aware training as a basis for world-model-based policy evaluation.

</details>


### [85] [RM-WAM: Reliable Memory-Augmented World Action Models for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15305)
*Cui Miao,Junhao Yu,Ping Liu,Ning Yang,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Xiaodong Wang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RM-WAM，一种可靠记忆增强的世界动作模型，将记忆视为依赖上下文的预测资源而非普遍有用的历史信息，通过效用感知门控实现长时序机器人操作中的选择性记忆利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型多基于当前观测做决策，在长时序任务中，视觉相似但交互历史与任务阶段不同的状态难以区分，可靠性不足；而直接引入历史观测又会带来过时、阶段不匹配或错误污染的信息，导致世界预测不准与闭环误差累积。

Method: RM-WAM用互补的短期视觉状态记忆与潜在事件记忆表示历史，分别捕捉近期交互动态与较长期状态转移；采用反事实记忆学习，在固定当前输入与预测目标的前提下变化可访问历史，从而学习上下文相关的记忆效用估计；并基于该估计设计效用感知门控机制，自适应地控制记忆注入世界-动作主干网络。

Result: 实验中RM-WAM体现了可靠且选择性记忆利用在长时序任务中的重要性。

Conclusion: 可靠、选择性的记忆利用对长时序机器人操作至关重要，RM-WAM通过效用感知的情景化记忆建模有效提升世界动作模型的决策可靠性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robotic manipulation by jointly predicting future visual states and actions, yet most existing methods make decisions primarily from the current observation. This limits their reliability in long-horizon tasks, where visually similar states may correspond to different interaction histories and task stages. Moreover, directly incorporating historical observations can introduce stale, stage-mismatched, or error-contaminated information, leading to inaccurate world prediction and accumulated closed-loop errors. We propose RM-WAM, a reliable memory-augmented World Action Model that treats memory as a context-dependent predictive resource rather than universally useful historical context. RM-WAM represents history using complementary short-term visual state memory and latent event memory, which respectively capture recent interaction dynamics and longer-term state transitions. A counterfactual memory learning scheme varies the accessible history while keeping the current inputs and prediction targets fixed, enabling the model to learn a context-dependent utility estimate for historical memory. Based on this estimate, a utility-aware gating mechanism adaptively controls memory injection into the world-action backbone. Experiments demonstrate that RM-WAM highlights the importance of reliable and selective memory utilization in long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [86] [An Open MuJoCo Simulation Framework for a Quadrotor Carrying a Tendon-Driven Soft Continuum Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15308)
*Haitham El-Hussieny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于MuJoCo的开源仿真框架，用于四旋翼下挂腱驱动软连续体机械臂，并给出建模参数、控制器设计与四个仿真场景的验证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行基座与高变形、欠驱动软臂的耦合动力学难以研究，缺少合适仿真器。

Method: 基于MuJoCo构建框架：机械臂用12段伪刚体链与正交柔顺铰链建模；3根间隔120°的腱贯穿各段实现任意方向弯曲；飞行器采用SE(3)几何跟踪控制器，旋翼分配矩阵由模型描述直接生成；采用闭式腱张力分配保证张力在限内且各方向弯矩一致。

Result: 四个仿真场景：36秒“8字+腱驱动末端扫掠”任务、姿态增益研究、弯曲工作空间准静态表征、末端脉冲扰动。标称增益下位置RMS误差3.1 cm，臂末端相对机体轴摆动达37 cm；低带宽姿态环会引发持续振荡；笔记本CPU上约15倍实时速度。

Conclusion: 该开源框架可有效仿真四旋翼-软连续体臂耦合系统，全部模型、控制器与复现脚本已发布。

Abstract: Combining aerial robots with soft continuum manipulators promises safe, compliant interaction with the environment, but the coupled dynamics of a free-flying base and a highly deformable, underactuated arm are difficult to study without a suitable simulator. This paper describes an open-source simulation framework, built on the MuJoCo physics engine, for a quadrotor that carries a tendon-driven soft continuum arm underneath its body. The arm is modelled as a pseudo-rigid-body chain of twelve segments joined by pairs of orthogonal compliant hinges. Three tendons, spaced \SI{120}{\degree} apart and routed through every segment, bend it in any direction. A geometric tracking controller on $SE(3)$ flies the vehicle; its rotor allocation matrix is generated directly from the model description. A closed-form tendon-tension allocation keeps every tension within its limits and gives the same bending moment in every direction. We report the full set of modelling choices and parameters, and we evaluate the framework in four simulated scenarios: a \SI{36}{\second} mission with a figure-eight and a tendon-driven tip sweep, an attitude-gain study, a quasi-static characterization of the bending workspace, and an impulsive disturbance applied to the arm tip. With the nominal gains the vehicle tracks the mission with \SI{3.1}{\centi\meter} RMS position error while the arm tip swings up to \SI{37}{\centi\meter} from the body axis. The gain study shows that a low-bandwidth attitude loop lets the arm excite sustained oscillations. The simulation runs about $15\times$ faster than real time on a laptop CPU. All models, controllers and scripts that reproduce the figures are released.

</details>


### [87] [CoLA: Multi-VLA Coordination via Learned Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15337)
*Ahaskar Kashyap,Jiawen Zong,Boyu Ma,Chuhao Zhou,Jingliang Li,Stefano V. Albrecht,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CoLA，一种在冻结 VLA 主干上训练轻量消息通道与共享扩散动作头、实现完全去中心化执行的多智能体协调框架，发现显式通信仅在信息私有时必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型通常作为孤立的单智能体策略部署。当多个此类智能体共享工作空间时，可通过互相观察隐式协调，或通过交换消息显式协调，但何时必须使用显式通信尚不明确。

Method: CoLA 保持各智能体 VLA 主干冻结，在其上训练轻量级消息通道与逐机械臂的扩散动作头，执行阶段完全去中心化，无需中心控制器。

Result: 在遮挡托盘交接任务中（颜色标记仅一个智能体可见），CoLA 成功率 76.0%，而全程置零消息仅 1.3%；在共享顶置相机的双臂与三臂交接任务中，移除消息无显著影响（90.6% vs 88.3%；63.5% vs 60.0%）；在双臂腕部视角变体上则下降（74.8% vs 64.3%）。冻结 π0.5 主干于遮挡托盘任务上，CoLA 以少量可训练参数达到与 LoRA 微调基线相当的成功率（37.3% vs 34.7%），且正确选盘率 71.8%，基线仅达随机水平（34.7% vs 33.3%）。

Conclusion: 显式通信仅在智能体信息私有（不可观测）时带来关键增益；CoLA 能以极少可训练参数实现高效多智能体协调，并可跨不同 VLA 主干泛化。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically deployed as isolated single-agent policies. When several such agents share a workspace, they can coordinate implicitly, by observing one another, or explicitly, by exchanging messages, but it remains unclear when explicit communication becomes necessary. We introduce CoLA (Coordination via Learned Adapters), which keeps each agent's VLA backbone frozen and trains a lightweight message channel and a per-arm diffusion action head on top, with fully decentralised execution. Across three tasks that differ in how much each agent can observe, the channel matters only when information is private. On an occluded tray handover, where the box's colour marker is visible to one agent only, CoLA reaches 76.0% success versus 1.3% with messages zeroed throughout training. On two-arm and three-arm handover tasks, removing messages has no measurable effect with a shared overhead camera (90.6% vs. 88.3% at two arms; 63.5% vs. 60.0% at three), but lowers success on the two-arm task's wrist-only variant (74.8% vs. 64.3%). This architecture generalises across different VLA backbones as well. With a frozen $π_{0.5}$ backbone on the same occluded tray task, CoLA matches the overall success rate of a LoRA-fine-tuned baseline (37.3% vs. 34.7%) using a fraction of the trainable parameters. When CoLA places the box, it selects the correct tray 71.8% of the time, whereas the independently fine-tuned baseline, whose placing arm never sees the marker, selects it at chance level (34.7% against 33.3%).

</details>


### [88] [LERR-Nav: Learning to Coordinate Exploration, Recovery, and Reminiscing for Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15362)
*Jingzhi Huang,Junkai Huang,Yuhang Peng,Keyu Mu,Hailong Huang,Haoyang Yang,Renbo Luo,Lujia Wang,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LERR-Nav目标导航框架，通过学习到的高层策略协调探索、恢复与回忆四类导航策略，在HM3D和MP3D上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知环境目标导航是embodied AI的核心难题，需要智能体将视觉与空间推理泛化到新布局。尽管LLM/VLM提升了语义建图、空间记忆与路点选择能力，但成功导航不只需选下一步路点：机器人还需识别停滞、摆脱无效行为、重新审视被忽略的目标线索。这既需要专门的导航策略，也需要协调这些策略执行的高层策略。作者基于失败导航回合的分析提出该框架。

Method: LERR-Nav包含四类互补导航策略：价值函数引导探索、LLM引导探索、停滞/重复行为的恢复、以及记忆引导的回忆。为支持历史感知的协调，维护执行记忆，保存所有策略执行的文本摘要与地图序列的滑动窗口。协调策略以Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct初始化高层视觉语言智能体，采用两阶段课程训练：第一阶段以地图为中心的监督微调（SFT）建立空间定位；第二阶段在变长导航片段上做SFT，根据当前地图与混合执行记忆学习下一步调用哪种导航策略。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D数据集上实验显示出最先进的导航性能，且消融研究验证了各组成部分的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过学习到的高层策略协调探索、恢复与回忆等专用策略，并结合执行记忆进行历史感知决策，可有效提升未知环境下的目标导航表现，各组件均被消融实验证实有效。

Abstract: Object Navigation in unseen environments remains a fundamental challenge in embodied AI, as it requires agents to generalize visual and spatial reasoning to novel layouts. While recent advances in large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) have enhanced semantic mapping, spatial memory, and waypoint selecting for navigation, successful navigation requires more than selecting the next promising waypoint. A robot must also recognize stalled progress, escape unproductive behaviors, and revisit overlooked target cues. Addressing these challenges requires both specialized navigation strategies and a high-level policy that coordinates their execution. Motivated by the analysis of failed navigation episodes, we propose LERR-Nav, an object navigation framework that coordinates exploration, recovery, and reminiscing through a learned high-level policy. The framework comprises four complementary navigation strategies: value-function-guided exploration, LLM-guided exploration, recovery from stalled or repetitive behavior, and memory-guided reminiscing. To support history-aware coordination, we maintain the execution memory that preserves textual summaries of all strategy executions alongside a sliding window of map sequences. To learn the coordination policy, we initialize a high-level vision-language agent from Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct and train it through a two-stage curriculum. The first stage uses map-centric supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to establish spatial grounding. The second stage performs SFT on variable-length navigation segments to learn which navigation strategy to invoke next based on current maps and the hybrid execution memory. Experiments on HM3D and MP3D demonstrate state-of-the-art navigation performance, while ablation studies validate the contributions of individual components.

</details>


### [89] [Have You Seen It Clearly? Uncertainty-Guided Belief Refinement for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15375)
*Jingzhi Huang,Junkai Huang,Yuhang Peng,Keyu Mu,Jiaru Zhong,Yi Wang,Lujia Wang,Lap-Pui Chau,Siyang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RefBeliefMap 不确定性引导建图框架与 RefBeliefNav 导航策略，实现零样本目标导航，在 HM3D/MP3D 上达到 SOTA 并完成四足机器人实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航方法利用大语言模型与视觉语言模型构建空间表示并引导探索，但存在两点不足：一是缺乏对"现有证据是否足以支撑导航决策"的可靠性评估；二是未能刻画新证据如何改变已访问位置的搜索价值，导致探索与重访难以协调。

Method: 1) RefBeliefMap：将目标位置信念与空间/语义证据不确定性区分开，在滑动记忆窗口上联合建模观测内不确定性与观测间分歧，评估两类证据通道的可靠性；当竞争前沿难以区分时，用不确定性估计驱动主动观测与语义推理，在导航承诺前精化证据。2) RefBeliefNav：在精化地图基础上，统一评估未探索前沿与历史路点，依据目标发现潜力、期望信息增益与导航代价进行联合决策，使历史位置可随新证据被重新评估。

Result: 在 HM3D 与 MP3D 数据集上，于所评估的零样本基线中取得最先进性能；消融实验验证各模块贡献；在 Unitree Go2 四足机器人上的真实场景实验验证了仿真到现实的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离并用不确定性量化目标信念与证据可靠性，并在统一目标下协调探索与重访，可有效提升零样本目标导航的性能与实机可迁移性。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Object Navigation (ZSON) requires an agent to locate target objects in unfamiliar environments without additional training for the test categories or environments. Recent approaches leverage large language models and vision-language models to construct spatial representations and guide exploration. However, whether the available evidence sufficiently supports a navigation choice, and how new evidence changes the search value of previously visited locations, remain insufficiently addressed. We propose RefBeliefMap, an uncertainty-guided mapping framework that distinguishes target-location belief from spatial and semantic evidence uncertainty. By jointly modeling intra-observation uncertainty and inter-observation disagreement over a sliding memory window, RefBeliefMap assesses the reliability of both evidence channels. When competing frontiers remain insufficiently resolved, these uncertainty estimates guide active observation and semantic reasoning to refine the evidence supporting target-location beliefs before navigation commitment. Building on the refined map, we introduce RefBeliefNav, which jointly evaluates unexplored frontiers and historical waypoints according to their target-finding potential, expected information gain, and navigation cost. This unified objective allows historical locations to be reconsidered as new evidence changes their search value, coordinating exploration and revisitation. Experiments on HM3D and MP3D demonstrate state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated zero-shot baselines, with ablation studies examining the contributions of modules. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further demonstrate the sim-to-real transferability of our method.

</details>


### [90] [A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Safety-Aware Haptic Shared Control in Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15422)
*Zihao Zhang,Karl Handwerker,Balint Varga*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将手术机器人触觉共享控制建模为集中训练-分散执行的两智能体合作马尔可夫博弈，用无模型多智能体强化学习比较MADDPG、rMAPPO与epigraph约束方法，并在仿真与真人操作下评估几何安全策略对任务执行与力协调的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉共享控制在共享末端执行器上融合人手与机器人作用力，同时保留操作者参与。但几何安全选择如何影响任务执行与力协调尚不明确，且通常缺乏完整解析交互模型，因此需要一个统一的基准来研究人类控制表征与约束处理的权衡。

Method: 将人-机交互建模为集中训练、分散执行的两智能体合作马尔可夫博弈；采用无模型多智能体强化学习框架进行联合策略自适应，无需完整解析交互模型；将学习的人类控制与任务调优的固定阻抗基线对比，并在共同几何任务定义下比较MADDPG、rMAPPO与epigraph约束方法。

Result: 仿真中rMAPPO总体取得最高回报与跟踪性能；epigraph方法运动更保守、几何暴露更低，几何迁移后未观察到碰撞或危险区域暴露。使用真实操作者输入时，固定阻抗训练的策更快且在所有记录试验中均成功；以学习伙伴训练的策表现出更强的指令对抗，而固定epigraph条件产生最一致的指令。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究触觉共享控制中人类控制表征与约束处理之间的权衡提供了基准，揭示了安全性、任务性能与力协调之间存在的取舍关系。

Abstract: Haptic shared control in surgical robotics combines human-side and robot-side forces at a shared end effector while preserving operator involvement. However, the effects of geometric safety choices on task execution and force coordination remain unclear. We model the interaction as a two-agent cooperative Markov game under centralized training and decentralized execution, comparing learned human control with a task-tuned fixed-impedance baseline. This model-free multi-agent reinforcement learning framework supports joint policy adaptation without requiring a complete analytical interaction model. It compares MADDPG and rMAPPO with an epigraph-constrained approach under common geometric task definitions. In simulation, rMAPPO generally achieved the highest return and tracking performance, whereas the epigraph approach produced more conservative motions and lower geometric exposure, including no observed collision or dangerous-region exposure after geometry transfer. With real operator input, fixed-impedance-trained policies were faster and successful in all recorded trials. Policies trained with a learned partner exhibited greater command opposition, while the fixed epigraph condition produced the most aligned commands. This work provides a benchmark for studying human-control representation and constraint-handling trade-offs in haptic shared control.

</details>


### [91] [Exploring the Limits of Combinatorial Generalization in Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15425)
*Xupeng Zhang,Yantai Yang,Chang Guo,Zhaokai Yin,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HSE 诊断框架与 RoboCombine 基准，揭示 VLA 长程操作失败源于原子技能间"起始状态差距"，并提出 PRTI 后训练策略提升技能重组泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型能执行原子技能，但长程操作需在无每种技能组合演示的情况下重组技能。作者发现全指令训练在有限的组合序列上达到 40.0% 有序成功率，但在未见重组上仅 7.7%，表明模型是在记忆序列模板而非真正的技能重组，因此需要诊断失败来源的方法与基准。

Method: 1) 提出 Hierarchical Subtask Execution (HSE) 诊断框架，通过规划器、触发器、策略三者共享的模块化接口暴露技能重组，将长程失败分解为可分离的模式；2) 提出 RoboCombine 基准，在无组合技能训练轨迹条件下评估技能重组，包含有序成功率与跨子任务边界转换转化率指标；3) 提出 Pose Robust Task Initialization (PRTI)，一种轻量级原子任务后训练策略，扩展起始状态覆盖范围以缓解"起始状态差距"。

Result: RoboCombine 揭示原子能力本身不足以保证有序执行，失败集中在转换处——下游子任务从前序子任务的交接状态启动，而非规范原子起始状态，作者称之为"起始状态差距"。在 RoboCombine 上，oracle 定时 HSE 下 PRTI 达到 18.16% 有序成功率，随机扰动为 4.21%，机器人重置 oracle 为 9.47%，且无需组合技能训练轨迹。

Conclusion: 组合泛化要求子任务具备"交接鲁棒"的原子技能，能把前阶段进展转化为可靠的下游执行，而非仅仅将任务分解为已知子任务。

Abstract: In embodied manipulation, Vision Language Action (VLA) models can execute atomic skills, but long-horizon manipulation requires recombining these skills without demonstrations for every skill combination. The full instruction route treats skill chaining as long-horizon imitation. In a controlled study, full instruction training on limited skill combination sequences reaches 40.0% ordered success on covered combinations but only 7.7% on recombination, suggesting memorization of sequence templates rather than robust skill recombination. We introduce Hierarchical Subtask Execution (HSE), a diagnostic framework that exposes skill recombination through a shared modular interface over the planner, trigger, and policy, decomposing long-horizon failures into separable modes. We introduce RoboCombine, a benchmark for skill recombination without combined skill training trajectories, with metrics for ordered success and transition conversion across subtask boundaries. RoboCombine reveals that atomic competence alone does not ensure ordered execution. Failures concentrate at transitions where downstream subtasks launch from handoff states from preceding subtasks rather than canonical atomic starts. We identify this mismatch as the start state gap. To address it, we introduce Pose Robust Task Initialization (PRTI), a lightweight atomic task post-training strategy that broadens start state coverage beyond canonical starts. Under oracle timed HSE on RoboCombine, PRTI reaches 18.16% ordered success, compared with 4.21% under random perturbation and 9.47% under a robot-reset oracle, without combined skill training trajectories. These results show that combinatorial generalization requires handoff robust atomic skills that convert first stage progress into reliable downstream execution, not merely decomposition into known subtasks. Code: https://github.com/AutoLab-SAI-SJTU/RoboCombine.

</details>


### [92] [Learning Safety-Constrained Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation for Lunar Surface Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15486)
*Alvaro Belmonte-Baeza,Miguel Cazorla,Gabriel J. García,Carlos J. Pérez-Del-Pulgar,Jorge Pomares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向四足移动操作机器人的约束强化学习框架，用单一全身策略跟踪任务空间6D末端位姿，在月面模拟环境中实现高精度与低约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月面作业中机器人故障不可恢复且无法持续人工监控，必须在保持自身完整性的前提下自主运行，因此需要在学习阶段就强制执行操作约束。

Method: 基于约束强化学习框架学习单一全身策略，跟踪任务空间6D末端位姿；通过约束学习机制在学习中施加操作限制；设计使移动与操作之间转换由奖励几何自然涌现（而非显式门控）的奖励形式；引入基于重力-惯性倾覆裕度（GIIM）并由月面低重力动力学启发的稳定性项；并给出源自终止目标乘性结构的奖励正则化设计规则。

Result: 在月面模拟环境中评估，覆盖3种地形形态、5个难度等级、月面重力，以及按JSC-1a模拟物标定并从松散到密实的月壤接触参数。标称条件下平均位置误差3.22 cm、姿态误差6.83°；该精度在不同月壤密度及代表性地形难度范围内保持；相比无约束基线，软约束违反时间最多降低一个数量级，且未出现硬约束违反。

Conclusion: 所提约束强化学习框架能够在月面环境下以单一全身策略兼顾6D位姿跟踪精度、地形与月壤适应性及操作安全性，验证了稳定性项与奖励正则化设计在低重力移动操作任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic systems for lunar surface operations must act autonomously and preserve their own integrity in an environment where failures are unrecoverable and continuous supervision is impossible. This paper presents a Constrained Reinforcement Learning framework for quadrupedal mobile manipulators that learns a single whole-body policy for tracking a 6D end-effector pose specified in task space, with operational limits enforced during learning through a constrained learning mechanism. We introduce a reward formulation in which the transition between locomotion and manipulation emerges from the reward geometry rather than from an explicit gating mechanism, a stability term derived from the Gravito-Inertial Inclination Margin and motivated by the reduced-gravity dynamics of the lunar surface, and a design rule for reward regularization that follows from the multiplicative structure of the termination-based objective. The framework is evaluated in a lunar-analog simulation spanning three terrain morphologies at five difficulty levels under lunar gravity, with regolith contact properties calibrated to the JSC-1a simulant and varied from loose to dense. The learned policy attains mean position and orientation errors of 3.22 cm and 6.83 deg. under nominal conditions, retains this precision across regolith densities and over the operationally representative range of terrain difficulty, and reduces soft-constraint violation time by up to an order of magnitude relative to an unconstrained baseline while incurring no hard-constraint violations.

</details>


### [93] [Phase-Gated Constrained Reinforcement Learning for End-to-End Capture and Detumbling of Non-Cooperative Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15491)
*Álvaro Belmonte-Baeza,Celia Redondo-Verdú,José L. Ramón,Miguel Cazorla,Leonard Felicetti,Jorge Pomares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出单一约束强化学习策略完成"接近—抓取—消旋"全流程主动碎片清除任务，通过平滑接近门机制将相位结构内生化，成功率99.5%且零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动碎片清除将接近、捕获、消旋分割为多个阶段专用控制器，依赖目标惯量已知、专用抓持接口或人工设计的接触切换逻辑，而这些环节恰是任务最脆弱之处。作者希望用单一策略端到端覆盖全任务，且不依赖目标质量惯量与精确状态。

Method: 将任务建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，采用单一约束强化学习策略驱动双臂自由飞行服务航天器。核心机制是"平滑接近门"，同时作用于三处：(1) 奖励函数，诱导而非强制相位结构；(2) 动作空间，以硬保证方式封堵退化的"抓取即拖拽"投机行为；(3) 约束集合，解决约束学习的归一化失效问题——跨相位约束因各相位负载不均而在最关键精度相位近乎失效。观测仅含噪声估计，目标质量与惯量未知。

Result: 1000次随机试验中成功率99.5%，全程满足接触力、执行器、基座反作用力与碰撞限制；可零样本迁移到未见几何构型与质心偏移，并在十倍目标质量范围内外推有效。

Conclusion: 单一约束强化学习策略可替代多阶段专用控制器流水线，实现免专用接口的摩擦抓取与全消旋；平滑接近门既是相位结构生成机制，也是约束归一化问题的通用补救方案，其失效模式与修复思路适用于同类多相位问题。

Abstract: Active debris removal requires a servicer spacecraft to approach a non-cooperative tumbling object, capture it, and arrest its rotation. This sequence is conventionally divided among phase-specific controllers, each depending on knowledge of the target's inertia, a dedicated grappling fixture, or a hand-designed switch at the contact-rich seam where such pipelines are most fragile. We show that a single constrained reinforcement learning policy can execute the entire mission, driving a dual-arm free-flying servicer from free-space closing through friction-based fixture-free capture to full detumbling of a target whose mass and inertia are unknown and whose state is observed only through noisy estimates. The enabling mechanism is a smooth proximity gate applied at three loci of a constrained Markov decision process. In the reward it induces the phase structure rather than imposing it; in the action space it forecloses a degenerate grab-and-pull exploit as a hard guarantee; and in the constraint set it resolves a normalization failure of constraint-based learning, in which a constraint spanning phases that load it unequally is rendered nearly inert in the phase where precision matters most. Both the failure mode and the remedy apply to any multi-phase problem of this kind. Over 1000 randomized trials the policy succeeds in 99.5% while holding its contact-force, actuator, base-reaction and collision limits throughout, transfers zero-shot to unseen geometries and centre-of-mass offsets, and extrapolates across a tenfold range of target mass.

</details>


### [94] [GNFormer: Gaussian Neural Field Transformer for Efficient Camera-based Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15522)
*Guangcheng Chen,Li He,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯神经场（GNF）及其实时预测网络GNFormer，用于相机占用预测，通过高斯与神经网络联合参数化提升表达力与基元利用率，在多个基准上取得最高IoU/mIoU且推理最快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯的相机占用预测方法存在两个问题：一是训练目标不足导致部分高斯被置于空白区域，基元利用率低、浪费计算与内存；二是高斯函数表达能力有限，难以建模复杂形状。

Method: 提出GNF作为三维表示，将体素位置映射为语义占用。高斯定义场的空间支撑并为神经解码提供特征，神经网络与高斯联合参数化该场，实现比单独高斯函数更强的占用建模能力。进一步设计GNFormer，一种简洁灵活的网络，从相机输入预测GNF。

Result: 在Occ-ScanNet、SSCBench-KITTI-360和nuScenes-SurroundOcc基准上，GNFormer在所比较的单帧方法中取得最高IoU和mIoU及最快推理速度；室内外场景分别仅需256和1024个高斯即可表示，显著少于先前基于高斯的方法。

Conclusion: GNF与GNFormer验证了高斯-神经联合参数化在相机占用预测中的有效性，能大幅减少基元数量并兼顾精度与速度，代码将开源。

Abstract: Camera-based occupancy prediction provides geometric and semantic scene understanding for robot perception and has been adopted in autonomous vehicle and UAV perception systems. Gaussian primitives offer a promising alternative by potentially representing only occupied regions, thereby concentrating computation and representational capacity where needed and reducing wasted computation and memory in empty space. However, existing Gaussian-based methods suffer from poor primitive utilization, as ineffective training objectives leave some Gaussians in empty space, while the limited expressiveness of Gaussian functions restricts their ability to model complex shapes. To address these limitations, we propose a novel 3D representation, Gaussian Neural Field (GNF), which can accurately map voxel positions to semantic occupancies. In GNF, Gaussians and a neural network jointly parameterize the field, where the Gaussians define its spatial support and provide features for neural decoding, while the neural network enables more expressive occupancy modeling than Gaussian functions alone. We further develop the Gaussian Neural Field Transformer (GNFormer), a streamlined and flexible method that predicts GNFs for camera-based occupancy prediction. Across Occ-ScanNet, SSCBench-KITTI-360, and nuScenes-SurroundOcc benchmarks, GNFormer achieves the highest IoU and mIoU and the fastest inference among the compared single-frame methods. In the experiments, GNFormer can represent indoor and outdoor scenes with only 256 and 1024 Gaussians, respectively, substantially fewer primitives than prior Gaussian-based methods. The code will be released.

</details>


### [95] [PointWalker: Mapless Quadruped Locomotion Directly From Raw LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15548)
*Sicen Li,Zhen Chu,Chao Li,Jun Zheng,Qiuguo Zhu,Jun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PointWalker，一个无需地图的四足机器人导航框架，单一强化学习策略直接以四路LiDAR原始点云为输入，实现多种异构地形通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在感知与控制之间插入地形地图或深度图像，前者维护成本高、易受里程计漂移与离群点影响且被可穿透格栅误导，后者受光照与表面材质限制，难以应对支撑稀疏或可透视的异构地形。

Method: 体素化点云经线性自注意力编码，再通过交叉注意力与本体感知融合，地形几何直接进入策略，无需地图或落脚点估计；采用一次PPO训练，配合地形感知奖励与可穿透格栅的双通道仿真。

Result: 单一策略覆盖全部七类地形，仿真平均成功率达90.1%；在Deep Robotics X30上以50 Hz机载零样本部署，跨越0.8米间隙、攀爬0.8米高台与45°钢格栅楼梯、承载50公斤负载，并完成2.5公里山地路线。

Conclusion: 直接从原始LiDAR点云学习策略的免地图方案可有效支撑四足机器人在复杂异构地形中的实地巡检任务，兼具通用性与实用性。

Abstract: Quadruped robots deployed for inspection and field missions must cross heterogeneous terrain, from stairs, slopes, and gaps to high platforms, stepping stones, and balance beams, where safe support is sparse or see-through. Existing methods interpose a terrain map or a depth image between sensing and control. Maps are costly to maintain, corrupted by odometry drift and outliers, and misled by see-through gratings, while depth cameras degrade with lighting and surface material. In this paper, we present PointWalker, a mapless framework whose single reinforcement learning policy directly consumes raw point clouds from four light detection and ranging (LiDAR) sensors. Voxelized points are encoded by linear self-attention and fused with proprioception through cross-attention, so terrain geometry reaches the policy without maps or foothold estimation. One proximal policy optimization (PPO) run, with terrain-aware rewards and dual-channel simulation of see-through gratings, yields one policy for all seven terrain classes with a 90.1% mean success rate in simulation. Deployed zero-shot on a Deep Robotics X30 at 50 Hz onboard, it crosses 0.8-m gaps, climbs 0.8-m platforms and 45° steel-grating stairs, carries a 50-kg payload, and completes a 2.5-km mountain route.

</details>


### [96] [Analytic Robot Self-Model: Learning Screw-Axis Kinematics for Rendering and Model-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15558)
*Chen Xu,Ning Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种显式解析的机器人自模型，无需形变网络或URDF/CAD模板：利用多视角图像与关节读数建立高斯外观并推断刚性连杆，用稀疏末端位姿独立辨识度量螺旋轴，从而得到闭式正运动学与解析雅可比。仿真七轴臂、十二关节四足和真实六轴臂均验证有效，真机末端位姿中位误差6.4 mm（标定名义模型为11.7 mm）。对照实验表明仅图像拟合不足以保证运动学准确：PSNR仅差0.54 dB的两个Panda模型，末端预测误差相差13.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉自建模虽能让机器人从观测中学习身体模型，但模型通常隐式编码运动学，不暴露可用于计算与控制的度量关节参数；且对图像的良好拟合本身并不能保证运动学准确。因此需要一种无需形变网络与URDF/CAD模板、能给出显式度量运动学参数的解析自模型。

Method: 多视角图像与关节读数用于建立高斯外观并为刚性连杆分配提供运动证据；稀疏末端执行器位姿独立辨识度量螺旋轴。基于此得到闭式正运动学与解析雅可比，并可外推至拟合范围之外。验证对象包括仿真七轴臂、仿真十二关节四足和真实六轴臂。

Result: 仿真中实现毫米级到达与抓放，以及四足闭环行走。真实机械臂上可渲染留出构型，末端位置预测中位误差6.4 mm，优于标定名义模型的11.7 mm，并支持借助外部标记反馈的到达任务。对照实验显示，仅关节轴不同的两个Panda模型PSNR仅差0.54 dB，但末端预测误差相差13.7倍，说明图像拟合不足以反映运动学精度。

Conclusion: 显式解析自模型可在无模板条件下获得度量准确的运动学，其闭式正运动学与解析雅可比支持超出拟合范围的评估与控制，且精度优于标定名义模型；图像拟合质量不能作为运动学准确性的充分判据。

Abstract: Visual self-modelling lets robots learn body models from observations, but such models usually encode kinematics implicitly rather than exposing metric joint parameters for computation and control. Moreover, a good fit to images does not by itself establish accurate kinematics. Here we present an explicit analytic robot self-model that requires neither deformation networks nor URDF/CAD templates. Multi-view images and joint readings establish Gaussian appearance and provide motion evidence for rigid link assignments, whereas sparse end-effector poses independently identify metric screw axes. The resulting closed-form forward kinematics and analytic Jacobian can be evaluated beyond the fitting ranges. We validate the model on a simulated seven-axis arm, a simulated twelve-joint quadruped and a real six-axis arm. In simulation, it achieves millimetre-level reaching and pick-and-place, as well as closed-loop quadruped walking. On the real arm, it renders held-out configurations, predicts end-effector positions with a median error of 6.4 mm, compared with 11.7 mm for a calibrated nominal model, and supports reaching with external marker feedback. A controlled comparison shows that image fit alone is not enough: two Panda models differing only in their joint axes are 0.54 dB apart in PSNR, yet their end-effector prediction errors differ 13.7-fold.

</details>


### [97] [Stereo-Planner: Learning Stereo-to-Trajectory Planning for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15560)
*Hongming Shen,Junjie Lu,Xun Chen,Wei Wang,Xinhang Xu,Bailing Tian,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Stereo-Planner，一种从双目 RGB 观测直接生成轨迹的端到端框架，实现零样本 sim-to-real 规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航系统依赖专用深度传感器或显式深度重建；直接从 RGB 学习轨迹生成面临 sim-to-real 域差距的挑战。

Method: 将轨迹生成建模为状态与尺度条件下的隐式双目几何查询。Scene Representor 利用预训练视觉基础模型提取跨域共享的几何线索；轻量 Trajectory Decoder 通过交错注意力分离轨迹-状态交互与场景特征检索，并引入双目基线作为度量尺度线索。完全在仿真中通过最小化可微轨迹代价训练，真实环境零微调部署。

Result: 仿真中指令速度 8 m/s 下成功率 95%，跨场景泛化强；仅用双目 RGB 感知即可与基于深度的规划器竞争。在搭载 NVIDIA Orin NX 的小型四旋翼上实现最高 7 m/s 自主飞行，机载 vision-to-action 平均推理时间 24.27 ms。

Conclusion: Stereo-Planner 验证了以双目 RGB 实现零样本 sim-to-real 轨迹规划的可行性，兼具泛化性与实时性，软件、仿真环境与硬件设计将开源。

Abstract: Existing navigation systems commonly rely on dedicated depth sensors or explicit depth reconstruction, while learning to generate trajectories directly from RGB images remains challenging due to the sim-to-real domain gap. In this work, we propose Stereo-Planner, an end-to-end vision-to-trajectory framework that enables zero-shot sim-to-real planning from stereo RGB observations. The key idea is to formulate trajectory generation as state- and scale-conditioned querying of latent stereo geometry. A Scene Representor incorporates a pretrained vision grounding foundation model to extract geometric cues shared across simulated and real-world environments, supporting generalization across visual domains. A lightweight Trajectory Decoder then translates these representations into trajectories through interleaved attention, separating trajectory-state interaction from scene-feature retrieval and incorporating the stereo baseline as a metric-scale cue. With these designs, Stereo-Planner is trained entirely on simulated data by minimizing differentiable trajectory costs and deployed directly in real-world environments without fine-tuning. Benchmark results demonstrate strong cross-scene generalization, with a 95% success rate in simulation environments at a commanded speed of 8 m/s, and competitive performance against depth-based planners using only stereo RGB observations for scene perception. Furthermore, deployment on a compact quadrotor equipped with an NVIDIA Orin NX enables autonomous flight at speeds of up to 7 m/s, with a mean onboard vision-to-action inference time of 24.27 ms. The software, simulation environments, and hardware designs will be open-sourced at https://github.com/Stereo-Planner/Stereo-Planner.

</details>


### [98] [Anticipatory Textile MMG-Strain Sensing for Confirm-Then-Assist Soft Knee Exosuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15563)
*Zheng Ma,Xiao Jin,Zhenhua Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一种针织纺织接口在同一500 Hz时钟下同步采集合成肌声图（MMG）与应变信号，实现运动起始阶段的方向判别与助力触发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体可穿戴机器人在运动起始时需提前给出助力，但现有信号存在固有缺陷：sEMG虽能早期反映神经肌肉驱动，却在汗液与电极位移下退化；运动学传感器只有在肢体实际运动后才响应；同时缺乏方向信息，导致助力时机与方向判断困难。

Method: 在单件针织纺织接口上集成三通道MMG阵列与前侧应变传感器，共用一个同步500 Hz时钟采样。利用MMG检测肌肉激活的早期时刻，并用同一时钟下应变斜率符号判定运动方向，无需训练或逐人标定。融合信号驱动两阶段"先确认后助力"策略，控制软体气动膝关节。

Result: 5名受试者中，MMG激活较应变检出的膝关节运动在坐-立转换中提前，各通道中位数为322–344 ms。同一时钟采样下，应变斜率符号在650次完整转换中649次正确判别方向，无训练与个体标定，将四分类平衡准确率从90.3%提升至95.6%。4名受试者240次试验中，机载控制器在80次坐-立中78次进入助力状态，80次等长共收缩中0次误触发；但在80次站-坐中27次误进入助力状态，直至保持超时退出，需引入拮抗屈肌测量装置在起始阶段解决该方向问题。

Conclusion: 在同一时钟上共采肌肉声学与纺织应变信号，可在运动起始附近发出充气指令，验证了MMG与应变融合用于软体可穿戴机器人起始助力与方向判别的可行性，但站-坐方向的误触发仍需通过对向传感器解决。

Abstract: Soft wearable robots must commit to assistance around movement onset, when the available signals are ambiguous. Surface electromyography (sEMG) detects neuromuscular drive early but degrades under sweat and electrode shift, and kinematic sensors respond only once the limb moves. This letter presents a knitted textile interface that co-samples a three-channel mechanomyography (MMG) array and an anterior strain sensor on a single synchronous 500 Hz clock. Across five participants, MMG activation preceded strain-detected knee motion during sit-to-stand by per-channel medians of 322-344 ms. Sampled on the same clock, the sign of the strain slope resolved movement direction on 649 of 650 completed transitions with no training or per-participant calibration, raising four-class balanced accuracy from 90.3% to 95.6%. The fused signals drove a two-stage confirm-then-assist policy for a soft pneumatic knee. Across 240 trials in four participants, the onboard controller entered the assist state on 78 of 80 sit-to-stand trials and on none of the 80 isometric co-contractions. It also entered the assist state on 27 of 80 stand-to-sit trials and left it at the hold timeout, motivating an antagonistic flexor gauge to resolve this direction at onset. Co-sampling muscle acoustics with textile strain on one clock lets the inflation command be issued around movement onset.

</details>


### [99] [Look Before You Move: Agent-Interface-Aligned Supervision for Open-World Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15572)
*Ronghe Qiu,Teli Ma,Jiaming Zhou,Zeying Gong,Yiting He,Qiang Huang,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AIDA数据采集管线：让教师通过已部署agent的技能接口直接选取并执行动作，为移动操作任务提供动作选择与主动视觉的示范；训练所得AIDA-OWMM在OWMM上达71.76%全任务成功率，并具备零样本真机迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界移动操作要求机器人依据语言指令完成"找物体—接近—搬运至目标容器"的全流程，训练VLM agent需要动作选择与主动视觉两类示范。现有管线多将规划轨迹转为高层动作标签，但最短路径进度对"该看哪里"的监督十分有限，主动视觉示范缺失。

Method: 提出Agent-Interface Data Alignment（AIDA）数据采集管线：教师直接通过部署agent的技能接口选择并执行动作，而非回放规划轨迹。针对移动操作设计维持目标可见、丢失目标恢复、接近以拾取或放置等教师技能。加入基于模板的空间推理监督，并使用共享harness统一组织训练与推理时的观测、历史与输出。

Result: AIDA-OWMM在170条留出的OWMM episode上取得71.76%的全任务成功率；教师敏感性分析表明主动视觉示范能提升学生性能；仿真训练的agent在真实移动机器人上无需额外训练达到38%端到端成功率；将AIDA应用于EmbodiedBench的导航子集亦验证了其在导航任务上的通用性。

Conclusion: 通过技能接口直接采集教师示范可有效对齐数据与部署接口，并为主动视觉提供监督信号，是训练开放世界移动操作VLM agent的可行且可迁移的范式。

Abstract: Open-world mobile manipulation requires a robot to find an object, approach it, and move it to a target receptacle from a language instruction. Training vision-language model (VLM) agents for these tasks requires demonstrations of both action selection and active vision. Existing pipelines often convert planned trajectories into high-level action labels, but shortest-path progress provides limited supervision for deciding where to look. We introduce Agent-Interface Data Alignment (AIDA), a data collection pipeline in which a teacher directly selects and executes actions through the deployed agent's skill interface. For mobile manipulation, we design teachers that maintain target visibility, recover lost targets, and approach for picking or placing. We add template-based spatial reasoning supervision and use a shared harness to organize observations, history, and outputs during training and inference. The resulting agent, AIDA-OWMM, achieves 71.76% full-task success on 170 held-out OWMM episodes. Teacher sensitivity analysis shows that active-vision demonstrations improve student success. The simulation-trained agent achieves 38% end-to-end success on a real mobile robot without additional training. Applying AIDA to a navigation subset of EmbodiedBench further demonstrates the pipeline's utility for navigation tasks.

</details>


### [100] [SIGN-MAPPO: State-Intent-Aware Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Driving under Imperfect V2X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15577)
*Zide Fan,Jiahui Yuan,Kaixin Zhang,Junhao Fan,Xiaohe Li,Yiru Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SIGN-MAPPO框架，通过车辆状态与意图感知的图注意力多智能体强化学习，实现不完美V2X通信下混合交通无信号交叉口的协同驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通无信号交叉口协同驾驶面临三大挑战：V2X通信不完美、人工驾驶车辆（HV）意图无法直接通信、各CAV独立生成的穿越决策可能互不兼容。

Method: 三项协同机制：（1）每个具备决策资格的CAV基于自车状态与可用同伴状态记录重建面向接收方的交通图，由共享图注意力策略生成带有HV意图预测特征的原始Stop/Go意图；（2）CAV意图通过带时间戳的V2V消息交换；（3）本地冲突感知仲裁层将各CAV意图转化为安全可执行动作，以应对同伴信息可能不一致的情况。

Result: SUMO主评估中完成率97.53%、碰撞率0.10%、平均穿越时间35.28 s；消融实验证实HV意图预测与CAV意图交换的互补贡献；在所评估的通信延迟与丢包范围内策略保持鲁棒；跨场景测试均实现完全清空，但在HV行为复合不可预测时安全裕度收窄。

Conclusion: SIGN-MAPPO能有效应对不完美V2X下的混合交通协同驾驶，性能与鲁棒性良好，但面对复合不可预测的HV行为时安全裕度仍需关注。

Abstract: Cooperative driving at mixed-traffic unsignalized intersections is challenged by imperfect vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication, human-driven vehicle (HV) intentions that cannot be directly communicated, and the potential incompatibility of independently generated crossing decisions. This paper proposes State-Intent-Aware Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Driving under Imperfect V2X (SIGN-MAPPO) to address these challenges through three coordinated mechanisms. Each decision-eligible connected and automated vehicle (CAV) first reconstructs a receiver-specific traffic graph from its ego state and the peer-state records available to it, from which a shared graph-attention policy generates a raw Stop/Go intention augmented with predicted HV-intent features. CAV intentions are then exchanged through timestamped vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) messages, and a local conflict-aware arbitration layer converts each CAV's intention into a safe executable action despite potentially inconsistent peer information. In the principal Simulation of Urban MObility (SUMO) evaluation, SIGN-MAPPO achieves a 97.53% completion rate, a 0.10% collision rate, and a mean intersection traversal time of 35.28 s. Ablation studies further confirm the complementary contributions of HV-intent prediction and CAV-intent exchange to this performance. The trained policy also remains robust under the evaluated communication-delay and packet-loss ranges, and cross-scenario tests show complete traffic clearance across the tested settings, though safety margins narrow under compound unpredictable HV behavior.

</details>


### [101] [StreamingWAM: Streaming World Action Modeling via Chunk-Wise Staircase Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15585)
*Shaojie Ren,Jingwen Sun,Zezhi Liu,Hongjin Chen,Xin Jin,Wei Zou,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出StreamingWAM流式扩散框架，联合解决世界动作模型部署中的推理效率与动作连续性两大问题，在仿真和真机上实现平滑且低延迟的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于预训练视频生成世界模型的World Action Models（WAMs）是有前景的通用机器人控制框架，但实际部署时执行不连续、抖动明显。现有方法要么提升推理效率，要么增强跨预测块的动作连续性，未能同时解决这两个问题。

Method: StreamingWAM是一个面向WAM机器人控制的流式扩散框架，包含三个核心组件：噪声阶梯（noise ladder）用于分块动作预测；前缀展开模块（prefix rollout module）用于跨块连续性；密集视频历史记忆（dense video-history memory）用于时间一致的conditioning。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0及真实机器人任务上验证，StreamingWAM在静态与动态场景下均改善了动作连续性，性能具有竞争力，稳态重规划延迟降至仿真72.9 ms、真机57.3 ms；动作过渡更平滑，流式阶梯结构降低了对执行视界K的敏感度。

Conclusion: StreamingWAM通过流式扩散机制统一缓解了WAM部署中的延迟与连续性矛盾，实现了低延迟、平滑且鲁棒的机器人控制。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs), often built upon pretrained video-generation world models, have emerged as a promising framework for general-purpose and open-ended robot control. However, their real-world deployment often suffers from discontinuous and jerky execution. Existing methods typically address this issue by either improving inference efficiency or enhancing action continuity across prediction chunks, without jointly addressing both challenges. To bridge this gap, we propose StreamingWAM, a streaming-diffusion framework for WAM-based robot control. StreamingWAM combines a noise ladder for chunk-wise action prediction, a prefix rollout module for cross-chunk continuity, and dense video-history memory for temporally consistent conditioning. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate that StreamingWAM improves action continuity across static and dynamic settings, achieving competitive performance while reducing steady-state replanning latency to 72.9 ms in simulation and 57.3 ms on the real robot. The method also produces smoother action transitions, while the streaming staircase reduces sensitivity to the execution horizon K.

</details>


### [102] [Imitation Learning via Discrete and Rhythmic Primitive Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15639)
*Rafid Saif,Auke Ijspeert,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NDC框架，用原始轨迹监督、每类原语仅需一个示例，即可组合DMP，统一离散与节律运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作由语义子任务构成，运动原语（MP）可建模这些子任务，但其组合通常依赖人工设计或样本效率低的强化学习；现有监督方法受限于标准数据集缺乏MP标签，且难以在离散与节律运动间泛化。

Method: NDC：以DMP的并行组合作为类别式原语选择的可微松弛；采用warp-invariant损失，在空间/时间变化下识别原语；使用分阶段训练课程学习选择策略与DMP参数，统一离散和节律运动。

Result: 在2D手写和3D机器人任务上评估，方法在多数任务与指标上优于基线；消融实验表明各设计选择对性能均有贡献。

Conclusion: NDC实现仅需原始轨迹监督、每原语单例示教的监督式DMP组合，统一离散与节律运动，并在多任务上验证有效。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation is inherently composed of semantically meaningful sub-tasks. While Movement Primitives (MPs) can model these components, their composition typically relies on manual engineering or sample-inefficient reinforcement learning. Existing supervised methods are limited by the lack of MP labels in standard datasets and often fail to generalize across discrete and rhythmic movements. We propose Neural Dynamic Composition (NDC), a supervised framework to compose Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) using only raw trajectories for supervision, given a single example of each primitive. Our approach exploits parallel combinations of DMPs as a differentiable relaxation of categorical primitive selection, and utilizes a warp-invariant loss formulation to identify primitives despite spatial or temporal variations. A staged training curriculum is employed to learn the selection policy and DMP parameters, unifying discrete and rhythmic movements in one framework. Evaluated on 2D handwriting and 3D robotic tasks, our method outperforms baselines on most tasks and metrics. An ablation study demonstrates how each design choice contributes to the system's performance.

</details>


### [103] [Recovering Low-Bit Failures in Vision-Language-Action Policies via Quantization-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15675)
*Seongmin Park,Jaewon Lee,Yujun Heo,Yoonsik Kim,Seheon Choi,Hyoung-rock Kim,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QCRL（量化条件强化学习），一种离线RL方法，通过进度优势估计与量化感知源条件，弥补量化VLA策略在全精度之间的任务完成差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化可降低视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的部署成本，但会损害任务完成能力。作者通过5个VLA模型、4050次真机试验系统揭示：QAT通常优于PTQ，但在长时程任务及视觉、语义分布偏移下仍与全精度（FP）存在显著差距，且其模仿学习目标未显式利用rollout成功率或部分完成信号。

Method: QCRL将基于进度的优势估计与量化感知源条件相耦合：FP rollout提供超越动作级蒸馏的参考经验；进度缩放的终端惩罚区分部分完成；量化感知条件在量化策略内选择有利的参考行为。评估涵盖π0.5与GR00T N1.5上的权重-only INT4 W4A16，以及π0.5上的联合权重-激活NVFP4 W4A4。

Result: 仅使用分布内数据，在π0.5 + INT4 W4A16下，QCRL在三种视觉偏移条件下达到FP平均得分的99.3%，同时节省42.9%模型内存；在NVFP4 W4A4下达到同一FP基线的100.7%，并借助NVFP4/FP8部署实现1.73倍加速（硬件增益相对Jetson Thor上的FP16）。

Conclusion: QCRL通过利用结果信号（rollout成功率与部分完成）有效缩小了量化VLA策略与全精度之间的性能差距，在保内存与提速度的同时接近甚至匹配FP基线。

Abstract: Quantization lowers deployment costs of vision-language-action (VLA) policies but can compromise task completion. Across five VLA models and 4,050 real-world trials, we systematically reveal that quantization-aware training (QAT) generally improves on post-training quantization (PTQ) but leaves substantial gaps to full precision (FP) on long-horizon tasks and under visual and semantic shifts, while its imitation-based objective does not explicitly use rollout success or partial completion. To exploit these outcome signals, we introduce Quantization-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (QCRL), an offline RL method coupling progress-based advantage estimation with quantization-aware source conditioning. FP rollouts supply reference experience beyond action-level distillation, progress-scaled terminal penalty distinguishes partial completion, and quantization-aware conditioning selects advantageous reference behavior within the quantized policy. We evaluate weight-only INT4 W4A16 on $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.5 and joint weight-activation NVFP4 W4A4 on $π_{0.5}$. Using only in-distribution data, QCRL reaches 99.3% of FP's mean score across three visual-shift conditions for $π_{0.5}$ with INT4 W4A16, alongside 42.9% model-memory savings. With NVFP4 W4A4, it reaches 100.7% of the same FP baseline, alongside a $1.73\times$ speedup using NVFP4/FP8 deployment. Hardware gains are relative to FP16 on Jetson Thor.

</details>


### [104] [Geometry-Aware Predictive Control for Walk-to-Run Bipedal Obstacle Crossing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15735)
*Aashish Sahu,R. Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何感知的预测控制框架，用于双足机器人在行走与跑步模式下的越障，通过五阶段轨迹生成器与约束MPC跟踪层协同生成骨盆与摆动腿运动，并在仿真中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人跨越障碍时需在行走与跑步间协调骨盆与摆动腿运动，兼顾横向间隙、身体对齐与步态平滑切换，而现有方法往往缺乏可解释的阶段结构与统一控制框架。

Method: 将越障过程表示为五个可解释阶段（越障前、前导腿离地、跨立、后随腿离地、恢复）；摆动腿抬升高度随障碍高度自适应，横向站距加宽与骨盆重定位随障碍深度调整；采用连续步态参数演化实现行走-跑步平滑过渡，无需离散控制器切换；生成的参考轨迹由约束MPC跟踪，以调节运动平滑性、离地可行性、触地行为与横向容许性。

Result: 在降阶仿真中对五个难度递增的障碍、在行走与跑步条件下评估；机器人成功跨越前四个障碍，最小机体-障碍间隙分别为0.036、0.036、0.059和0.017 m；第五个最难障碍导致碰撞，界定了当前降阶公式的工作边界。

Conclusion: 该方法为智能障碍感知的双足运动提供了一种紧凑、可解释且计算高效的基线，并明确了现有降阶建模的能力边界。

Abstract: This paper presents a geometry-aware predictive control framework for bipedal obstacle crossing across walking and running regimes. The proposed approach integrates a phase-structured trajectory generator with a constrained model predictive control (MPC) tracking layer to generate coordinated pelvis and swing-foot motions during obstacle traversal. The crossing process is represented through five interpretable phases: pre-crossing, leading-leg clearance, straddle, trailing-leg clearance, and recovery. Within this structure, swing-foot elevation is adapted as a function of obstacle height, while lateral stance widening and pelvis repositioning are introduced as functions of obstacle depth to improve transverse clearance and body alignment. A continuous gait-parameter evolution enables smooth walk-to-run transition without discrete controller switching. The generated references are tracked using MPC to regulate motion smoothness, clearance feasibility, touchdown behavior, and lateral admissibility. The framework is evaluated in reduced-order simulation over five progressively difficult obstacles under walking and running conditions. The robot successfully crosses the first four obstacles, achieving minimum body-obstacle clearances of 0.036, 0.036, 0.059, and 0.017 m, respectively. The fifth and most demanding obstacle leads to collision, identifying the current operating boundary of the proposed reduced-order formulation. Overall, the method provides a compact, interpretable, and computationally efficient basis for intelligent obstacle-aware bipedal locomotion.
