</details>


### [105] [Distributed area coverage path planning subject to the inclusion of a headland path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15739)
*Mogens Plessen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种确定性分布式区域覆盖路径规划方法，强制包含地头路径，在非凸含障碍区域上为多机具生成协同全覆盖路径，并讨论无约束机库分配可达全局最优的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业等场景中多机具协同覆盖非凸、含障碍区域时，需同时满足工作幅宽、作业/行驶能力上限、非完整动力学约束及起止机库分配，且地头路径对覆盖完整性至关重要，而传统Boustrophedon模式存在不足。

Method: 给出确定性（非随机）分布式路径规划算法：输入一般为非凸且可能含障碍的区域、多台机具、至少一个机库及各类约束（工作幅宽、作业容量、行驶容量、非完整动力学、可能的起止机库指派），为每台机具计算分布式路径以共同实现全域覆盖，并强制纳入地头路径；同时讨论若干变体。

Result: 算法可为各机具生成满足约束的分布式路径并实现完整区域覆盖；讨论的变体中，当机库分配不受约束时可获得该问题的全局最优解；对比分析表明了地头路径对覆盖的必要性及其相对Boustrophedon模式方法的优势。

Conclusion: 地头路径的纳入对区域覆盖规划具有重要作用，所提确定性分布式方法在满足多重机具约束下能有效完成覆盖，并在特定变体中达到全局最优，优于Boustrophedon式方法。

Abstract: A deterministic method for the problem of distributed area coverage path planning subject to the inclusion of a headland path is presented. Given a, in general, non-convex area to be covered that may, in general, include obstacle areas, multiple robots, at least one robot depot, and constraints on each machinery such as a working width, work capacity limits, travel capacity limits, nonholonomic dynamical constraints and, possibly, a robot start and end depot assignment, distributed paths for each machinery are calculated that collectively enable full area coverage. Variations are discussed, in particular, a scenario where unconstrained depots assignment can attain a global optimal solution to the problem. The importance of inclusion of a headland path for area coverage, as well as the benefits in comparison to an alternative Boustrophedon-pattern based approach are highlighted.

</details>


### [106] [Multimodal Sensor-Gated Mid-Air Launch and Vision-Based Recovery of a Child UAV on a Hovering Mothership Drone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15740)
*Aashish Sahu,R. Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由非视觉传感门控任务转换的母机-子机四旋翼系统，通过单波束LiDAR、RTK定位与载体推力实现空中释放与回收的可靠确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从悬停载体上发射微型无人机并在飞行中回收，需在载体下降前可靠确认子机已稳固就位。视觉虽可提供相对引导，但无法直接反映载荷转移，且下视相机在接近触地时易丢失基准标记。

Method: 在回收甲板安装朝上的单波束LiDAR，利用迟滞与去抖间隔（由子机遮挡足迹与穿越速度推导）给出二值占用状态；RTK定位条件化载体悬停；载体推力作为载荷转移的本体感知指示。开展十次室外任务（F450级母机、Q250级子机、视觉引导回收）及MuJoCo全自主基准回收仿真。

Result: 十次室外任务中九次成功，释放与捕获全程维持RTK固定解；仿真中触地距甲板中心0.077 m，而标记检测在距对接构型约0.2 m内丢失；释放与捕获时载体推力分别变化-17.0 N与+17.3 N，与子机18.0 N悬停推力误差在6%以内。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种传感架构：视觉负责引导接近，测距与本体感知信息用于佐证最终对接决策。

Abstract: Launching a micro unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) from a hovering carrier and recovering it in flight requires reliable confirmation that the child is securely seated before the carrier is allowed to descend. This is challenging because vision provides relative guidance but does not directly indicate load transfer, and a downward-facing camera can lose its fiducial near touchdown. This letter presents a mothership-child quadrotor system in which mission transitions are gated by complementary nonvisual sensing. An upward-facing single-beam LiDAR on the recovery deck provides a binary occupancy state using hysteresis and a debounce interval derived from the child's occluding footprint and crossing speed; real-time kinematic (RTK) positioning conditions the carrier hover; and carrier thrust provides a proprioceptive indication of load transfer. In ten outdoor missions using an F450-class mothership and a Q250-class child with camera-guided recovery, nine completed successfully while RTK-fixed positioning was maintained through release and capture. A complementary MuJoCo study of fully autonomous fiducial-based recovery achieved touchdown 0.077 m from the deck center, while marker detection was lost within the final approximately 0.2 m above the docked configuration. In simulation, carrier thrust changed by -17.0 N at release and +17.3 N at capture, agreeing within 6% with the child's 18.0 N hover thrust. The results support a sensing architecture in which vision guides the approach, while range and proprioceptive information corroborate the final docking decision.

</details>


### [107] [Model-Based Trailer-Sway Suppression for Autonomous Passenger Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15747)
*Alec McFadyen,Pablo Covarrubias De La Torre,Connor McGovern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对轻型乘用车牵引拖车的"拖车摆动"失稳问题，建立了以舌载为显式稳定性参数的横摆平面线性状态空间模型，设计LQR控制器抑制铰接角发散，并用仅安装于牵引车的传感器，通过卡尔曼滤波估计拖车横摆角速度和铰接角；在MuJoCo多体仿真中验证了该方案在不同车速和舌载（含负舌载）工况下能显著降低拖车摆振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拖车摆动是轻型乘用车牵引拖车严重事故的持续诱因，但在当前自动驾驶系统进入的新一轮自动化浪潮中仍基本未被解决。货物在拖车车轴后方的错误装载会降低甚至反转通过挂钩传递的舌载，导致牵引车后轴垂向载荷损失、侧向轮胎力下降，使耦合系统横摆稳定性恶化，从而产生拖车侧向发散振荡（拖车摆动），在车速与载荷不利组合下进一步发展为铰接角迅速发散、丧失方向控制力的折叠（jackknifing）事故。因此需要一种可用于自动驾驶车辆的防摆控制方案。

Method: 1) 建立轻型车-拖车组合体横摆平面动力学的线性状态空间模型，参数化使舌载成为显式的稳定性决定量；2) 基于该模型综合线性二次型调节器（LQR），以抑制铰接角发散为目标；3) 鉴于消费级拖车不安装传感器，控制器所需的两个拖车状态（拖车横摆角速度与铰接角）由卡尔曼滤波仅利用牵引车上的测量量进行估计；4) 在MuJoCo多体仿真中实现并评估控制器，覆盖多种纵向车速与舌载工况，包括负舌载工况。

Result: 在给定车辆上使用LQR控制转向、并基于牵引车IMU与LiDAR数据时，拖车振荡显著减小；仿真覆盖了不同纵向速度及包括负舌载在内的舌载条件，结果一致表明该方法能有效抑制拖车摆动。

Conclusion: 结果表明，LQR控制的防摆系统是未来自动驾驶车辆一种可行的控制方法，为在自动驾驶时代解决拖车摆动这一长期被忽视的安全问题提供了技术路径。

Abstract: Trailer sway is a persistent contributor to severe crashes among light passenger vehicles towing trailers, yet it remains largely unaddressed by the autonomous vehicle systems now entering this new era of automation. Improper cargo placement rearward of the trailer axle reduces, and can invert, the tongue load transmitted through the hitch. The resulting loss of vertical load at the tow vehicle's rear axle diminishes the lateral tire force available there and degrades yaw stability of the coupled system, admitting a divergent lateral oscillation of the trailer known as trailer sway. Under adverse combinations of speed and loading, this oscillation can progress to jackknifing, in which the articulation angle diverges rapidly and directional control is lost. This work develops a linear state-space model of the yaw-plane dynamics of a light vehicle-trailer combination, parameterized to expose tongue load as an explicit stability-determining quantity. From this model a linear quadratic regulator is synthesized to suppress articulation-angle divergence. Because consumer trailers carry no sensors, the two trailer states the controller needs, trailer yaw rate and articulation angle, are estimated by a Kalman filter from measurements taken only on the tow vehicle. The controller is then implemented and evaluated in a MuJoCo multibody simulation across a range of longitudinal speeds and tongue-load conditions, including the negative-tongue-load regime. Results show a significant decrease in trailer oscillation when controlling a given vehicle's steering with an LQR using tow-vehicle IMU and LiDAR data. These findings indicate that LQR controlled anti-sway systems are a viable control method for future autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [108] [Kinematic Reward, Measured Effort: Hardware-Validated RL Morphology Optimization of a Planar 2R Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15756)
*Gunesh Gupta,Arvind Kumar Mishra,Sohom Chakrabarty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于RSL-RL PPO的强化学习框架，以Yoshikawa可操作度指数为核心奖励优化2R SCARA机械臂的形态，并在硬件上验证所优化形态能显著降低关节负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yoshikawa可操作度指数虽常被用作机械臂最优形态设计的经典度量，但此类优化几乎都在仿真中作为纯运动学量研究，很少在硬件上验证，缺乏实际效果确认。

Method: 采用RSL-RL PPO的强化学习框架，以Yoshikawa运动学可操作度指数作为核心奖励，优化2R SCARA机械臂的形态；先用圆形任务解析闭式最优解验证方法能收敛到该最优，再应用于无解析最优解的椭圆与矩形路径；最后将RL优化形态迁移至2R硬件机械臂进行验证。

Result: 与最差形态相比，方法给出的最优形态使第二关节负载在椭圆路径降低38.4±0.6%、矩形路径降低41.3±0.3%，第一关节负载降低15%；且在圆形任务中成功复现解析闭式最优解。

Conclusion: 该RL框架可在有解析解时复现运动学最优，并在无解析解的复杂路径上给出有效形态，且硬件验证表明优化形态能实质性地降低关节负载，弥补了以往仅在仿真中研究的空白。

Abstract: Yoshikawa's Manipulability Index is generally used as a classical measure to define an optimal morphology of a manipulator. However, any such optimization is almost always studied as a purely kinematic quantity in simulation and is rarely validated on hardware. We present a reinforcement-learning framework with RSL-RL PPO that optimizes the morphology of a 2R SCARA-type manipulator arm with Yoshikawa's kinematic manipulability index as a core reward, and the analytically derived closed-form optimum for the circular task is used to verify that our method recovers it. Then the same method is applied to custom elliptical and rectangular paths having no analytical optimum. The RL-optimized morphologies are then transferred to a 2R hardware manipulator to validate its effect in reduction of joint load in the hardware. Compared to the worst morphology, the optimum morphology delivered by our method reduces second joint load by 38.4 +/- 0.6% for the ellipse path and 41.3 +/- 0.3% for the rectangle, and first joint load by 15%.

</details>


### [109] [Competitive Task Allocation with Temporal Proposal Windows and Improved Artificial Potential Fields for Dual-Arm Industrial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15763)
*Surya Prakash S. K,Bhuvan Narula,Jagannath Prasad Sahoo,Jayakumar VK,Praful Hambarde,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向双臂系统的基于距离的竞争式任务分配框架，融合实时视觉感知与无碰撞运动规划，通过时序窗口机制与改进人工势场（iAPF）实现动态重分配和双臂协调，仿真与Kinova Gen3 Lite硬件实验验证其在效率、安全性与覆盖率上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业自动化需要能在异构物体布局中协调操作的自主协作系统。现有系统依赖预设轨迹和刚性分配策略，导致工作空间利用率次优并存在碰撞风险，因此需要一种能够自适应任务分配与协调避障的双臂框架。

Method: 提出基于距离的竞争式任务分配框架：采用时间窗口机制，让每条机械臂在连续五帧视觉检测基础上评估目标后再进行基于距离的最优选择，支持动态重分配与空间冲突消解（阈值精度0.01m）；运动规划采用改进人工势场（iAPF），包含指数型目标引力、臂间斥力、归位力与速度阻尼力，实现双臂协调运动。系统集成YOLOv8 OBB检测（30 FPS）与10 Hz控制回路。

Result: 仿真结果在距离效率、碰撞风险和工作空间覆盖率方面优于市场化、轮询和固定分配策略。使用两台6-DOF Kinova Gen3 Lite的硬件实验中，同构场景下相较固定分配平均速度提升约47%且零碰撞；混合物体场景验证了通用处理能力，时间开销增加约11%，但换取了更优的安全裕度。

Conclusion: 该框架结合视觉感知与iAPF运动规划，可有效实现双臂动态协调分配与无碰撞操作，具备面向工业装配与分拣任务的实用部署能力。

Abstract: Modern industrial automation demands autonomous collaborative systems capable of coordinated manipulation in workspaces with heterogeneous object arrangements. Existing systems rely on predetermined trajectories and rigid allocation strategies, causing suboptimal workspace utilization and collision risks. This paper presents a distance-based competitive task allocation framework for dual-arm systems, integrating real-time vision perception with collision-free motion planning. A temporal window mechanism enables each arm to evaluate object detections over five consecutive vision frames before committing to optimal distance-based selection, featuring dynamic reallocation and spatial conflict resolution at 0.01m threshold accuracy. An improved Artificial Potential Field (iAPF) framework provides coordinated dual-arm movements through exponential goal attraction, inter-arm repulsion, home-seeking, and velocity damping forces. Simulation results validate superiority over market-based, round-robin, and fixed allocation strategies in distance efficiency, collision risk, and workspace coverage. Hardware experiments using two 6-DOF Kinova Gen3 Lite manipulators demonstrate approximately 47% average velocity improvement over fixed allocation in homogeneous scenarios with zero collisions, while mixed object scenarios confirm universal handling with approximately 11% time overhead offset by superior safety margins. The framework integrates YOLOv8 OBB detection at 30 FPS with 10 Hz control loops, demonstrating practical deployment capability for industrial assembly and sorting operations.

</details>


### [110] [SurgiCLAW: An Interpretable Atomic-Skill Agent for Precision Robotic Micromanipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15766)
*Angen Ye,junjie xie,Yujia Song,Junkai Huang,Dapeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SurgiCLAW：将MLLM限定于术中视觉理解、结构化解释与原子手术技能选择，物理执行交由可验证的确定性控制程序，在脑机接口植入任务上实现94.6%技能选择准确率与88.0%实机任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人领域端到端低层动作预测受限于精度不足、决策不可解释与数据稀缺，需要兼顾长程任务理解与精密执行的可信框架。

Method: 分层技能框架：MLLM负责术中视觉理解、结构化解释与原子手术技能选择，底层由确定性控制程序执行；针对脑机接口植入任务构建"状态—技能调用"数据集，并通过视觉与状态扰动扩充；先监督微调学习结构化技能调用，再用动作正确性、操作前置条件与格式有效性三项奖励进行优化。

Result: 固定测试集上技能选择准确率94.6%、宏平均F1为93.0%；50次闭环实机植入试验任务成功率达88.0%；并完成子任务成功率、离线模型对比与消融实验评估。

Conclusion: 将MLLM限制在高层语义决策、把执行交给可验证控制程序的分层范式，可在精密手术场景中提升动作选择正确性、操作安全性与输出可解析性。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and vision--language--action models have provided robots with new capabilities for long-horizon task understanding and planning. In surgical robotics, however, end-to-end prediction of low-level actions remains limited by insufficient precision, opaque decisions, and scarce data. This paper presents SurgiCLAW, a hierarchical skill-based framework for precision surgical manipulation. SurgiCLAW confines an MLLM to intraoperative visual understanding, structured explanation, and atomic surgical-skill selection, while delegating physical execution to verifiable deterministic control programs. For brain--machine-interface implantation, a representative high-precision surgical task, we construct a state-to-skill invocation dataset and expand it through visual and state perturbations. The model first learns structured skill invocation through supervised fine-tuning and is then optimized using three rewards for action correctness, operation prerequisites, and format validity, improving action selection, operational safety, and output parseability. We evaluate subtask success, offline model comparison, real-robot performance, and ablations. On a fixed test set, SurgiCLAW achieves 94.6% skill-selection accuracy and 93.0% macro-averaged F1 (Macro-F1). Across 50 closed-loop real-robot implantation trials, it achieves an 88.0% task success rate.

</details>


### [111] [Hand2Robot: Object-Centric 4D Hand Reconstruction from Human Demonstrations for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15769)
*Zetao Du,Weiqing Wang,Hanbing Zhang,Yimiao Zhou,Ye Shi,Liang Wang,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hand2Robot：以物体为中心的4D手部重建框架，通过手-物交互优化与运动重定向，从人类视频生成机器人动作监督，用于策略后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示可捕获与任务相关的操作技能且无需目标机器人，但手部运动无法直接作为机器人动作监督。现有手部重建与运动重定向路线，仅恢复手部运动无法保证物理一致的手-物交互。

Method: 提出以物体为中心的4D手部重建框架Hand2Robot：从人类执行目标任务的视频中提取交互片段、重建物体几何，并通过手-物交互优化与运动重定向生成机器人动作监督。物体跟踪方面，结合观测置信度与时序运动约束，从逐帧位姿候选中选取全局一致的物体位姿轨迹；手部细化方面，以物体几何与运动为参考，结合分阶段2D对齐与物体局部接触约束，细化手部轨迹与手指关节配置。

Result: 在DexYCB与HO3D-v3上，物体位姿精度和手-物交互质量均有提升；在三个真实机器人灵巧操作任务上，重建并重定向的人类演示能有效补充有限的机器人数据，用于目标任务的策略后训练。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心的4D手部重建与交互优化，可生成物理一致的机器人动作监督，缓解真实机器人数据稀缺问题，支撑灵巧操作策略的后训练。

Abstract: Human demonstrations can capture task-relevant manipulation without using the target robot, but the recorded hand motions cannot directly serve as robot action supervision. Hand reconstruction and motion retargeting provide a direct route to such supervision, yet recovering hand motion alone does not ensure physically consistent hand-object interactions. To address this issue, we introduce Hand2Robot, an object-centric 4D hand reconstruction framework for robot policy post-training. Given videos of humans performing the target task, Hand2Robot extracts interaction clips, reconstructs object geometry, and generates robot action supervision through hand-object interaction optimization and motion retargeting. For object tracking, we combine observation confidence with temporal motion constraints to select a globally consistent object pose trajectory from per-frame pose candidates. For hand refinement, we use object geometry and motion as a reference and combine staged 2D alignment with object-local contact constraints to refine hand trajectories and finger joint configurations. Experiments on DexYCB and HO3D-v3 show improved object pose accuracy and hand-object interaction quality. Experiments on three real-robot dexterous manipulation tasks further show that reconstructed and retargeted human demonstrations can effectively supplement limited robot data for target-task policy post-training.

</details>


### [112] [$Δ$ Flow Matching for Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15794)
*Kuangji Zuo,Tuo An,Xinying Guo,Gen Li,Mengfei Zhao,Jiaqi Bai,Bofan Lyu,Zhuoyuan Yu,Jinghan Yang,Jiayi Li,Jindou Jia,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Δ-flow 用近期历史加轻度噪声替代固定高斯先验作为生成源，使源与目标更接近，只需学习小残差Δ，从而实现高效训练与单步推理，在 VLA 与 WAM 策略上显著提升效率、泛化性与动作平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型的流匹配生成方法（VLA 生成动作块，WAM 额外生成未来视觉状态）都从固定高斯先验开始生成，忽略了过去与未来状态、动作之间的时间连续性。这导致训练成本高、采样需要多步迭代，对带额外视觉分支的 WAM 尤为昂贵。作者希望利用时间连续性来缩短传输路径、降低训练与推理开销。

Method: 提出 Δ-flow：不再从固定高斯先验采样，而是用近期历史（含轻度噪声）构造生成源。由于源更接近目标，整体传输被缩减为一个可高效学习的小残差 Δ，并支持单步推理。方法被集成进 VLA 策略 π_{0.5} 以及 WAM 策略 ImageWAM、Fast-WAM，并研究了动作表示与视觉信息对历史到未来预测的影响。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，ImageWAM 一个 epoch 达 90% 成功率（原需六个），Fast-WAM 两个 epoch 达 95%（原需八个）；LoRA 微调下 π_{0.5} 一个 epoch 即超过基线五个 epoch 的表现。单步推理在 π_{0.5}、ImageWAM、Fast-WAM 上分别带来 1.54×、2.49×、4.45× 加速，并保持动作平滑，相比一步高斯采样动作 jerk 降低 4.5×。在 LIBERO-Plus 上达到 SOTA 泛化性能。

Conclusion: Δ-flow 简单而有效。动作历史源可直接用于预训练 WAM 策略而无需重训练；推理时替换高斯动作源并单步采样基本保持甚至略提升任务成功率。整体提升了机器人基础模型的效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Flow matching is a common generative recipe for robot foundation models. Vision-language-action models (VLAs) generate action chunks, while world-action models (WAMs) additionally generate future visual states. Both start generation from a fixed Gaussian prior, despite temporal continuity between past and future states and actions. This requires costly training and iterative sampling, especially for WAMs with an additional visual branch. We introduce $Δ$-flow, which constructs the source from recent history with mild noise rather than from a fixed Gaussian prior. This moves the source closer to the target, reducing transport to a small residual $Δ$ that can be learned efficiently and enables single-step inference. We integrate $Δ$-flow into the VLA policy $π_{0.5}$ and the WAM policies ImageWAM and Fast-WAM. On LIBERO, ImageWAM reaches 90% success in one epoch rather than six, and Fast-WAM reaches 95% in two epochs rather than eight. With LoRA fine-tuning, $π_{0.5}$ exceeds its baseline's five-epoch performance after one epoch. Single-step inference gives $1.54\times$, $2.49\times$, and $4.45\times$ speedups on $π_{0.5}$, ImageWAM, and Fast-WAM, respectively. It preserves action smoothness, achieving a $4.5\times$ reduction in action jerk compared with one-step Gaussian sampling. $Δ$-flow reaches state-of-the-art generalization performance on LIBERO-Plus. Action-history sources can be applied to pretrained WAM policies without retraining. Replacing the Gaussian action source at inference largely preserves task success with a single sampling step and can slightly improve it. We further study how action representation and visual information in action learning affect history-to-future prediction in $Δ$-flow. These results show that $Δ$-flow is simple yet effective, improving the efficiency and generalization of robot foundation models.

</details>


### [113] [ReTrace: Cross-Stage Failure Attribution for Self-Improving Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15814)
*Yue Yu,Bin Zhu,Junhui Li,Jiayu Wang,Shijie Zhou,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReTrace框架，通过自主物理交互与回溯推理，让多模态基础模型在无演示、不更新参数的条件下自我改进机器人操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态基础模型已能直接控制机器人，但通过自主交互提升操作能力仍具挑战。核心难点在于跨阶段依赖任务中的失败学习：某动作可能局部成功，却导致后续阶段失败，难以归因与纠正。

Method: ReTrace是一个自我改进框架，依赖自主物理交互与回溯推理。它将下游失败回溯归因到早期决策，提出针对性修正，并通过后续交互进行验证；多次尝试后，将验证有效的修正逐步固化为可复用的操作技能，用于指导后续执行。无需提供演示，也不更新模型参数。

Result: 在Franka Research 3机器人上对四项真实操作任务进行评估。在两个强跨阶段依赖任务上，GPT-6 Astra-low的插头插入成功率由0%提升至80%，倒球任务由0%提升至100%；GPT-6 Sol-xhigh分别由0%提升至10%和由0%提升至90%。所学技能可跨模型与推理配置迁移，并在多数评估设置下降低推理时决策成本。

Conclusion: ReTrace验证了通过回溯推理与自主交互实现机器人操作自我改进的可行性，在强跨阶段依赖任务上带来显著性能提升，且技能具备迁移性并降低决策开销。

Abstract: General-purpose multimodal foundation models are increasingly capable of directly controlling robots, yet improving their manipulation capabilities through autonomous interaction remains challenging. A key challenge to such self-improvement is learning from failures in complex manipulation tasks involving cross-stage dependencies, where an action may succeed locally but cause a later stage to fail. To address this challenge, we introduce ReTrace, a self-improvement framework for robotic manipulation through autonomous physical interaction and retrospective reasoning, without provided demonstrations or model-parameter updates. ReTrace retrospectively traces downstream failures to earlier decisions, proposes targeted corrections, and evaluates them through subsequent interactions. Across attempts, ReTrace progressively consolidates validated corrections into a reusable manipulation skill that guide subsequent execution. We evaluate ReTrace on four real-world manipulation tasks using a Franka Research 3 robot. On the two tasks with strong cross-stage dependencies, GPT-6 Astra-low improves from 0% to 80% success on plug insertion and from 0% to 100% on ball pouring, while GPT-6 Sol-xhigh improves from 0% to 10% and 0% to 90%, respectively. The learned skills also transfer across model and reasoning configurations and reduce inference-time decision cost in most evaluated settings.

</details>


### [114] [P-BFM: Perceptive Behavioral Foundation Models for Humanoid Control via Unsupervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15833)
*Xue Wang,Yitang Li,Yiming Zhang,Siqin Wang,Junhan Sun,Zhihao Liu,Yaojie Shen,Jingbo Yuan,Hao Zhao,Qingdu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: P-BFM 是一种感知式行为基础模型，将人形机器人控制分解为地形无关的意图与地形条件的实现，并引入显式根指令分离全局移动，实现无需地形专用示范的地形自适应全身行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界人形控制中，同一行为意图在不同地形约束下需要根本不同的运动，用地形匹配的示范来覆盖这种变化需要枚举行为与地形几何的组合空间，代价极高。

Method: 提出 P-BFM：前向-后向表示将运动参考、目标状态与奖励函数映射到不含地形信息的共享意图空间；感知策略学习在周围地形几何下实现各意图。同时引入显式根指令（ERI），将全局移动（朝向与平面速度）与行为意图分离，局部全身适应交由感知策略完成。

Result: 在多种挑战性地形上，P-BFM 在运动保真度、根控制精度与扰动恢复上优于强基线；单一冻结策略支持运动跟踪、目标到达、奖励推断、遥操作与地形感知恢复，并可零样本迁移到使用机载深度感知的 Unitree G1。

Conclusion: 将人形控制分解为地形无关意图与地形条件实现，并显式分离全局移动，可实现一个固定意图在不同地形下产生恰当的不同行为，且无需地形专用示范，展现了传统 BFM 不具备的能力。

Abstract: Behavioral foundation models (BFMs) provide humanoids with reusable repertoires of whole-body behaviors; in real-world humanoid control, the same behavioral intent may require fundamentally different motions under different terrain constraints. Covering such variation with terrain-matched demonstrations requires enumerating a combinatorial space of behaviors and terrain geometries. We introduce P-BFM, a perceptive behavioral foundation model that factorizes humanoid control into terrain-invariant intent and terrain-conditioned realization. A forward-backward representation maps motion references, target states, and reward functions into a shared intent space without terrain information, while a perceptive policy learns how to realize each intent under the surrounding terrain geometry. We further identify a complementary limitation of purely latent control: behavioral latents can preserve local motion patterns while losing precise global progress. We therefore introduce Explicit Root Instructions (ERI), which separates global transport (e.g., heading and planar velocity) from behavioral intent, while leaving local whole-body adaptation to the perceptive policy. This decomposition leads to a characteristic capability absent from conventional BFMs: one fixed intent induces different, terrain-appropriate behaviors, including changes in step height, stride, posture, foothold placement, and contacts, without terrain-specific demonstrations. Across challenging terrains, P-BFM improves motion fidelity, root-control precision, and perturbation recovery over strong baselines. A single frozen policy further supports motion tracking, goal reaching, reward inference, teleoperation, and terrain-aware recovery, and transfers zero-shot to a Unitree G1 using onboard depth sensing.

</details>


### [115] [REFLEX: A Learned On-Board Reflex for Robust Autonomous Collision Recovery of Soft Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15867)
*Jakob Hermeler,Luca Girardi,Gabriel Maquignaz,Stefan Leutenegger,Stefano Mintchev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出REFLEX——一种完全运行于飞控板上的碰撞恢复飞行模式，无需接触传感器或伴飞计算机，采用本体感受循环策略实现高速撞击后的自主恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗撞机身能让四旋翼承受撞击，但无法保证撞击后的自主恢复；现有板载估计器的"创新门控"机制会在高速撞击后拒绝有效的外部速度测量，从而破坏恢复过程。

Method: REFLEX不依赖位置、速度、航向或高度估计，用本体感受循环策略稳定机体；激活期间重新初始化平移估计，并用置信门（trust gate）检查估计器一致性后再交回原装自驾仪；策略在仿真中用PPO训练，训练回合的初始状态取自47次真实碰撞的撞后状态。

Result: 硬件实验中，REFLEX在5.03±0.09 m/s下恢复22次撞墙中的19次，而匹配的原装自驾仪仅恢复14次中的1次，其中包括单次飞行中连续15次恢复；另一次探索性飞行在同一架次内完成6.02和6.05 m/s的两次恢复，将已报道的自主撞墙恢复速度提高64%。

Conclusion: REFLEX证明仅凭板载本体感受感知即可实现高速碰撞后的自主恢复，性能显著优于原装自驾仪，并刷新了自主撞墙恢复的速度纪录。

Abstract: Collision-resilient airframes allow quadrotors to survive impacts, but do not ensure autonomous post-collision recovery. Innovation gating in stock onboard estimators often rejects valid external velocity measurements after high-speed impacts, compromising recovery. We present REFLEX, a collision-recovery flight mode that runs entirely on the flight controller, requiring neither contact sensors nor a companion computer. Its proprioceptive recurrent policy stabilizes the vehicle without position, velocity, heading, or altitude estimates. The translational estimate is reinitialized while REFLEX is active, and a trust gate checks estimator consistency before returning control to the stock autopilot. The policy is trained in simulation using proximal policy optimization, with episodes initialized from post-impact states measured in 47 real collisions. In hardware experiments, REFLEX recovered 19 of 22 wall impacts at 5.03 $\pm$ 0.09 m/s, compared with 1 of 14 for the matched stock autopilot. These included 15 consecutive recoveries in a single flight. A separate exploratory flight demonstrated two additional recoveries at 6.02 and 6.05 m/s within the same flight, exceeding the previously reported velocity for autonomous wall-collision recovery by 64%.

</details>


### [116] [Efficient Hybrid Hard--Soft Signal Temporal Logic MPC for Driving under Multimodal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15934)
*Kumar Manas,Adrian Paschke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合硬–软STL MPC框架，用单一共享自车轨迹应对多模态交互不确定性，并通过可达性筛选与向量化符号构造将规划时延降至约四分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式交通中的运动规划需同时满足安全、遵守交规并在多模态行为不确定性下保持舒适通行。若将这些要求全部作为硬约束会降低可行性，而全部作为代价函数又会使安全关键要求与通行效率相互妥协。因此需要一种按重要性分层处理的规划方法，同时还需解决STL符号图构造开销过大的问题。

Method: 提出混合硬–软信号时序逻辑（STL）MPC框架：针对每个保留的行为假设优化同一条共享自车轨迹。将最小距离与速度谓词设为显式硬约束，把STL鲁棒性软项用于塑造可行驾驶走廊内的间隙、舒适性与交互质量，从而在无需分支特定应急策略的前提下保持对离散交互模式的鲁棒性。此外，引入可达性认证的相关性筛选与向量化符号构造，在不改变可行集与目标函数的情况下降低计算开销。

Result: 在六个高速与交叉口场景上的闭环蒙特卡洛评估中，该方法在所比较的规划器中取得最高严格成功率，且未观察到碰撞或违规行为，规划时延约为未优化形式的四分之一。消融实验分离出两层作用：硬约束定义安全包络，软项在该包络内改善间隙与运动质量。

Conclusion: 硬–软STL MPC的分层设计可在多模态交互不确定性下同时兼顾安全性、规则合规性与舒适通行，共享轨迹结构避免分支爆炸；配合可达性筛选与向量化构造，可在保持解集与目标不变的前提下显著降低计算成本，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Motion planning for automated vehicles in interactive traffic has to simultaneously ensure safety, respect traffic rules, and maintain comfortable progress under multi-modal behavior uncertainty. Imposing all these requirements as hard constraints reduces feasibility, whereas treating them all as costs allows safety-critical requirements to be traded against progress. We propose a hybrid hard--soft Signal Temporal Logic (STL) MPC framework that optimizes a single shared ego trajectory against every retained behavior hypothesis: minimum-distance and speed predicates are imposed as explicit hard constraints, while soft STL robustness terms shape clearance, comfort, and interaction quality within the resulting feasible driving corridor. This retains robustness to discrete interaction modes without branch-specific contingent ego policies. We further identify substantial symbolic graph-construction overhead and introduce reachability-certified relevance screening and vectorized symbolic construction to reduce computational cost while preserving the feasible set and objective. Closed-loop Monte Carlo evaluation over six highway and intersection scenarios shows the highest strict success rate among the compared planners with no observed collisions or rule violations, at roughly a quarter of the planning latency of the unoptimized formulation. An ablation study separates the two layers: the hard constraints define the safety envelope, while the soft terms improve clearance and motion quality within it.

</details>


### [117] [Jacobian State Augmented Viable Kinematic Safety Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15936)
*Siddharth Singh,Joshua Vaughan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于雅可比的运动学安全滤波器，通过引入增广状态变量并嵌套二次规划，在线计算可行规划器，以在保证关节位置与速度约束的同时维持底层滤波器可行性，并在2R及6-DoF机械臂仿真中验证其计算效率与可行性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全滤波器（SF）可设计可证明安全的控制器，保证在滤波器可行时安全集的前向不变性；预测安全滤波器（PSF）虽能改善约束满足并减少短视单步滤波导致的可行性损失，但相关基于生存性（viability）的方法需近似生存核，计算代价高昂。因此需要一种兼顾可行性与计算效率的安全滤波方法。

Method: 提出基于雅可比的运动学安全滤波器：1）引入含任务空间速度的增广状态变量，对任务空间加速度进行滤波，以在施加关节位置与速度约束的同时保持底层滤波器可行性；2）设计嵌套二次规划（QP）公式；3）定义生存性包络的保守内近似，并在线计算可行规划器。

Result: 在简化2R平面机械臂仿真环境中验证，相较基线CBF-QP滤波器性能显著提升；实验表明相比传统PSF与参考调节器（Reference Governor）方法计算时间更优，且可行性违反显著减少；在仿真6-DoF机械臂上的简化演示表明该方法对更高自由度场景具有潜在可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提基于雅可比的运动学安全滤波器通过嵌套QP与生存性包络保守内近似，在保持可行性的同时降低计算开销，在2R机械臂上优于CBF-QP基线并与PSF、参考调节器相比减少可行性违反，且具备向高自由度系统扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Safety Filters (SF) are leveraged to design certifiably safe controllers, which ensures forward invariance on a defined safe set when the filter is feasible. Predictive Safety Filters (PSF) embed these constraints to a closed-loop model-based optimization method to improve constraint satisfaction, and reduce feasibility loss caused by myopic, one-step filtering. Related viability-based methods emphasize approximating a viability kernel which ensures admissible control for all future time, but requires significant computational expense. This work introduces a Jacobian-based kinematic safety filter leveraging an augmented state variable with task-space velocity and filters task-space acceleration to preserve the feasibility of a low-level filter while enforcing joint position and velocity. The proposed method introduces a nested Quadratic Program (QP) formulation, defines a conservative inner approximation of viability envelope and computes a viable planner online. The method is validated in a simplified 2R planar manipulator simulation environment and shows significant improvement compared to a baseline Control Barrier Function Quadratic Program (CBF-QP) based filter. The experiments highlights the improvements in terms of computation time compared to traditional PSF and Reference Governor approach while also showing significant reduction in feasibility violations. Lastly, a simplified demonstration on a simulated 6-DoF manipulator highlights the potential scalability of the method to higher degree-of-freedom setups.

</details>


### [118] [A Hardware-Partitioned micro-ROS Mini-Vehicle for Operator-Supervised Neural Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15966)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本微型Ackermann智能车平台，采用硬件分区的"感知-决策-执行"架构，用于嵌入式神经驾驶研究。实验表明无线传感而非神经推理是延迟瓶颈，平台在12次自主行驶中实现零碰撞，且编码器-IMU融合将里程计闭环误差降低22%，软件开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为嵌入式神经驾驶研究提供一个低成本、可复现的实验平台，通过合理的硬件分区在MCU级车辆上隔离实时安全控制与学习型计算，兼顾安全性与研究灵活性。

Method: 构建微型Ackermann车辆，采用硬件分区架构：ESP32摄像头节点通过Wi-Fi向主机传输帧，主机运行神经推理与状态估计；ESP32-S3底盘控制器在micro-ROS客户端下以100 Hz每轮PID环路执行安全关键驱动。学习组件在车外运行，操作员通过手柄监督。同时对完整闭环管线进行特性分析，并采用编码器-IMU融合进行里程计估计。

Result: 闭环神经驾驶中，无线传感而非神经推理主导延迟，采集到执行中位延迟为59.5 ms；固件看门狗在无线波动时保持上一安全指令，平台在12次自主会话中实现零碰撞、零人工干预；编码器-IMU融合将里程计闭环误差降低22%。

Conclusion: 经过精心分区的MCU级车辆能够支持嵌入式AI、无线控制与操作员监督式自主驾驶的可复现实验，且平台软件开源。

Abstract: We present a low-cost miniature Ackermann vehicle for embedded neural-driving research, organized as a hardware-partitioned perceive-decide-act agent: an ESP32 camera node streams frames over Wi-Fi to a host computer, where neural inference and state estimation run, while an ESP32-S3 chassis controller executes safety-critical actuation in a 100 Hz per-wheel proportional-integral-derivative (PID) loop beneath a micro-ROS client. The partition isolates real-time control and safety on the vehicle while learning-based components run off-board, supervised by an operator from a gamepad. We characterize the complete pipeline during real closed-loop neural driving. Wireless sensing, rather than neural inference, dominates latency, with a 59.5 ms median capture-to-actuation delay; the platform absorbed wireless variability by holding the last safe command under a firmware watchdog, operating across twelve autonomous sessions with zero collisions or manual interventions; and encoder-inertial-measurement-unit (IMU) fusion cut odometry loop-closure error by 22%. These results indicate that carefully partitioned microcontroller-class vehicles support reproducible experiments in embedded AI, wireless control, and operator-supervised autonomous driving. The platform software is open source.

</details>


### [119] [PathLift: Depth-Conditioned Metric Lifting for Visual-Navigation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16103)
*Gwanjun Shin,Hyeongseok Suh,Hongmo Jung,Jongwoo Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PathLift是一个逐路径点、深度条件化的适配器，可将预训练视觉导航策略输出的无量纲轨迹“抬升”为深度可行的度量路径，单次前馈即可提升仿真与真机闭环导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉导航策略只给出方向、不给出距离，部署时需额外抬升步骤把无量纲轨迹转成控制器可跟踪的度量路径。现有标量抬升采用统一缩放，忽略逐路径点校正；而把单目深度注入策略内部的方法，会继承深度模型在障碍物附近和近距离的预测误差。

Method: PathLift作为逐路径点的深度条件化适配器，消费显式、传感器级深度源（RGB-D、双目或投影LiDAR），在规范化先验上拟合每个路径点的幅值与横向残差，从而在单次前馈中把策略的无量纲意图转换为深度可行的度量路径，无需测试时优化。

Result: 在仿真中跨多种视觉导航策略，以及在真实轮式机器人上使用双目与LiDAR深度，PathLift均持续提升闭环导航表现；无需测试时优化即可提升真实世界成功率。

Conclusion: 采用逐路径点、传感器级深度条件化的适配器，可在不重训策略、不做测试时优化的前提下，将通用视觉导航策略的无量纲意图可靠转化为度量路径，是一种通用且有效的抬升方案。

Abstract: Generalist visual-navigation policies tell a robot which direction to go but not how far. A separate lifting step is needed at deployment to convert their unitless trajectory into a metric path that a controller can track; existing scalar lifters apply uniform scaling that misses per-waypoint corrections, while in-policy approaches that inject monocular depth into the policy inherit the prediction errors of the depth model near obstacles and at close range.
  We present PathLift, a per-waypoint depth-conditioned adapter that lifts a pre-trained visual-navigation policy by consuming an explicit, sensor-grade depth source (RGB-D, stereo, or projected LiDAR) and fitting per-waypoint magnitude and lateral residuals to a canonicalized prior.
  PathLift consistently improves closed-loop navigation across diverse visual-navigation policies in simulation and on a real wheeled robot under both stereo and LiDAR depth. By turning the policy's unitless intent into a depth-feasible metric path in a single feed-forward pass, the adapter lifts real-world success without test-time optimization.

</details>


### [120] [Constrained Differentiable Policy Learning for Safe Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16119)
*Yan Rui Tan,Praveen Krishnapur,Zeyu Feng,Han Bao,Yew-Soon Ong,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将避障建模为显式约束，借助可微仿真以原始-对偶梯度法训练四旋翼策略，提出有界间隙约束函数与LME聚合，理论证明收敛到可行策略，实验改善安全-性能权衡并实现零样本真机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可微仿真方法通常将安全作为加权惩罚项，需要人工调节任务性能与约束满足之间的权衡系数，难以直接指定期望的安全水平。

Method: 将避障表述为显式约束，通过可微仿真器使用原始-对偶梯度下降-上升训练四旋翼策略；提出有界间隙（bounded clearance）约束函数，在安全边界附近提供有效梯度并防止大违规破坏对偶优化稳定性；引入对数均值指数（LME）聚合，将轨迹与训练批次中的关键单步安全违规平滑聚合为单一约束，由单一对偶变量控制。

Result: 理论上证明所提可微仿真优化在温和假设下收敛到可行策略；实验表明相较固定惩罚与现有策略学习基线，安全-性能权衡更优，并泛化到更密集的分布外环境；学习策略无需微调即可零样本迁移至Gazebo/PX4仿真与实体四旋翼。

Conclusion: 以显式约束替代加权惩罚并结合有界间隙约束与LME聚合，可实现对安全水平的直接指定，在保证收敛性的同时获得更优的安全-性能权衡，并具备仿真到现实的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation enables efficient policy optimization through analytic gradients, yet safety is commonly incorporated as a weighted penalty, requiring manual tuning of the trade-off between task performance and constraint satisfaction. We instead formulate obstacle avoidance as an explicit constraint and train quadrotor policies using primal--dual gradient descent--ascent through a differentiable simulator. We introduce two key components to enable effective constrained training. First, we propose a bounded clearance-based constraint function that provides informative gradients around the safety boundary while preventing large violations from destabilizing primal--dual optimization. Second, we introduce log-mean-exp (LME) aggregation to smoothly emphasize critical per-step safety violations across a trajectory and training batch within a single constraint governed by one dual variable. Together, these components enable the desired safety level to be specified directly while balancing constraint satisfaction and task completion. We theoretically prove that the proposed optimization with differentiable simulation converges to feasible policy under mild assumptions. Experiments demonstrate improved safety--performance trade-offs over fixed-penalty and existing policy-learning baselines, as well as generalization to denser out-of-distribution environments. Finally, the learned policy transfers zero-shot to Gazebo/PX4 and a physical quadrotor without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [121] [Directional Pullback Regularization for Long-Horizon Rollout of Latent World Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16139)
*Shatadal Mishra,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Nejib Ammar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出方向性拉回正则化（DPR），通过在解码状态空间定义运动学感知的各向异性黎曼度量并经由解码器雅可比拉回至潜空间，改善自动驾驶潜空间长时程滚动的质量，在10秒开环自车运动预测上优于无约束与欧氏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶学习型世界模型在无约束潜空间中运行，但其所表征的物理系统受严格非完整约束：地面车辆无法实现瞬时纯侧向运动。作者试图探究：滚动质量的提升是否可通过为潜状态转移赋予运动感知的几何结构来实现，而非重新设计预测器架构。

Method: 提出Directional Pullback Regularization (DPR)：一种训练时方法，在解码状态空间定义运动学感知的各向异性黎曼度量，并通过解码器雅可比将其拉回至潜流形，使解码为侧向车辆运动的潜方向被赋予更高代价，同时保留原有的编码器-动力学-解码器主干。据此构建三路对照：无约束潜滚动（ULR）、欧氏拉回正则消融（EPR）与完整方向性方法（DPR）。

Result: 在统一10秒测试时域的开环自车运动滚动评估中，训练滚动长度设为1、3、5、10步。四种训练设置下，DPR的10秒最终位移误差均低于ULR与EPR。相对精度增益最大出现在3步滚动监督模型，DPR较ULR将10秒最终位移误差降低约44%。控制相关权衡最优出现在更长滚动训练模型，尤其10步设置下，DPR在位移误差、侧向运动一致性与前向运动保持方面同时优于ULR与EPR。

Conclusion: 通过在潜空间引入运动学感知的各向异性几何（而非改动预测器架构），可有效提升自动驾驶长时程潜滚动的精度与物理一致性；方向性拉回正则化在多种训练滚动长度下均带来稳定增益，并在长滚动训练下实现精度与控制相关指标的综合改善。

Abstract: Learned world models for autonomous driving operate in unconstrained latent spaces, yet the physical systems they represent are governed by strict non-holonomic constraints: ground vehicles cannot realize instantaneous pure lateral motion. We study long-horizon latent rollout modeling for autonomous driving and ask whether rollout quality can be improved by assigning a motion-aware geometry to latent transitions, rather than by redesigning the predictor architecture. Starting from an unconstrained latent rollout baseline, we introduce Directional Pullback Regularization (DPR), a training time method that defines a kinematics-aware anisotropic Riemannian metric in decoded state space and pulls it back through the decoder Jacobian to the latent manifold. The resulting latent-space metric assigns higher cost to latent directions that decode to lateral vehicle motion, while preserving the original encoder-dynamics-decoder backbone. This yields a clean three-way comparison among an Unconstrained Latent Rollout model (ULR), an Euclidean Pullback Regularization ablation (EPR), and the full directional method (DPR). We evaluate all models on open-loop ego-motion rollout over a common 10-second test horizon, using training rollout lengths of 1, 3, 5, and 10 steps. Across all four training regimes, DPR achieves lower 10-second final displacement error than both ULR and EPR. The strongest relative accuracy gain appears for models trained with 3-step rollout supervision, where DPR reduces the 10-second final displacement error by approximately 44 percent relative to ULR. The strongest overall control-relevant tradeoff is obtained for longer rollout-trained models, especially at 10 steps, where DPR simultaneously improves displacement error, lateral motion consistency, and forward motion preservation relative to both ULR and EPR.

</details>


### [122] [FlowVLA: Enabling World Dynamics Understanding through Pluggable 3D Flow Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16160)
*Junhyeong Go,Mingyu Jung,Woobin Im,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowVLA是一个重建引导、可插拔的3D流学习框架，仅在训练阶段利用时序对齐的3D流监督VLA模型，推理时移除辅助头，不改变架构、不增加3D传感器输入或推理开销，从而提升机器人操作的鲁棒性与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型主要依赖2D图像-文本先验，缺乏对与物理动作相关的3D世界基本动态的准确表征。对静态2D视觉线索的过度依赖限制了VLA模型实现鲁棒机器人操作的能力。

Method: 提出FlowVLA框架：仅训练时使用时序对齐的3D流，无需显式3D传感器输入，也不改变推理时架构。设计模块化3D流学习框架与双层流监督：点级流引导模型预测任务关键3D点的未来位移，TCP级流聚合这些运动以对齐机器人TCP轨迹，从而聚焦语义上有意义的动态区域，将场景级运动与精确控制信号耦合。训练时仅增加轻量辅助头。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，FlowVLA一致地提升了操作性能、零样本鲁棒性，并优化了数据效率。

Conclusion: FlowVLA在推理时丢弃辅助头，因此不引入额外3D传感器输入、架构修改或推理时计算，为VLA模型提供内在的动作相关动态理解，是一种高效且可插拔的方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D reconstruction enable the extraction of temporally consistent 3D motion from RGB observations; however, current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models still predominantly rely on 2D image-text priors. Therefore, they do not accurately represent the fundamental dynamics of the 3D world associated with physical actions. This over-reliance on static 2D visual cues limits the capacity of VLA models in achieving robust robot manipulation. To address these limitations, we introduce FlowVLA, a reconstruction-guided, pluggable 3D flow learning framework aimed at empowering VLA models with an inherent understanding of action-relevant dynamics. Instead of requiring explicit 3D sensor inputs or altering the inference-time architecture, our method is designed to utilize temporally aligned 3D flow only during training. This allows the model to learn the dynamic evolution of the physical scene while preserving the computational efficiency of conventional 2D VLA backbones. To bridge scene-level dynamics with robotic manipulation, we propose a modular 3D flow learning framework with dual-level flow supervision. Specifically, point-level flow guides the model to forecast the future displacements of task-critical 3D points, while TCP-level flow aggregates these motions to align with the robot's TCP trajectories. This dual-level flow supervision encourages the model to focus on semantically meaningful dynamic regions, effectively coupling scene-level motion with precise control signals. Extensive evaluations across simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that FlowVLA consistently improves manipulation performance, zero-shot robustness, and optimizes data efficiency. FlowVLA adds only lightweight auxiliary heads during training and discards them at inference. Therefore, FlowVLA introduces no additional 3D sensor inputs, architectural modifications, or inference-time computation.

</details>


### [123] [Extending Wire-Driven Parallel Robots to Multi-Floating-Link Systems with an Inter-Body Wire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16173)
*Shintaro Inoue,Kento Kawaharazuka,Temma Suzuki,Sota Yuzaki,Keita Yoneda,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过引入"体间索"（inter-body wire），将传统单末端执行器的并联索驱动系统扩展为多浮动连杆系统，并用双体协同搬运假人的救援实验验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并联索驱动系统（parallel wire-driven systems）能传递大载荷（可达钢丝破断强度）、作用距离可达数十米，因而适合大工作空间内的强力驱动，近年还把树木、立柱等外部环境作为锚点实现现场驱动。然而，传统并联索驱动系统通常只考虑单一末端执行器：多个浮动连杆共享工作空间时，索与连杆之间会发生干涉，因此难以同时操控多个浮动连杆。该研究正是为解决这一多浮动连杆并行驱动难题而展开。

Method: 提出引入"体间索"（inter-body wire）的新构型：在多个浮动连杆之间增设索连接，从而在共享工作空间中主动约束和协调各连杆的相对运动，规避索与连杆的干涉，实现多浮动连杆的并联索驱动。方法上以真实机器人平台搭建双体（two bodies）并联索驱动系统开展实验。

Result: 在真机实验中，两个浮动连杆（双体）协同支撑并抬起一个假人（mannequin），并将其转移到担架上，完成了模拟救援搬运动作，验证了所提多浮动连杆并联索驱动的可行性与协同控制效果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，并联索驱动可从单一末端执行器扩展至多浮动连杆系统，为大型物体操纵、救援支援等更广泛任务提供了潜力。

Abstract: Parallel wire-driven systems are a framework in which multiple wires are connected to an end effector, and the end effector is driven by the tensions of those wires. Because they can transmit large forces, up to the breaking strength of the wires, over lengths on the order of several tens of meters, they can realize powerful actuation in large workspaces. In recent years, studies have also investigated connecting wires to external environments such as trees and columns and forming parallel wire actuation on site. However, conventional parallel wire-driven systems often assume a single end effector, and because of interference between wires and links in a shared workspace, it has been difficult to handle multiple floating links simultaneously. In this study, we realize multi-floating-link parallel wire actuation by introducing an inter-body wire. In real-robot experiments, the two bodies supported and lifted a mannequin and transferred it onto a stretcher as a rescue motion. This shows that parallel wire actuation can be extended from a single end effector to a multi-floating-link system, and indicates its potential for broader tasks such as the manipulation of large objects and rescue support.

</details>


### [124] [One-Shot Learning for Double-Shot Latte Art: Adapting Cobot Pouring Skills with Dynamic Movement Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16179)
*Zain Khan,Victor Hernandez Moreno,Dinh Tung Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将单次拉花演示分割为五个功能阶段，各阶段用独立笛卡尔动态运动基元（CDMP）编码，显著提升复现精度并实现无需重演示的尺度泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉花需要高灵巧性与精度，是具挑战性且具商业价值的任务；单次学习可降低对大量演示数据的依赖，但单一模型难以刻画阶段间差异明显的运动需求。

Method: 采用单次学习（one-shot learning）系统，将一次咖啡师演示分割为五个功能上不同的阶段，每个阶段编码为带独立参数的笛卡尔动态运动基元（CDMP），并与单一 CDMP 基线进行对比。

Result: 相较单 CDMP 基线，分阶段编码将过渡端点误差降低 3.2–4.5 倍、图案笔画形状误差降低 2.7 倍，倾倒阶段壶体倾角复现 RMS 误差低于 1 度；通过单一空间偏移与缩放配方即可将倒注动作从 280 mL 杯泛化至 400 mL 杯，无需重新演示。

Conclusion: 基于阶段的 CDMP 方法可推广至任何各阶段具有不同空间或时间要求的演示任务，并支持根据任务需求调整复现。

Abstract: Latte art is a complex task requiring dexterity and precision, making it a challenging and commercially relevant task. A one-shot learning system is employed in which a single barista demonstration is segmented into five functionally distinct phases, each encoded as an independent Cartesian Dynamic Movement Primitive (CDMP) with its own parameters. Against a single-CDMP baseline, per-phase encoding reduces transition endpoint error by 3.2-4.5 times and art-stroke shape error by 2.7 times, reproducing jug tilt to below 1 degree RMS in the pouring phases. The learnt pour scales from a 280mL to a 400mL cup through a single spatial offset and a scaled recipe, with no re-demonstration. The phase-based CDMP approach generalises beyond latte art to any demonstration task whose phases have distinct spatial or temporal requirements whilst demonstrating the ability to adapt reproductions to suit different task requirements.

</details>


### [125] [MSC: Multi-Stage Caster-aware Control Framework with Uncertainty-Aware NMPC and RL-based Execution Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16199)
*Jinho Park,Jeongtae Lee,Jeonghyeok Lim,Keun Ha Choi,Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出多阶段脚轮感知控制（MSC）分层框架，通过不确定性感知NMPC与强化学习执行层，缓解脚轮错位引起的接触非线性与规划-执行偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由旋转脚轮因机械简单、承载能力强而广泛用于工业AMR/SDV，但低速或高曲率机动时脚轮错位带来强接触非线性，导致指令速度(v, ω)与实际运动间存在明显规划-执行差距；现有脚轮感知策略高度依赖状态估计可靠性，在不确定过渡工况下执行不稳定。

Method: 提出MSC分层框架，包含两个核心机制：一是不确定性感知NMPC（CU-MPC），利用预测不确定性动态调度脚轮感知代价与输入正则化，抑制过激机动；二是基于强化学习的执行层，在部分可观测条件下估计并预测脚轮状态，将高层速度指令转化为适配当前接触条件的可执行轮速。

Result: 在Isaac Sim仿真与真实机器人上验证，MSC相较现有解耦补偿方法具有明显比较优势：显著提升脚轮状态估计精度，减少错位，并增强脚轮敏感机动中的跟踪鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MSC通过规划与执行一体化、不确定性感知与学习式执行相结合，有效缩小脚轮驱动平台的规划-执行差距，提升脚轮敏感工况下的鲁棒性与可执行性。

Abstract: Freely swiveling casters are widely adopted in industrial AMRs/SDVs for their mechanical simplicity and load capacity. However, caster misalignment during low-speed or high-curvature maneuvers induces strong contact nonlinearities, widening the planning-execution gap between commanded $(v, ω)$ and realized motion. Existing caster-aware strategies are highly sensitive to state-estimation reliability, causing unstable execution in uncertain transition regimes. To address this, we present Multi-Stage Caster-aware Control (MSC), a planning-execution integrated hierarchical framework featuring two core mechanisms. First, an uncertainty-aware NMPC (CU-MPC) dynamically schedules caster-aware costs and input regularization using predictive uncertainty to suppress overly aggressive maneuvers. Second, a reinforcement learning-based execution layer estimates and predicts caster states under partial observability, transforming high-level velocity commands into physically executable wheel speeds adapted to current contact conditions. Validations in Isaac Sim and real robots demonstrate that MSC offers a clear comparative advantage over existing decoupled compensation approaches by significantly improving caster-state estimation accuracy, minimizing misalignment, and enhancing tracking robustness in caster-sensitive maneuvers.

</details>


### [126] [Embodied Skill Bench: Benchmarking How Well Agents with Embodied Skills Perform on Physical Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16238)
*Zhi Han,Zihan Guo,Chenxi Zeng,Zicai Cui,Yue Xin,Ming Zhou,Dongzhan Zhou,Weinan Zhang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将VLA策略、感知模型与程序性知识封装为带具身假设与使用条件的"具身技能"，构建了250个技能与EmbodiedSkillBench基准，并提出Agent-Directed Reuse (ADR)模式；实验表明技能的价值随智能体能力增强而从"补足能力"转向"降低成本"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地通过VLA策略、感知模型和程序性知识控制机器人，但这些模块因接口、具身假设和使用条件不一致而难以复用，且其在物理任务上的收益尚不明确。因此需要统一封装方式并建立可量化评测的基准。

Method: 将上述模块封装为"具身技能"（embodied skills），在智能体技能基础上显式附加具身假设与使用条件，从1,052个资源中构建250个动作、感知与经验技能。在此基础上提出EmbodiedSkillBench：在闭环"观察-动作"循环中运行智能体，记录成功率、动作、token数与实际耗时；支持三种模式——原生控制、仅技能执行、以及新提出的Agent-Directed Reuse (ADR)，即智能体保留原生控制权，并对每个技能决定调用、修改或忽略。

Result: 在100个扰动化的LIBERO-Pro任务、4个智能体上评测：仅技能执行使GPT-5.6-Sol从11%提升至63%，Kimi-K3与Claude-Opus-5提升约20个百分点，同时token用量减少58%–85%；但四者最终均落在61%–64%区间，GPT-6-Astra从原生控制的92%降至61%。在ADR模式下，GPT-6-Astra回升至85%（距原生控制仅差7个百分点），token减少14.1%、时间减少11.9%；以Jev作为选择运动方向与夹爪动作的技能时，达到81%，token减少50%、时间减少57%。

Conclusion: 技能对智能体的帮助上限取决于其超出智能体已有能力的部分。随着智能体变强，具身技能的作用从"提供缺失能力"转向"降低成本"；对已能通过原生控制解决多数任务的强智能体，完全限制为技能会得不偿失。ADR通过保留原生控制并选择性调用技能，在接近原生性能的同时显著节省token与时间。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly control robots using VLA policies, perception models, and procedural knowledge. These modules are difficult to reuse because their interfaces, embodiment assumptions, and conditions of use differ, and their benefits on physical tasks remain unclear. We package them as embodied skills that extend agent skills with explicit embodiment assumptions and conditions of use, building 250 action, perception, and experience skills from 1,052 resources. On this basis we introduce EmbodiedSkillBench, which runs an agent in a closed observation-action loop and records success, actions, tokens, and wall-clock time. It supports three modes: native control, skill-only execution, and Agent-Directed Reuse (ADR), a mode we propose in which the agent keeps its native controls and decides for each skill whether to invoke, modify, or ignore it. Across four agents on 100 perturbed LIBERO-Pro tasks, we find that skills help an agent only as far as they exceed what it can already do. Skill-only execution lifts GPT-5.6-Sol from 11% to 63% and Kimi-K3 and Claude-Opus-5 by about 20 points, while cutting their token usage by 58% to 85%. Yet all four agents then end up between 61% and 64%, and GPT-6-Astra falls from 92% under native control to 61%. This suggests that as agents grow stronger, the role of embodied skills shifts from supplying missing capability to reducing cost: GPT-6-Astra already solves most tasks through native control, so restricting it to skills removes more than it adds. Under ADR, which lets it keep native control and call skills selectively, GPT-6-Astra recovers to 85%, within 7 points of native control, while using 14.1% fewer tokens and 11.9% less time. With Jev as a skill choosing motion directions and gripper actions, it reaches 81% with 50% fewer tokens and 57% less time. The code is available at https://github.com/HanZhi306/EmbodiedSkill-Bench.

</details>


### [127] [Same Goal, Different Words: Spatial Instruction Sensitivity in World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16242)
*Ali-Adeeb Abbas,Esmaeil Seraj,Behrad Toghi,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文评估世界-动作模型在等价空间目标描述下保持操纵意图的一致性。基于3个模型、4个RoboLab场景的864次rollout，发现以参考物体而非被移动物体描述关系会使稳定放置率平均下降12.2个百分点；指令改述的影响因空间关系而异；语言理解测试揭示关系反转，且加入图像会干扰原本正确的纯文本理解；稳定放置率从任意时刻的43.2%降至回合结束时的21.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型理应在空间目标的等价描述下保持一致的操纵意图，但聚合分数可能掩盖模型对措辞的敏感性。因此需要检验模型的可操控性、语言理解与grounding，避免评价被表面成功率掩盖。

Method: 固定初始状态与任务目标，对3个模型在4个RoboLab场景中的864次rollout进行评测；比较以参考物体描述关系与以被移动物体描述关系两种指令表述，并考察不同空间关系下指令改述的影响；另做独立的策略语言理解测试，对比纯文本与加入图像的情形；在rollout层面区分空间放置与指令遵循，并分别统计任意时刻与回合结束时的稳定放置率。

Result: 以参考物体而非被移动物体描述关系时，稳定放置率平均下降12.2个百分点；指令改述的影响随空间关系变化，说明聚合分数会掩盖措辞敏感性；独立语言理解测试发现关系反转，加入图像会破坏原本正确的纯文本理解；移动错误物体也可能满足所要求的关系，稳定放置率从任意时刻43.2%降至回合结束21.8%。

Conclusion: 现有世界-动作模型评测不足以反映其可操控性、语言理解与grounding。评价应检验等价指令下的一致性、确认被移动的是目标物体，并验证所要求的位置关系在整个rollout结束前是否保持。

Abstract: World-action models should preserve the intended manipulation across equivalent descriptions of a spatial goal. We evaluate this consistency in 864 rollouts from three models across four RoboLab scenes, holding initial states and task goals fixed. Describing relations through the reference object rather than the object-to-move lowers the rate of reaching a stable placement by 12.2 percentage points on average. The effect of instruction rephrasing varies across spatial relations, so aggregate scores can hide sensitivity to wording. Standalone tests of the policies' language understanding identify relation reversals and show that adding images can disrupt otherwise correct text-only interpretations. Rollout analysis also distinguishes spatial placement from instruction following: moving the wrong object can satisfy the requested relation, and rates of stable placement fall from 43.2% at any time to 21.8% at episode end. These findings motivate a more thorough evaluation of world-action models' steerability, language understanding, and grounding. Evaluations should test consistency across equivalent instructions, verify that the intended object moved, and check whether the requested arrangement persists to the end of the rollout.

</details>


### [128] [PACE: Temporal-Role Tactile Conditioning for Chunk-Based Insertion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16308)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Shixiong Xu,Shuai Chen,Yangwei You*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACE是一个面向chunk式插入策略的时序角色触觉条件化框架，将触觉流分为持续接触状态、突变接触事件和接触后果预测三种时序角色，分别用于生成条件化、chunk修正和训练期表征塑形，在真实机器人插入任务中平均成功率达90.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的插入任务需要机器人结合视觉对齐与触觉调整。但现有视触觉chunk式策略把触觉反馈压缩进单一的生成时融合通路，导致持续接触状态、突变接触事件与未来接触后果都被隐含在单一表征中建模，缺乏对不同时序角色的区分处理。

Method: PACE（Phase-Aware Contact-Event Conditioning）将共享触觉流组织为三种时序角色：1）持续接触状态用于条件化动作chunk的生成；2）突变接触事件用于对已生成chunk进行修正；3）接触后果预测在训练阶段塑形动作表征。该设计在保持chunk级动作连续性的同时，为不同时序角色的接触反馈分配独立通路。

Result: 在五项真实机器人插入任务中，PACE平均成功率达90.7%，较纯视觉与生成时触觉融合基线高出44个百分点以上；在ManipulationNet基准上，于六种几何间隙配置（含工业级0.1 mm间隙）下保持88.7%成功率。消融实验验证了三种时序角色的互补贡献；机理分析表明PACE能区分正常插入进程与需即时修正的接触事件；跨信号与跨架构实验说明该时序角色原则可推广至不同物理反馈信号与策略骨干。

Conclusion: PACE通过为触觉反馈的三种时序角色分配差异化通路，突破了单一生成时融合的表征瓶颈，在真实插入任务与高精度基准上均取得显著性能提升，且具备跨信号、跨架构的泛化性，为视触觉chunk式操作策略提供了有效的时序条件化范式。

Abstract: Contact-rich insertion requires robots to combine visual alignment with tactile adjustment. However, existing visuotactile chunk-based policies collapse tactile feedback into a single generation-time fusion pathway, leaving persistent contact states, abrupt contact events, and future contact consequences implicitly modeled in a single representation. We present PACE (Phase-Aware Contact-Event Conditioning), a temporal-role tactile conditioning framework for chunk-based insertion policies. PACE organizes a shared tactile stream into three temporal roles: persistent contact states condition action-chunk generation, abrupt contact events correct the generated chunk, and contact-consequence prediction shapes action representations during training. This design preserves chunk-level action continuity while assigning distinct pathways to different temporal roles of contact feedback. Across five real-robot insertion tasks, PACE achieves an average success rate of 90.7%, outperforming vision-only and generation-time tactile-fusion baselines by over 44 percentage points; on the ManipulationNet benchmark, PACE maintains 88.7% success across six geometry-clearance configurations, including an industrial-grade 0.1-mm clearance. Ablations confirm the complementary contributions of the three temporal roles, mechanistic analyses show that PACE separates normal insertion progress from contact events requiring immediate correction, and cross-signal and cross-architecture experiments indicate that this temporal-role principle extends across different physical feedback signals and policy backbones. Videos and materials are available at https://pace-insertion.github.io/.

</details>


### [129] [ReAP: From Visual Failure Recognition to Action-Conditioned Risk Prediction for Runtime Failure Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16309)
*Jingyuan Liu,Yunpeng Qing,Changxu Wei,Zhi Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReAP——一种未来感知的运行时监控框架，通过视觉失败识别与动作条件失败预测相结合，在动作执行前预判机器人策略的失败风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在执行中仍十分脆弱，可靠部署需要能及时预警失败的运行时监控。现有监控器多依赖当前观测或策略信号估计风险，未显式预测所提议动作的未来后果，难以在动作执行前预判风险。

Method: ReAP包含两个组件：（1）视觉检测器：在成功与失败的轨迹上训练，学习与失败相关的表征空间并估计当前风险；（2）动作条件预测器：在该空间中预测由动作导致的未来表征，从而估计未来失败风险。运行时两者提供互补信号，分别用于当前状态失败识别和未来感知风险评估。

Result: 在仿真与真实环境中、跨多种机器人策略的实验表明，ReAP能有效平衡失败检测准确率与预警时效性。

Conclusion: ReAP通过显式预测动作的未来后果，实现了兼具准确性与及时性的机器人策略运行时失败监控，为可靠部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, generalist robot policies remain brittle during execution. Reliable deployment therefore requires runtime monitoring that provides timely warnings of failure. Existing monitors commonly estimate risk from current observations or policy signals without explicitly predicting the future consequences of proposed actions. To anticipate such risks before action execution, we introduce ReAP, a future-aware runtime monitoring framework that combines visual failure recognition with action-conditioned failure prediction. Specifically, ReAP first trains a visual detector on successful and failed rollouts to learn a failure-relevant representation space and estimate current risk. An action-conditioned predictor then forecasts the resulting future representation in this space to estimate future failure risk. At runtime, the detector and predictor provide complementary signals for current-state failure recognition and future-aware risk assessment. Experiments across diverse robot policies in simulation and the real world demonstrate that ReAP effectively balances failure-detection accuracy and warning timeliness.

</details>


### [130] [Listen Before Act: Audio-Conditioned Robot Manipulation with ListenVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16313)
*Jiaqi Fan,Rui Xu,Jiakun Xia,Xuyu Fan,Shuailei Ma,Yuefeng Wang,Qing Wang,Jiarui Zhang,Yili Fu,Xu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ListenVLA：基于GR00T N1.7的音频条件VLA策略，通过音频令牌与特征调制实现声音引导操作，并提出音频-动作绑定目标与LIBERO-Audio基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要连接视觉感知与语言指令到动作生成，但许多操作任务依赖视觉难以可靠推断、且需通过物理交互才能引发的听觉线索，形成能力空白。

Method: 在GR00T N1.7上构建音频条件策略：结合语义音频嵌入与波形声学线索，基于因果音频历史建模；以音频令牌和特征调制条件化连续动作生成；提出音频-动作绑定目标，用上下文匹配示范将听觉差异关联到动作差异，监督不同音频条件下流速度预测的短时差异（共享纯噪声动作输入与固定非音频观测），并配合全序列动作监督；同时构建八任务LIBERO-Audio基准。

Result: 在LIBERO-Audio及三个真实世界操作任务上表现优异；消融研究支持绑定监督与音频调制的贡献；音频替换测试表明声音会影响动作预测。

Conclusion: ListenVLA验证了音频条件对VLA操作的有效性，音频-动作绑定监督与音频调制是性能提升的关键，代码与视频已公开。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robotic manipulation by connecting visual perception and language instructions to action generation. However, many manipulation tasks depend on auditory cues that cannot be reliably inferred from visual observations and may need to be elicited through physical interaction. To address this gap, we present ListenVLA, an audio-conditioned VLA policy built on GR00T N1.7 for sound-guided manipulation. ListenVLA combines semantic audio embeddings with waveform-derived acoustic cues over a causal audio history and conditions continuous action generation through audio tokens and feature modulation. Moreover, we introduce an audio-action binding objective that uses context-matched demonstrations to link auditory differences to demonstrated action differences. The objective supervises short-horizon differences between flow-velocity predictions under different audio conditions, with shared pure-noise action inputs and fixed non-audio observations, alongside full-chunk action supervision. Furthermore, we develop LIBERO-Audio, an eight-task benchmark covering sound-based target selection, responses to auditory events, and physical interactions that reveal acoustic evidence. Experiments on LIBERO-Audio and three real-world manipulation tasks show excellent performance. Ablation studies support the contributions of binding supervision and audio modulation, while audio swap tests show that sound influences action predictions. Our code and videos are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/listenvla-F18D

</details>


### [131] [Constraint-Decomposed Impedance Control from Generative Policy for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16316)
*Kyungseo Park,Byeongdo Lim,Jungbin Lim,Minseok Choi,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出约束分解阻抗控制：利用生成策略采样多样性，通过PCA从twist集合推断可行/受约束子空间，构造固定6×6阻抗增益矩阵，降低约束违背接触力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接物体操作（开门、拧螺栓、拉抽屉）对末端运动施加运动学约束，神经策略难以满足；阻抗控制虽比混合力位控制更灵活，但任务合适的阻抗增益难以选取。

Method: 对同一观测下生成策略产生的轨迹集合，对末端twist做PCA确定可行twist子空间，据此构造6×6阻抗增益矩阵。多数铰接物体的可行twist子空间与构型无关，故子空间与增益只需估计一次并保持固定；少数不满足独立性时，估计包含局部可行子空间的更高维构型无关子空间，仍可固定增益。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界的单自由度和多自由度物体实验中，相比各向同性阻抗控制，所提方法在保持任务执行的同时持续降低了违反约束的接触载荷。

Conclusion: 以策略采样多样性估计twist可行子空间并转化为固定阻抗增益，是一种计算开销小、可泛化于常见铰接物体操作的约束分解阻抗控制方案。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation tasks such as opening doors, tightening bolts, and pulling drawers impose kinematic constraints on a robot end-effector's motion that neural policies may fail to satisfy. Unlike hybrid force-position control that strictly separates force and position control into orthogonal subspaces, impedance control modulates a mechanical impedance system in the form of a virtual inertia, damping, and stiffness. However, selecting task-appropriate impedance gains remains challenging for robot policies. In this paper, we address this problem for the case of manipulating articulated objects. Our constraint-decomposed impedance control method uses the sampling diversity of a generative policy to estimate the feasible motion directions in twist space. The key idea is to infer the feasible and constrained subspaces of twists from an ensemble of trajectories generated by the policy from the same observation. Principal component analysis (PCA) of the end-effector twists determines the feasible twist subspace, which is then used to form 6x6 impedance gain matrices. For many common articulated objects, the feasible twist subspace is independent of the object configuration, so the subspace and resulting gains are estimated once and held fixed during manipulation, minimizing computational overhead. For less common cases where this independence does not hold, we instead estimate a configuration-independent twist subspace of higher dimension that contains the local feasible subspaces, allowing the same fixed-gain strategy to be used. Experiments across simulated and real-world single- and multi-DoF objects show that the proposed method consistently reduces constraint-violating contact loads relative to isotropic impedance control while maintaining task execution.

</details>


### [132] [Imitation Learning of Avoidance Modes for Efficient Multimodal Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16326)
*Changyu Lee,Byung-Kwan Lee,Kanghoon Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SideNet-MPC，用模仿MM-MPC专家模式决策的图神经网络替代避障侧向枚举，每个控制周期至多求解2个OCP，在混合交通闭环仿真中达到专家93%成功率且计算耗时降低48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型预测控制（MM-MPC）通过对每个避让侧组合求解一次OCP来避免移动障碍物间的局部极小值，但其计算成本随决策关键障碍物数量指数增长，需要一种降低计算量的方法。

Method: 将MM-MPC作为专家，用图神经网络模仿学习其最小代价模式（每个障碍物一个避让侧）；网络边编码每对障碍物在车辆首次到达任一障碍物时刻的预测间隙，使阻塞偏好通道的邻居可反转建议侧向；保留专家的选择规则（比较最小代价模式与此前执行模式），仅用网络建议替代最小代价模式，故每周期至多求解2个OCP，实际输入仍来自带控制障碍函数约束的OCP而非网络输出。

Result: 100次种子匹配的混合交通闭环蒙特卡洛实验中，SideNet-MPC达到专家93%的成功率，单线程每控制周期耗时18.6ms，比专家在16个并行线程上的35.5ms减少48%；在受控间隙扫描中，其建议侧向的反转间隙区间与专家一致，而无成对边的建议器（同数据训练）从不反转，并在确定性场景的阻塞门处全部死锁。

Conclusion: 通过图神经网络模仿专家模式决策并以OCP保证可行性与安全性，SideNet-MPC在保持接近专家成功率的同时显著降低在线计算量，且成对边编码对侧向反转与避免死锁至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal model predictive control (MM-MPC) avoids local minima among moving obstacles by solving one optimal control problem (OCP) per combination of avoidance sides, so its computational cost grows exponentially with the number of decision-critical obstacles. We propose SideNet-MPC, which replaces this enumeration with a mode decision learned by imitating MM-MPC as an expert. A graph neural network is trained to predict the expert's minimum-cost mode, one avoidance side per obstacle. Its edges encode, for each obstacle pair, the gap predicted for the moment the vehicle first reaches either obstacle, so a neighbor that blocks a preferred passage can reverse the proposed side. The expert's selection rule compares its minimum-cost mode with the previously executed mode; SideNet-MPC keeps this rule and substitutes the network proposal for the minimum-cost mode. Hence at most two OCPs are solved per control period, and every applied input still comes from an OCP with control barrier function constraints rather than from the network. In $100$ seed-matched closed-loop Monte Carlo runs of mixed traffic, SideNet-MPC reaches $93\%$ of the expert's success rate and needs \SI{18.6}{ms} per control period on a single thread, 48\% less than the expert's \SI{35.5}{ms} on sixteen parallel threads. In a controlled gap sweep, its proposed side reverses in the same gap interval as the expert's, whereas proposers without pairwise edges, trained on the same data, never reverse and all deadlock at the blocked gates of a deterministic scene.

</details>


### [133] [Toward Study on the Effects of Robot's Humorous Apology on Human's Trust Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16334)
*Gayoung Kim,Go-Eum Cha,Wonse Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个2×2被试间实验，考察幽默机器人道歉对信任恢复的作用及错误严重性的调节效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人错误会降低用户信任与依赖意愿，道歉有助于信任恢复，但其效果可能随道歉策略和错误严重性而变化，幽默道歉的作用尚不明确。

Method: 采用2（错误严重性：低 vs. 高）×2（道歉策略：通用 vs. 幽默）被试间因子设计；基于开源Warehouse Robot Interaction Simulator开展在线实验，参与者完成协作任务并经历机器人错误与随后的道歉，随后测量信任、信任恢复、交互体验与复用意愿。

Result: 此为研究提案，尚未报告实证结果；提出假设：低严重性错误后，幽默道歉比通用道歉带来更强的信任恢复，但该优势在高严重性错误后减弱甚至反转。

Conclusion: 研究预期揭示幽默道歉在信任恢复中的边界条件，表明其有效性取决于错误严重性，为机器人道歉设计提供依据。

Abstract: Robot errors can disrupt human-robot collaboration by reducing user trust and willingness to rely on the robot. Robot apologies can support trust recovery after such errors, but their effects may vary with apology strategy and error severity. This study therefore presents a user experiment based on a 2x2 between-subjects factorial design to examine the effect of humorous robot apologies on user trust recovery and the moderating role of error severity. The design crosses robot error severity (low vs. high) with apology strategy (generic vs. humorous). An online experiment will be conducted using the open-source Warehouse Robot Interaction Simulator. Participants will complete a collaborative task, experience a robot error followed by an apology, and report trust, trust recovery, interaction experience and reuse intention. We hypothesize that humorous apologies will produce greater trust recovery than generic apologies after low-severity errors, but that the advantage of humor will weaken or reverse after high-severity errors.

</details>


### [134] [PINT: A Unified Model with Shared Predictive Intent for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16355)
*Xinyu Guo,Chao Jing,Li Yi,Xingyu Chen,Zhengxing Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PINT是一个统一模型，通过共享的"块级预测意图（Predictive Intent）"协调VLM语义推理、VGM世界建模与动作生成，同时保留各专家专属通路，在无需具身预训练下显著提升分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作可受益于预训练VLM的语义推理与VGM的世界建模能力，但现有统一模型通过多层共享注意力直接融合二者，会削弱各自专用能力，难以充分发挥其专长。

Method: 在VLM序列中插入可学习的Intent Tokens形成块级Predictive Intent，用以条件化VGM与动作专家（AE）；仅替换VGM的prompt条件并在推理时保留VGM，AE通过单向注意力读取其表示。Predictive Intent不预先规定编码内容，而是由VLM、VGM、AE的原生预测目标联合学习。训练分两阶段：阶段1冻结VGM，使未来视频预测以任务相关未来动态塑造Intent；阶段2固定学得的Intent，微调VGM与AE。

Result: 无需具身预训练，PINT在分布内（ID）表现强劲并大幅提升OOD泛化：RoboTwin 2.0-Hard达15.5%（Fast-WAM 1.8%、StarVLA 3.2%），LIBERO-Plus达79.5%（分别为51.5%与75.0%）。在五项真实双臂任务中，PINT达68% ID与24% OOD成功率，对比预训练Motus的35%/9%与预训练π0.5的67%/35%。

Conclusion: 通过块级Predictive Intent协调并保留专家专属通路，PINT在无需具身预训练下实现了更强的ID性能与显著更优的OOD泛化，优于现有统一模型。

Abstract: Generalist robot manipulation can benefit from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) for semantic reasoning and video generation models (VGMs) for world modeling. However, existing unified models that directly fuse them through shared attention at multiple layers can make it harder to draw on their specialized capabilities. We introduce PINT, a unified model that coordinates semantic reasoning, world modeling, and action generation through a shared chunk-level Predictive Intent while preserving expert-specific pathways. PINT inserts learnable Intent Tokens into the VLM sequence to form this representation, conditioning both the VGM and an Action Expert (AE). It replaces only the VGM's prompt conditioning and retains the VGM at inference, where the AE reads its representations through one-way attention. Rather than prescribing what Predictive Intent should encode, PINT learns it jointly from the native prediction objectives of the VLM, VGM, and AE, which provide complementary views of the same upcoming task execution. Training uses two stages: Stage 1 freezes the VGM so that future video prediction shapes Predictive Intent with task-relevant future dynamics, while Stage 2 fixes the learned Intent and adapts the VGM and AE. Without embodied pretraining, PINT achieves strong in-distribution (ID) performance and substantially improves out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization, reaching 15.5% on RoboTwin 2.0-Hard compared with 1.8% for Fast-WAM and 3.2% for StarVLA, and 79.5% on LIBERO-Plus compared with 51.5% and 75.0%, respectively. Across five real-world bimanual tasks, PINT reaches 68% ID and 24% OOD success, compared with 35%/9% for pretrained Motus and 67%/35% for pretrained $π_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [135] [RoboBugBench: Benchmarking Automated Repair of Real Robot Software Under an Audited Behavioral Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16369)
*Alexey Tuzikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboBugBench：33 个真实、经执行验证的 ROS 2 导航栈缺陷，采用编译+物理仿真行为作为判定 oracle，而非单元测试。三个前沿模型单次修复率 24.2%–36.4%（无显著两两差异）；难度取决于 oracle 类型（过程存活 66.7% vs 行为判定 22.2%，p = 0.0027）。对抗性审计显示伪造补丁 11/11 被现行规则接受，分布规则可拒绝 4 个且无假拒；剩余误差为可观测性下界，需靠插桩而非更严判据提升区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体日益参与机器人软件修改，但其修复能力缺乏度量：机器人领域没有可继承的 oracle——缺陷表现为行为，且仓库几乎不含能捕获该行为的测试。因此需要一个以执行与仿真行为为判据的基准来量化修复能力，并检验 oracle 本身的可靠性。

Method: 构建 RoboBugBench：固定版本 ROS 2 导航栈中 33 个真实、经执行验证的缺陷；候选补丁的评分方式为编译并通过物理仿真驱动机器人，而非运行单元测试。三个前沿模型每实例仅 1 次尝试。对构造出的 oracle 进行对抗性审计，比较现行规则与同一数据上的分布规则，并分解出伪造补丁接受率、假拒代价与可观测性界。

Result: 模型修复率 24.2%–36.4%，两两差异不显著；按 oracle 类型分层：过程存活类 66.7%，行为判定类 22.2%（实例级 p = 0.0027）。对抗审计中，压制失败但未修复缺陷的伪造补丁被现行规则接受 11/11；分布规则拒绝其中 4 个且假拒零成本。残差为可观测性下界——观测通道丢弃的信息无法由任何谓词恢复。

Conclusion: 机器人修复评测的难度主要由 oracle 而非领域决定；区分度只能通过插桩获得，而非收紧判据。报告的修复率应视为界：因 oracle 无法区分而被高估，又因一致性判定在运行间噪声下拒绝正确修复而被低估。发布语料、判据组、伪造补丁套件与缓存遥测。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly modify robot software, but whether they can repair it is unmeasured, because the domain supplies no oracle to inherit: a robot bug manifests as behavior, and robot repositories ship almost no tests that capture it. We present RoboBugBench, a benchmark of 33 real, execution-validated defects in a pinned ROS 2 navigation stack, in which a candidate patch is graded by compiling it and driving the robot through a physics simulation rather than by running a unit test. Three frontier models, each given 1 attempt per instance, repair 24.2%-36.4%, with no pairwise difference significant; difficulty tracks the oracle rather than the domain, at 66.7% on instances graded by process survival against 22.2% on those graded by what the robot did (instance-level p = 0.0027). Because the oracle is constructed rather than inherited we audit it adversarially, and report the decomposition that audit yields: counterfeit patches which suppress a failure without repairing the defect are accepted by the shipped rule 11/11, while a distributional rule over the same data rejects 4 at no cost in false rejects. The residual is an observability bound (what the observation channel discards, no predicate over it can recover), so discrimination must be bought with instrumentation rather than stricter criteria, and our repair rates are bounds: inflated by what the oracle cannot distinguish, deflated by unanimity's rejection of correct fixes under run-to-run noise. We release the corpus, criterion batteries, counterfeit suite and cached telemetry.

</details>


### [136] [LABIT: Long-Horizon Robotic Assembly Benchmark for Industrial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16398)
*Constantin Schempp,Christian Friedrich,Luca Kofler,Laura Suhm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出LABIT——一个面向长时程操作的工业机器人装配基准，涵盖DIN标准装配任务（拧紧、放置、插入、卡扣），在高/低公差条件下评估，并提供基于装配复杂度分类的基准协议与新性能指标及基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配学习已从抓取、插入等离散单任务发展到完整装配构建，但现有基准多聚焦单一任务，难以覆盖现代制造流程的复杂性与步骤间相互关联；因此需要覆盖更广泛装配与子装配步骤的基准。

Method: 提出LABIT工业机器人装配基准，包含DIN规范装配任务（拧紧、放置、插入、卡扣）及高、低公差条件；设计基准测试协议与基于装配复杂度分类的新型性能指标，并提供零件清单及对应CAD模型；采用传统力-位置控制进行基线评估。

Result: 报告了按图元、子任务、装配类别和子装配划分的可靠性（reliability）与得分（score）指标，作为该基准的基线结果；相关数据发布于项目网站。

Conclusion: 由于装配步骤具有连续性，先前误差会影响后续操作，LABIT尤其适用于评估智能机器人系统的鲁棒性；其协议、指标、零件与CAD模型为机器人装配研发提供基础工具。

Abstract: Robot learning has undergone significant advancements in the domain of compliant control and robotic assembly. The development of novel methodologies has transcended the capacity to address discrete tasks, such as grasping or inserting, and has evolved to encompass the comprehensive construction of entire assemblies. Conventional benchmarks, which are predominantly focused on single tasks, are no longer adequate to address the complexity and interconnected nature of modern manufacturing processes. Consequently, the necessity for benchmarks to encompass a broader spectrum of assembly and subassembly steps is becoming apparent. In light of these developments, we propose LABIT, an industrial robotic assembly benchmark for long-horizon manipulation. This benchmark encompasses DIN-norm assembly tasks, including screwing, placing, inserting, and snapping, under both high and low tolerance conditions. The successive nature of these assembly steps implies that prior errors can influence subsequent manipulation. Consequently, this benchmark is particularly relevant for assessing the robustness of intelligent robotic systems. The proposed benchmarking protocols and novel performance metric, based on a taxonomy of assembly complexity in conjunction with the comprehensive list of parts and their corresponding CAD models, serve as essential tools for research and development in the field. To provide a baseline, we evaluate the benchmark using traditional force-position control and report reliability and score metrics per primitive, subtask, assembly category, and subassembly. Accompanying data is available at https://irp-hka.github.io/robotic-assembly-benchmark/.

</details>


### [137] [Estimating the Causal Impact of Social Navigation Signals in Corridor Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16411)
*Francesco Baldo,Luca Castri,Gloria Beraldo,Nicola Bellotto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对人机共享环境中的社交导航信号决策问题，提出用因果推断模型区分混淆因素，发现忽略混淆的观测估计会得出社会信号有害的错误结论，而因果干预估计表明该信号实际有益，强调了因果模型在社交机器人导航信号决策中的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类共享环境中部署机器人时，不仅需要决定如何导航，还需判断何时值得发出专门的社交信号。传统的基于观测的推断在存在混淆因素时会偏置信号与任务结果之间的关系估计，导致错误决策。因此需要一种能 disentangle 这些关系、估计干预效应的因果推断框架。

Method: 研究一个走廊场景中的人机交互，将交互表示为因果 DAG，涉及机器人动作、行人占用、可用空间、任务结果以及一个受控混淆变量（静态障碍）。将干预估计与仅条件化于机器人动作（不显式建模干预）的观测估计进行对比。

Result: 结果表明，考虑混淆变量会逆转朴素观测分析得出的结论：观测估计错误地显示社交信号有害，而因果估计显示其有益。这一差异凸显了未考虑混淆会导致错误结论。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了因果模型对于社交机器人导航中情境感知信号决策的重要性，提示在决定是否发出社交信号时应采用因果推断以校正混淆偏置。

Abstract: Deploying robots in human-shared environments requires deciding not only how to navigate, but also when dedicated social signals are worth emitting. Classic observation-based inference can be misleading in such settings: confounding factors may bias the estimated relationship between a signal and the task outcome, leading to incorrect decisions. Causal inference provides a framework for disentangling these relationships by modelling the mechanisms linking observations, actions, and outcomes, and by estimating interventional effects after accounting for confounding paths. We study a human-robot interaction scenario in a corridor and use a causal model to estimate whether a social navigation signal causally improves transit success. We represent the interaction as a causal DAG involving robot action, pedestrian occupancy, available space, task outcome, and a controlled confounder (static obstacles). We compare the interventional estimate with the observational estimate obtained by conditioning only on the robot action, which does not explicitly model interventions. Our results show that accounting for confounding variables can reverse the conclusions drawn from naive observational analysis: the observational estimate wrongly suggests that the social signal is detrimental, whereas the causal estimate shows that it is beneficial. This discrepancy highlights how failing to account for confounding can lead to incorrect conclusions and demonstrates the importance of causal models for context-aware signalling decisions in social robot navigation.

</details>


### [138] [SHOES: Sim-to-real Hardware-adaptation via On-policy Expansion of Skills for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16457)
*Kota Tsuruhara,Tsung-Yuan Chen,Kento Kawaharazuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SHOES框架，通过冻结名义策略、仅微调轻量身份初始化调制模块，并引入SafeBox工作空间约束，实现四足机器人对鞋类引起形态变化的样本高效在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可借鞋类改变地面接触界面以适应不同地形，但传统运动控制器针对固定形态训练，当鞋类改变足部几何、质量与摩擦时性能会显著下降，因此需要一种能在硬件上高效适应鞋类引起形态变化的方法。

Method: SHOES冻结仿真训练的名义策略，仅微调轻量、以身份映射初始化的调制模块，从而保留初始行为并实现高效硬件适应；同时提出SafeBox，一种工作空间感知的路点采样器，将在线适应约束在用户定义工作空间内。在装备鞋具的Unitree Go2上进行仿真与真机实验，并与FiLM-only、Linear Adapter-only及残差RL基线对比。

Result: 仿真结果表明，SHOES在鞋类引起形态变化后能以有限额外适应量大幅恢复运动性能；在相同适应预算下优于FiLM-only、Linear Adapter-only和残差RL基线；真机实验进一步验证了SHOES在硬件部署中的有效性。

Conclusion: SHOES通过冻结名义策略加轻量调制模块与SafeBox约束，为鞋类形态变化下的四足运动控制提供了一种样本高效、可硬件部署的适应方案。

Abstract: Humans expand their locomotion capability by wearing appropriate footwear, such as hiking boots for mountains or skates for ice. Footwear changes the ground-contact interface and enables traversal of otherwise difficult terrain. In contrast, locomotion controllers are typically trained for a fixed morphology, and their performance can degrade when shoes alter foot geometry, mass, and friction. We propose SHOES, a sample-efficient framework for adapting locomotion policies to footwear-induced morphology changes. SHOES freezes a simulation-trained nominal policy and fine-tunes only lightweight, identity-initialized modulation modules on the robot, preserving the initial behavior while enabling efficient hardware adaptation. We also introduce SafeBox, a workspace-aware waypoint sampler that constrains online adaptation within a user-defined workspace. The simulation results with a shoe-equipped Unitree Go2 show that SHOES substantially recovers locomotion performance after footwear-induced morphology changes using limited additional adaptation. Under the same adaptation budget, SHOES outperforms FiLM-only, Linear Adapter-only, and residual-RL baselines. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 fitted with human shoes further evaluate the effectiveness of SHOES for hardware deployment.

</details>


### [139] [DeTac: Dual-branch Decoupling of Texture and Force Signals in Vision-Based Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16463)
