Method: BoxWAM 以任务相关物体的有向包围盒（oriented bounding box）轨迹替代稠密未来帧，通过显式"想象"以物体为中心的未来运动，构造一个轻量的预测目标，以捕捉机器人动作引发的关键物理变化。

Result: 包围盒轨迹预测显著降低计算成本，同时提升动作预测性能。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心的紧凑状态表示可作为机器人操作中基于视频的世界动作建模的高效替代方案。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) are a category of policy models that ex- plicitly predict both actions and future world states. They have been shown to outperform action-only models across various robotic manipulation tasks. How- ever, representing the future with full-frame video prediction can be computation- ally expensive during both training and inference. This raises a central question: what compact state representation preserves the planning and supervision bene- fits of future prediction? In this paper, we propose BoxWAM, which replaces dense future frames with oriented bounding box trajectories of task-relevant ob- jects. By explicitly imagining object-centric future motion, our method provides a lightweight prediction target that captures the key physical changes induced by robot actions. We show that box trajectory prediction substantially reduces com- putational cost while improving action prediction performance, suggesting that object-centric compact states can serve as an efficient alternative to video-based world action modeling for robotic manipulation. Result videos can be found on https://box-wam.github.io

</details>


### [175] [Sim4RAD: A MATLAB Toolkit for Design-Phase Sizing, Actuator Selection and URDF-Model Verification of Serial Robotic Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17534)
*Jingchen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sim4RAD is a MATLAB toolkit for actuator sizing and verification of robot arms, built on a parameterized rigid-body model with static, dynamic, stochastic, and workspace/dexterity analyses. Applied to a 7-DOF anthropomorphic arm, it shows joint sizing is driven by posture rather than sampled motion, reveals the torque–dexterity trade-off of a payload-coaxial wrist joint, and demonstrates that the motion scenario dominates torque demands. 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Actuator sizing is an early and consequential design decision: joint torque and velocity specifications determine the motors, gearboxes, and brakes before any CAD model exists, so errors at this stage propagate into the whole design. Existing workflows lack a lightweight, reproducible tool that supports both the specification phase and the subsequent verification phase with the same analysis suite.

Method: The authors develop Sim4RAD, a MATLAB toolkit that constructs a rigid-body model from a small parameter set and provides four analyses: (1) a static analysis combining lever-arm sampling with a posture search, yielding rated torque at a chosen payload envelope and a reproducible peak torque; (2) a dynamics pipeline that reshapes a trajectory under one of four constraints, including the actuator torque-speed envelope; (3) a stochastic analysis over many random motions so specifications rest on a motion class rather than a single trajectory; (4) a workspace and dexterity analysis separating reachability from reach quality. Replacing the synthesized model with a URDF file repurposes the same analyses for verification. The toolkit is evaluated on a 7-DOF anthropomorphic arm in three layouts differing in shoulder axis order and in whether the last wrist joint is coaxial with the payload.

Result: Static demand at half arm length is nearly identical across the three layouts and sizes the joints rather than the sampled motion. Reordering the shoulder axes leaves the worst-case torque unchanged at 55 N·m and the reachable volume within 8%. A last wrist joint coaxial with the payload eliminates its gravity torque, reduces the adjacent wrist joint by almost one third, and cuts the last joint demand tenfold in a 5 m/s scan, at the cost of dexterity (mean 0.570 to 0.348). The same arm requires 20–37 times more torque for a 5 m/s target than for a 2 s motion.

Conclusion: Actuator sizing should be driven by the fixed motion scenario rather than by an individual sampled trajectory, since the scenario, not the trajectory, must be determined first. Shoulder axis reordering has limited impact on worst-case torque and reachable volume, whereas making the last wrist joint coaxial with the payload yields large torque savings at a measurable dexterity cost. Verification should be performed on the URDF model of the detailed design; the toolkit is openly available at https://github.com/LasterLi/Sim4RAD.

Abstract: Actuator sizing is an early and consequential decision: joint torque and velocity specifications fix the motors, gearboxes and brakes before any CAD model exists. Sim4RAD is a MATLAB toolkit for that phase and for the verification that follows it. It builds a rigid-body model from a handful of parameters, and four analyses supply the specification: a static analysis combining lever-arm sampling with a posture search, giving a rated torque at a chosen payload envelope and a reproducible peak torque; a dynamics pipeline that reshapes a trajectory under one of four constraints, including the actuator torque-speed envelope; a stochastic analysis over many random motions, so a specification rests on a class of motions, not one trajectory; and a workspace and dexterity analysis that separates where the arm can reach from how it can reach there. Replacing the synthesized model by a URDF file turns the same analyses to verification. A 7-DOF anthropomorphic arm is compared in three layouts differing in the shoulder axis order and in whether the last wrist joint is coaxial with the payload. The static demand at half arm length is nearly the same in all three, and it sizes the joints rather than the sampled motion; reordering the shoulder axes leaves the worst-case torque unchanged at 55 N m and the reachable volume within 8 percent. A last wrist joint coaxial with the payload removes its gravity torque, reduces the adjacent wrist joint by almost a third and cuts the last joint demand tenfold in a 5 m/s scan, at the price of dexterity (mean 0.570 to 0.348). The same arm needs 20 to 37 times more torque for a 5 m/s target than for a 2 s motion: the scenario, not the trajectory, must be fixed first. Verification should use the URDF model of the detailed design; the toolkit is available at https://github.com/LasterLi/Sim4RAD.

</details>


### [176] [ATOM: Abstract Transition and Option Modeling - Experience-Informed Hierarchical Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17555)
*Changxuan Yao,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATOM：分层图规划+可复用option策略，实现大规模3D环境高效重复导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模3D环境（如3D场景图）中重复导航任务规划开销大，需兼顾全局最优性与计算效率。

Method: 在3DSG的Rooms/Places等分层图上，将每条有向抽象转移关联一个导航option；为每个转移计算并存储可复用option策略及其状态依赖cost-to-go；跨任务维护option起始状态的平滑经验分布，据此逐步精化抽象转移代价。

Result: 相比单体MDP基线，累积重复规划时间减少97.5%，轨迹接近Global Places A*最短路径参考约7%以内；经验驱动精化使抽象转移代价估计误差再降10.3%；F1Tenth实车完成全部5个导航查询，43%的跨区域option执行复用先前解。

Conclusion: ATOM通过分层抽象与可复用option策略及经验驱动的代价精化，显著提升重复导航效率并保持近最优轨迹，且经实车验证可行。

Abstract: We propose ATOM (Abstract Transition and Option Modeling), a hierarchical navigation framework that combines higher-level graph planning with reusable lower-level option policies for efficient repeated navigation in large-scale 3D environments represented by hierarchical structures such as 3D scene graphs (3DSGs). ATOM operates on a hierarchical graph representation, implemented using, for instance, the Rooms and Places layers of a 3DSG, where each directed abstract transition is associated with a navigation option. For each transition, ATOM computes and stores a reusable option policy along with its state-dependent cost-to-go. Across repeated navigation tasks, ATOM maintains a smoothed empirical distribution over option initiation states and uses it to progressively refine the corresponding abstract transition cost. Ablation studies show that ATOM reduces cumulative repeated-planning time by 97.5% relative to a monolithic MDP baseline while maintaining trajectories within approximately 7% of a Global Places A* shortest-path reference. Experience-informed refinement further reduces abstract transition-cost estimation error by 10.3%. Physical experiments validate ATOM on an F1Tenth vehicle, successfully completing all five navigation queries with 43% of inter-region option executions reusing previously generated solutions.

</details>


### [177] [Mobility-Budget-Constrained Submodular Maximization for Distributed Semantic Multi-Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17562)
*Changxuan Yao,Sandilya Sai Garimella,Lu Gan,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分布式语义探索框架，融合Dirichlet建图、预算约束轨迹选择与RAG协调，兼顾信息增益与可达性，在保证近似最优性下实现覆盖与语义精度的领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人探索在通信与移动性约束下需权衡信息增益与可达性，现有方法难以在预算受限条件下同时保证协调质量与路径可行性。

Method: 每个机器人对前沿进行聚类；利用单次预算受限的Dijkstra搜索计算地形与朝向感知的最小代价路径；以Dirichlet熵的期望下降（边际信息增益）选择可行路径；采用Resource-Aware distributed Greedy (RAG)进行分布式协调。

Result: 单机器人每轮选择为精确最优；团队每轮联合信息增益至少为中心化最优的一半，且在通信受限时界值平缓退化。十次网格世界消融显示覆盖率、语义mIoU与地图确定性提升，熵和单位距离遍历代价降低。预算敏感性实验中中间预算最优。在照片级真实感全系统仿真中与五种原生协调规则对比，取得最高平均覆盖率与语义精度，且唯一满足单轨迹移动预算。

Conclusion: 该分布式语义探索框架在预算与通信约束下具有良好的理论保证与实证性能，优于最短距离前沿选择及多种协调规则。

Abstract: Multi-robot exploration under communication and mobility constraints requires balancing information gain with reachability. We present a distributed semantic exploration framework that combines Dirichlet mapping, budget-constrained trajectory selection, and Resource-Aware distributed Greedy (RAG) coordination. Each robot clusters frontiers, computes terrain- and heading-aware least-cost paths with a single budget-limited Dijkstra search, and selects feasible paths by marginal information gain, represented by the expected reduction in Dirichlet entropy. Each robot's per-round selection is exact, the team's joint per-round information gain is at least half the centralized optimum, and the bound degrades gracefully under limited communication. Ten-run gridworld ablations show improved coverage, semantic mIoU, and map certainty with lower entropy and traversal cost per unit distance than shortest-distance frontier selection. Budget-sensitivity experiments show that an intermediate budget best balances path reach and receding-horizon execution. In photorealistic full-system simulation against five coordination rules run in their native published forms, the proposed method attains the highest mean coverage and semantic accuracy while being the only one that respects the per-trajectory mobility budget.

</details>


### [178] [EgoCPT: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from a Single Ego-Centric Video with Contact-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17572)
*Zhixin Sun,Kairui Shi,Tianao Ren,Ming Yang,Mark Cutkosky,James Jingxi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EgoCPT，从第一人称视频直接学习高质量、物理可行的灵巧手重定向轨迹，并可部署到真实机器人，在噪声与高质量两个基准上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人专家演示采集困难，而互联网上人类第一人称视频丰富易得。但从人类视频学习灵巧操作的核心挑战在于具身差异大，难以重建物理上可落地的机器人轨迹。

Method: 仅需第一人称RGB视频与参考手部姿态轨迹（可由现成软件或专用相机获取）。提出接触感知优化框架，迭代优化机器手与物体之间的接触点，从而对噪声手部姿态具有鲁棒性，直接输出可部署的重定向灵巧手轨迹。

Result: 在五个操作任务上，噪声EgoDex手部姿态重建整体成功率67.3%，运动捕捉硬件采集的高质量TACO重建成功率92.0%，在两个基准上均超过多个基线。

Conclusion: EgoCPT为从第一人称视频学习灵巧手轨迹提供了无需真实机器人演示的有效范式，兼顾鲁棒性与可部署性，验证了接触感知优化的关键作用。

Abstract: While expert demonstrations on real robots are difficult to collect, human ego-centric videos are easy to collect and abundant on the internet. However, learning dexterous manipulation policies from human videos remains challenging---one of the most important challenges being the difficulty of reconstructing physically grounded robot trajectories due to the large embodiment gap. In this paper, we propose EgoCPT, a novel paradigm for learning high-quality retargeted dexterous hand trajectories directly from an ego-centric video, which can then be deployed directly on real robots. The only inputs to our method are an ego-centric RGB video and a reference hand pose trajectory, which can be easily obtained through off-the-shelf software or specialized cameras during recording. Our method is particularly robust to noisy hand poses attributed to our novel contact-aware optimization framework, which iteratively optimizes the contact points between the robot hands and objects. Across five manipulation tasks, our method achieves overall success rates of 67.3% on noisy EgoDex hand-pose reconstructions and 92.0% on high-quality TACO reconstructions collected using motion-capture hardware, outperforming multiple baselines on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [179] [TacSpin: Tactile Tracking of In-Gripper Object Rotation on SO(3) via Axis-Magnitude Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17574)
*Yuhao Wu,Linhao Song,Huan Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacSpin 仅用两个视觉触觉传感器即可在 SO(3) 上跟踪夹爪内物体旋转，通过分离估计旋转轴与幅度并融合双指接触边界信息，实现标准物体上更低的平均检查点误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夹爪内物体运动跟踪是机器人操作的重要反馈来源，但接触覆盖有限、形变不均，使触觉估计困难；现有方法依赖最小二乘拟合刚体模型，估计精度受限。

Method: TacSpin 不采用最小二乘拟合刚体模型，而是分别估计旋转响应的轴与幅度；对每个手指的响应利用接触边界线索进行细化，再依据两指估计的一致性进行融合，从而在 SO(3) 上跟踪物体旋转。

Result: 在标准物体上，TacSpin 取得了更低的平均检查点误差；在真实物体上，利用从标准球体迁移的标定，定性地展示了不同接触几何下的跟踪效果。

Conclusion: 分离旋转轴与幅度并结合接触边界线索与双指一致性融合，是仅用两个视觉触觉传感器实现夹爪内物体旋转跟踪的有效途径，并具备向真实物体与不同接触几何迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Tracking object motion within a gripper provides essential feedback for robotic manipulation. However, limited contact coverage and uneven deformation make tactile estimation challenging. In this study, we present TacSpin, a method that tracks in-gripper object rotation on SO(3) using only two vision-based tactile sensors. The key idea is to estimate the axis and magnitude of the rotation response separately, rather than fitting a rigid-body model by least squares. Each finger's response is further refined with contact-boundary cues. The two fingers are then fused according to their agreement. On standard objects, TacSpin achieves lower mean checkpoint errors. Qualitative demonstrations on real-world objects, using calibration transferred from a standard sphere, illustrate tracking under different contact geometries.

</details>


### [180] [Training Generalist Navigation Policies via Action Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17593)
*Bowen Feng,Keqin Wang,Yulong Yang,Amogh Joshi,Christine Allen-Blanchette,Dhruv Shah,Felix Heide*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出动作自蒸馏（ASD）方法，无需人工标注语言或推理标签，利用开源VLM同时作为教师与学生策略，通过特权轨迹反馈实现语言条件化的机器人导航推理学习，在13项真实室内外导航任务上显著优于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言是人类表达与推理目标的自然接口，但训练语言条件化的机器人策略与推理通常依赖昂贵的人工标注。作者希望摆脱对人工标注语言与推理标签的依赖，实现可扩展的通用导航策略学习。

Method: ASD 使用开源 VLM 同时充当教师网络与学生策略。训练时，自教师接收特权轨迹反馈（privileged trajectory feedback），推断潜在导航目标及目标导向的推理；学生则在仅使用机载观测的条件下，以推断出的导航目标为条件，预测未来自我视角（ego-frame）轨迹，并输出场景语义、规划与推理的描述。

Result: 在13项具有挑战性的真实室内外导航任务中，该方法相比最强基线：室内场景成功率提升30%，多阶段任务提升30%，在室外任务上达到95%的成功率。

Conclusion: 特权轨迹反馈能够支持自蒸馏，从而在无需人工标注的情况下学习可扩展的通用导航策略，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Language provides a natural interface for humans to specify and reason about goals, but training language-conditioned robotic policies and reasoning typically requires expensive manual annotations. We propose Action Self-Distillation (ASD), a method for learning language-conditioned reasoning for robotic navigation without human-annotated language or reasoning labels. ASD uses an open-source VLM, which contains broad world knowledge and reasoning capabilities, as both a teacher network and a student policy. During training, the self-teacher receives privileged trajectory feedback and infers the underlying navigation goal together with goal-oriented reasoning. The student is then trained to condition on the inferred navigation goal, predict future ego-frame trajectory, and provide a description of the scene semantics, plan, and reasoning, from onboard observations alone. Across 13 challenging indoor and outdoor real-world navigation tasks, our method improves success rate over the strongest baseline by $30\%$ in indoor settings, $30\%$ on multi-stage tasks, and achieves $95\%$ success rate on outdoor tasks, validating that privileged trajectory feedback can enable self-distillation for scalable generalist navigation policies without human annotations.

</details>


### [181] [From Pixels to Executable Physics: Behavior-Grounded World Models for Embodied Action Consequence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17630)
*Ruo Chen,Jiahua Pang,Yingjie Niu,Hongyi Lin,Jun Tian,Yang Zhu,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DR-WM，一种融合机器人-地形接触动力学的物理一致世界模型，用于预测四足机器人动作后果，并在关键物理指标及动作选择任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外环境中地形与接触条件多变，相同动作指令会产生不同运动结果，可靠的导航与路径选择需要预测这些结果。现有视频世界模型虽能生成视觉上合理的未来画面，但难以刻画机器人动力学与地形交互，导致对动作结果预测过于乐观。

Method: 从初始RGB图像出发重建场景几何，并引入稠密、无量纲的地形接触属性先验，利用真实世界动作-响应数据校准；所得地形模型支持具有空间变化接触属性（如摩擦）的物理仿真。给定机器人初始状态与候选动作序列，仿真其未来运动，用生成的相机轨迹与渲染几何指导视频世界模型训练，再用生成的未来观测更新地形重建，实现无需额外真实图像的迭代式模型推演。

Result: 实验表明，DR-WM在刻画动作结果的关键物理指标上显著优于仅几何基线和统一物理参数基线，并在所评估基线中取得最佳性能。在90个挑战性场景中，配备DR-WM的8B视觉语言模型在六项评估指标中的五项上优于未辅助的32B视觉语言模型。

Conclusion: 通过纳入机器人-地形接触动力学，DR-WM实现了物理一致的未来预测，生成具有物理依据的预测结果，从而支持更明智的动作选择。

Abstract: In outdoor environments, varying terrain and contact conditions can cause identical action commands to produce different motion outcomes for quadruped robots. Predicting these outcomes is essential for reliable navigation and informed path selection. Existing video world models can generate visually plausible futures, yet often struggle to capture robot dynamics and terrain interactions, leading to overly optimistic predictions of action outcomes. We present DR-WM, a physically consistent world model that incorporates robot-terrain contact dynamics to predict the consequences of quadruped actions. Starting from an initial RGB image, our method reconstructs scene geometry and integrates dense, dimensionless priors over terrain contact properties, calibrated using real-world action-response data. The resulting terrain model supports the simulation of physics with spatially varying contact properties such as friction. Given the initial state of the robot and candidate action sequences, we simulate its future motion and use the resulting camera trajectories and rendered geometry to guide the training of a video world model. The generated future observations are then used to update the terrain reconstruction, enabling iterative model rollouts without additional real-world images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DR-WM substantially outperforms geometry-only baselines and baselines with uniform physical parameters on key physical metrics characterizing action outcomes, while achieving the best performance among the evaluated baselines. These physically grounded predictions support more informed action selection: across 90 challenging scenarios, an 8B vision-language model augmented with DR-WM outperforms an unassisted 32B vision-language model on five of six evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [182] [Acting Through Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17638)
*Yiwei Zhang,Yantai Yang,Jin Gao,Jin Lou,Andong Chen,Yufei Liu,Boyang Xing,Yuchen Zhu,Weiming Hu,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现世界动作模型(WAM)表征中存在持续的高范数空间离群点，其动作相关性在归一化与K/V投影后仍保留；提出RegisterWAM，通过可学习非空间register并结合观测流匹配(FM)监督抑制空间离群点、将高范数响应集中于register，并额外引入噪声观测FM目标，在LIBERO-Plus达81.64%、RoboTwin 2.0达93.0%，并提升真机操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型虽用生成式视觉表征来条件化机器人动作，但这些表征如何组织与动作相关的信息尚不清楚，尤其是其中的高范数空间离群点的作用与机制未被阐明。

Method: 1) 识别WAM表征中持续存在的高范数空间离群点，并验证其动作相关性在归一化与K/V投影衰减其极端幅值后仍保留；2) 提出RegisterWAM，向世界模型加入可学习的非空间register，并直接用于动作条件化；3) 仅加register无法完全消除空间离群点，故配合观测流匹配(FM)损失监督，抑制空间离群点并将高范数响应集中于register；4) 观察到无register时观测FM监督同样改善动作学习，据此增加噪声观测FM目标，学习从受损输入恢复当前场景。

Result: 在无需额外具身预训练数据或基础模型的情况下，LIBERO-Plus成功率81.64%，RoboTwin 2.0平均成功率93.0%，并提升真实世界操作性能。

Conclusion: 高范数空间离群点的动作相关性即使在幅值被衰减后仍存在，说明其编码了动作相关信息；通过register重组织并配合观测FM监督，可有效将动作相关表征从空间离群点转移到非空间register，同时改善动作学习，且该方法在仿真基准与真实操作中均有效。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) use generative visual representations to condition robot actions, yet how these representations organize action-relevant information remains poorly understood. We identify persistent high-norm spatial outliers in WAM representations and find that their action relevance persists even after normalization and K/V projection attenuate their extreme magnitude. Motivated by this observation, we introduce RegisterWAM, which adds learnable non-spatial registers to the world model and uses them directly for action conditioning. Registers alone do not fully eliminate spatial outliers; pairing them with observation flow-matching (FM) loss supervision suppresses spatial outliers while concentrating high-norm responses in registers. Beyond this reorganization, observation FM loss supervision also improves action learning without registers, motivating an additional noised-observation FM objective that learns current-scene recovery from corrupted inputs. The resulting model achieves 81.64\% success on LIBERO-Plus without additional embodied pretraining data or foundation models, reaches 93.0\% average success on RoboTwin 2.0, and improves real-world manipulation performance.

</details>


### [183] [Code2Worlds-2: Physically Plausible Agent Harness for Embodied World Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17651)
*Haoyu Zhang,Siming Li,Zeyu Zhang,Yi Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Code2Worlds-2 将 4D 世界生成升级为可执行的具身仿真，通过 SimReady 场景编译器和仿真器在环 4D 精炼器，把视觉产物转化为可被物理引擎加载并逐步求解接触的 MuJoCo 场景，并提出 SimCode4D 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码大模型虽能由自然语言合成动态 4D 世界，但结果只是视觉产物：缺少碰撞几何、质量、摩擦、惯量、关节结构等物理语义，无法驱动物理仿真；其动力学是脚本化的"看起来合理"，而非为执行而设计。因此需要一种可执行世界——物理引擎能加载并步进、能物理求解接触。

Method: 保留原有双流生成管线，并追加两个模块：(1) SimReady 场景编译器，将生成的视觉场景转为仿真器兼容表示——通过凸分解分配碰撞网格、用视觉语言模型估计物理属性、推断轻量支撑图、实例化引擎的接触/摩擦/动力学约束、执行重力沉降，最终导出为 MuJoCo MJCF；(2) 仿真器在环 4D 精炼器，实现"编译-仿真-批评-精炼"的自动智能体框架，把视觉动力学转为可执行的刚体与铰接动力学，并演化物理设置而非仅代码，用仿真器批评器替代纯视觉运动批评器，检查穿透深度、碰撞对、稳定性、接触力、关节限位与机器人可达性。

Result: 提出 SimCode4D——基于状态的基准，包含五个任务模板用于评估具身仿真。在 SimCode4D 及自然、室内、工业、城市场景与服务场景上的实验表明，Code2Worlds-2 能生成物理合理、仿真器兼容且可被机器人交互的场景。

Conclusion: Code2Worlds-2 弥合了 4D 视觉生成与物理仿真之间的鸿沟，使生成内容成为可被物理引擎加载、步进并求解接触的可执行世界，并通过仿真器在环迭代显著提升物理合理性与机器人可交互性。

Abstract: Recent coding large language models (LLMs) can synthesize dynamic 4D worlds from natural language, but the resulting content remains a visual artifact. It lacks physical semantics such as the collision geometry, mass, friction, inertia, and joint structure, required to enable it to simulate the physical world, and its dynamics are scripted to look plausible rather than designed to execute. What is needed is an executable world: one that a physics engine can load and step, resolving contact physically. We present Code2Worlds-2, an extension of Code2Worlds that turns 4D world generation into an embodied world simulation. We keep the original dual-stream generation pipeline intact and append two modules. First, a SimReady Scene Compiler that turns a generated visual scene into a simulator-compatible representation: it assigns collision meshes via convex decomposition, estimates physical attributes with a vision-language model, infers a lightweight support graph, instantiates the engine's contact, friction, and dynamics constraints, and applies a gravity settling pass, before exporting the scene as MuJoCo MJCF. Second, a Simulator-in-the-Loop 4D Refiner that implements an automated agent harness-a compile, simulate, critique and refine loop-that converts visual dynamics into executable rigid and articulated dynamics and evolves the physical settings rather than only the code, replacing the purely visual motion critic with a simulator critic that inspects penetration depth, collision pairs, stability, contact forces, joint limits, and robot reachability. We further propose SimCode4D, a state-based benchmark with five task templates for evaluating embodied simulation. Experiments on SimCode4D and across natural, indoor, industrial, urban, and service scenes show that Code2Worlds-2 produces physically plausible, simulator-compatible, and robot-interactable scenes.

</details>


### [184] [Towards a Systematic Study of Embodied System 2 over Full Task Execution Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17657)
*Yuxi Wei,Ye Fang,Shuai Yang,Tong Wu,Tai Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出SCOPER套件（含bench与harness），用于研究具身智能体在观察驱动下的规划、执行与恢复能力，并通过证据记忆与进度验证在不额外训练的情况下显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身大脑（System 2）需将抽象目标转化为动作，同时发现场景条件并检验已执行技能是否达到预期效果；但开环评估无法测试由智能体决策塑造的观测，部分闭环评估在执行过程中证据有限或假设技能结果为理想状态，缺乏对完整任务执行周期的诊断手段。

Method: 提出SCOPER套件：SCOPER-bench结合高层目标、持续视觉观测、共享的基于规则的System 1低层动作以及受控执行失败；通过构建与验证流程在BEHAVIOR和ProcTHOR上生成870个任务，并配套约3,500个与被评估智能体执行绑定的情境问题。为解决困难，SCOPER-harness将证据记忆（Evidence Memory）与进度验证（Progress Verification）与动态任务议程和有界探索相结合，无需额外模型训练。

Result: 基准结果显示智能体在保留场景证据、监控进度和从意外结果中恢复方面存在困难。在两个开源骨干模型和两个模拟器上，harness使任务成功率提升11.1-28.6%；经harness加持的9B模型在两个模拟器上均优于直接执行的27B模型。在使用200个真实机器人演示观测的离线下一动作评估中，harness还将完整调用准确率提升14.7%和15.3%。

Conclusion: SCOPER为在整个任务执行周期中诊断和改进具身推理提供了实验环境，表明无需额外训练即可通过证据记忆、进度验证与动态议程显著提升具身智能体的执行与恢复能力。

Abstract: An embodied brain, or System 2, must turn an abstract goal into actions while discovering relevant scene conditions and checking whether executed skills achieved their intended effects. Open-loop evaluations cannot test observations shaped by an agent's decisions, while some closed-loop evaluations provide limited evidence during execution or assume idealized skill outcomes. We introduce SCOPER, a suite for studying observation-grounded planning, execution, and recovery. SCOPER-bench combines high-level goals, ongoing visual observations, a shared rule-based System 1 for low-level actions, and controlled execution failures. Its construction and validation pipeline yields 870 tasks across BEHAVIOR and ProcTHOR, complemented by about 3,500 situated questions tied to the evaluated agent's execution. Benchmark results reveal difficulties in retaining scene evidence, monitoring progress, and recovering from unexpected outcomes. To address these difficulties, SCOPER-harness combines Evidence Memory and Progress Verification with a dynamic task agenda and bounded exploration, without additional model training. Across two evaluated open-source backbones and two simulators, the harness improves task success by 11.1-28.6%; a harnessed 9B model outperforms a directly executed 27B model on both simulators. In an offline next-action evaluation using observations from 200 real-robot demonstrations, the harness also improves full-call accuracy by 14.7% and 15.3%. SCOPER provides a setting for diagnosing and improving embodied reasoning throughout a complete task execution cycle.

</details>


### [185] [PrT-Flow: Probability-Tractable Flow Matching for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17667)
*Congzhong Sun,Ning Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PrT-Flow，一种概率密度可计算的可逆Flow Matching方法，通过JTT与CET实现多模态多步动作生成与精确似然，支持SAC类actor-critic训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型与Flow Matching能表达灵活的机器人动作分布，但许多强化学习算法还需要每个动作的概率（似然），而现有方法难以同时兼顾多模态多步生成与可计算概率密度。

Method: 提出PrT-Flow：用Jacobian-Tractable Transformer (JTT)使每个采样步可逆，从而高效计算Jacobian行列式与概率密度；并用Conditional Euler-step Training (CET)训练有限步，监督采样中单步位移而非瞬时速度。理论上证明在特定假设下CET与拟合Flow Matching的Euler求解器更新具有相同极小值点。

Result: 在Robomimic Lift上经300轮模仿学习达到96%成功率，与扩散策略基线持平；在OGBench Cube-Triple上经60万步在线训练达到96%成功率。

Conclusion: 具有可计算概率密度的可逆网络能够学习有效的机器人策略，并支持SAC风格actor-critic训练；代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion models and Flow Matching can represent flexible distributions over robot actions, but many reinforcement learning algorithms also require the probability of each action. We introduce PrT-Flow (Probability-Tractable Flow Matching), which computes probability density while retaining multimodal, multi-step action generation. Its Jacobian-Tractable Transformer (JTT) makes each sampling step invertible and enables efficient computation of its Jacobian determinant. We train these finite steps with Conditional Euler-step Training (CET), which supervises their displacement over one step during sampling rather than their instantaneous velocity. We prove that, under certain assumptions, CET has the same minimizers as fitting the Euler-solver updates of Flow Matching. PrT-Flow achieves 96% success on Robomimic Lift after 300 epochs of imitation learning, matching the diffusion policy reference, and 96% success on OGBench Cube-Triple after 600k online steps. These results demonstrate that invertible networks with tractable probability densities can learn effective robot policies and support SAC-style actor-critic training. Our code is available at https://github.com/Sunlight-zero/PrT-Flow .

</details>


### [186] [OccWAM: Occupancy World-Action Models with Joint Scene-to-Ego Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17688)
*Qihang Huang,Wenhui Huang,Collister Chua,Shiyu Fang,Zhan Chen,Songyan Zhang,Zhidong Wang,Daiying Tian,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 OccWAM，一种占用原生（occupancy-native）的世界-动作模型，通过流匹配联合生成稠密未来占用与连续自车轨迹，并引入"场景到自车"两阶段世界-动作流匹配，在闭环驾驶、开环规划与未来占用预测上均达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占用感知规划器通常以层级耦合（占用作为前置全预测或动作条件下的推演）或并行多任务（独立分支/辅助损失）方式连接占用预测与动作生成，这些接口保留了显式的生成顺序或角色不对称，限制了世界预测与动作生成在轨迹形成过程中的相互交互。

Method: 提出 OccWAM，以场景表示为条件，通过流匹配联合生成稠密未来占用与连续自车轨迹；进一步提出两阶段"场景到自车"世界-动作流匹配：第一阶段预测以场景为中心的占用及绝对自车轨迹，随后将同一预测世界按初始轨迹诱导的未来自车坐标系重新索引，用相邻位姿增量表示自车运动，并进行第二次联合流匹配以细化以自车为中心的占用与运动。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 与 nuScenes 上的大量实验表明，该方法在闭环驾驶、开环规划及未来占用预测三项任务上均取得 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: OccWAM 通过对称的联合世界-动作生成取代层级或并行耦合，且不同于基于视频的世界-动作模型，它保留显式语义几何以支持直接规划推理，而无需像素合成。

Abstract: Occupancy world models provide a structured, metric representation of future driving scenes, making them a promising basis for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing occupancy-aware planners typically couple occupancy prediction and action generation through either hierarchical coupling or parallel multi-task learning. Hierarchical methods use occupancy either as a fully predicted future preceding action generation or as an action-conditioned rollout for trajectory evaluation, whereas parallel multi-task methods predict occupancy alongside planning through a separate branch or auxiliary objective. These interfaces retain either an explicit generation order or a role asymmetry, limiting reciprocal interaction between world prediction and action generation during trajectory formation. We propose OccWAM, an occupancy-native world-action model that jointly generates dense future occupancy and continuous ego trajectories via flow matching conditioned on scene representations. Building on this joint formulation, we introduce scene-to-ego world-action flow matching, a two-stage formulation. The first stage predicts scene-centric occupancy together with an absolute ego trajectory, providing a stable representation of future scene evolution. The same predicted world is then re-indexed into the future ego frames induced by the initial trajectory, where ego motion is represented through adjacent pose increments and a second joint flow refines ego-centric occupancy and motion for action-aligned planning. Unlike video-based world-action models, OccWAM preserves explicit semantic geometry for direct planning reasoning without pixel synthesis. Extensive experiments on Bench2Drive and nuScenes demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in closed-loop driving, open-loop planning, and future occupancy prediction.

</details>


### [187] [HelpMeOut: A Neuropsychology-Grounded Benchmark for Error-Aware Monitoring of Household Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17694)
*Haoran Wang,Jingwei Huang,Anqing Duan,Ho Seok Ahn,Bruce Macdonald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HelpMeOut是一个面向具身AI的"错误感知监控"基准，将日常动作错误研究操作化为2万对活动片段，覆盖9类神经心理学动机的错误类别；评估显示现有监测器、符号、变点及前沿LLM提示基线表现均较差，常漏检执行错误，暴露了标准目标推断与任务完成类基准的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身家庭任务基准通常假设人类能正确执行任务，但这一假设对认知障碍者或老年人不成立——他们的日常活动会产生结构化的执行错误。为弥补该空白，需要构建能监控执行过程并诊断错误的基准。

Method: 将日常动作错误研究操作化为20000对活动片段，覆盖九类神经心理学动机的错误类别；每个片段中智能体观察流式人类活动轨迹，需监控执行是否正常，并尽早诊断结构化错误。评测了学习型监测器、符号方法、变点检测以及前沿LLM提示基线。

Result: 这些方法作为错误监测器表现都很差，常常在执行出错时漏检。

Conclusion: 结果表明标准的目标推断与任务完成基准留下了空白，HelpMeOut可作为错误感知辅助智能体的测试平台。

Abstract: We introduce HelpMeOut, an embodied AI benchmark for error-aware monitoring of household task execution. Existing embodied household benchmarks typically assume that humans execute tasks correctly, but this assumption does not hold for cognitively impaired persons or older adults whose everyday activities produce structured execution errors. To address this gap, HelpMeOut operationalizes research on everyday action errors into 20000 paired activity episodes, covering nine neuropsychologically motivated error categories. In each episode, agents observe a streaming human activity trajectory and must monitor whether the execution is unfolding correctly, diagnosing any structured errors as early as possible. We evaluate learned-monitor, symbolic, change-point, and frontier LLM prompting baselines. Results show that these methods perform poorly as error monitors, often missing when execution goes wrong. These results suggest a gap left by standard goal-inference and task-completion benchmarks, making HelpMeOut a testbed for error-aware assistive agents.

</details>


### [188] [The Human--VLA Communication Gap: What Humans Leave Implicit and What Policies Cannot Recover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17746)
*Yiqing Xu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究人类自然指令与VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型之间的"人机沟通鸿沟"，将其拆分为"信息鸿沟"（任务语义、指代、事件结构未明确）与"执行鸿沟"（任务已完全指定但组织与调用方式影响执行），通过控制变量实验在π0.5等模型上验证，发现人类倾向于压缩指令，而当前策略在语义与事件结构重建上表现薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人可能掌握任务技能，但在人类以自然方式下达指令时失败。作者旨在系统刻画人类自然表达任务目标的方式与VLA模型能力之间的错位，为以人为中心的策略学习与接口设计提供明确改进方向。

Method: 提出将human-VLA沟通鸿沟分解为信息鸿沟与残余执行鸿沟的分析框架；刻画人类自然表达已演示目标的方式，并对同一物理任务的受控变体进行评估；以π0.5为详细分析对象，并在π0-FAST与Cosmos3-Edge上验证趋势一致性。

Result: 人类随任务复杂度提升而愈发压缩指令，更依赖上下文指代与隐式执行结构；当前策略与该行为存在错位——对指代依赖容忍度较好，但在需重建任务语义或事件结构时表现吃力；即使信息鸿沟已解决，执行仍对命令粒度与调用状态敏感；π0-FAST与Cosmos3-Edge呈现一致趋势。

Conclusion: 研究识别出以人为中心策略学习与交互接口的具体改进目标，以更好地将人类自然目标转化为机器人行为。

Abstract: A robot may know how to perform a task yet fail when a human asks for it naturally. We study this human--VLA communication gap by separating an \emph{information gap}, where task semantics, grounding, or event structure remain unresolved, from a residual \emph{execution gap}, where even a fully specified task depends on how it is organized and invoked. To do this, we characterize how people naturally express demonstrated objectives and evaluate controlled variants of the same physical tasks, with detailed analysis on $π_{0.5}$ and consistent trends on $π_0$-FAST and Cosmos3-Edge. Our study reveals that humans increasingly compress instructions as tasks grow, relying more on contextual grounding and implicit execution structure. Current policies are misaligned with this behavior: they tolerate grounding dependence relatively well, but struggle when task semantics or event structure must be reconstructed. Even after the information gap is resolved, execution remains sensitive to command granularity and invocation state. These findings identify concrete targets for human-centered policy learning and interfaces that better bridge natural human objectives to robot behavior. Project website: https://commgap.github.io/.

</details>


### [189] [I3L: Iterative Interactive Imitation Learning for Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17748)
*Wensi Ai,Christopher Luey,Karthik Dharmarajan,Evans Han,Yunfan Jiang,Stef Ren,Jianwen Xie,Jiajun Wu,Li Fei-Fei,Ruohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: I3L是一套集成硬件与学习系统，通过全身纠错（JoyLo+低成本开源接口）与迭代策略精炼，缩小离线训练到可靠部署之间的"最后一公里"差距，在长时程全身移动操作任务上平均提升自主成功率68.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线从遥操作演示训练的策略在训练分布之外会退化。这一差距在长时程全身移动操作中尤为严重：长时程放大了误差累积，而手臂、躯干与底盘协调运动使穷尽式演示覆盖不现实，因此存在"最后一公里"可靠性缺口。

Method: 提出I3L集成硬件与学习系统，将全身纠错与迭代策略精炼相结合。硬件端为低成本、开源的JoyLo+接口，采用策略跟踪式leader臂与触控触发接管（touch-triggered takeover），实现跨身体各部位的协调纠错；算法端采用迭代部署与再训练，使操作者针对更新后策略的失败进行纠错（HG-DAgger式人在回路纠错）。

Result: 在三种机器人本体、八个仿真与真实任务（含四个长时程全身移动操作任务）上评估。受控仿真实验表明四轮HG-DAgger在两任务上取得最高最终成功率。该协议下，I3L相对基础策略平均提升自主成功率68.3个百分点，每个任务至少达90%成功率；在相同训练轨迹数下，比仅用演示训练平均高35.6个百分点。跨任务平均，每回合接管时长在四轮中下降72%。

Conclusion: I3L有助于弥合离线训练与可靠部署之间的最后一公里差距，并为长时程全身移动操作中纠错数据的采集与使用提供了实践性洞见。

Abstract: Policies trained offline from teleoperated demonstrations can degrade outside their training distribution. This gap is particularly acute in long-horizon whole-body mobile manipulation, where long horizons amplify compounding errors and coordinated arm, torso, and base motion makes exhaustive demonstration coverage impractical. We introduce I3L, an integrated hardware and learning system that combines whole-body correction with iterative policy refinement to address this last-mile reliability gap. Its low-cost, open-source JoyLo+ interface uses policy-tracking leader arms and touch-triggered takeover to enable coordinated corrections across the body parts, while iterative deployment and retraining allow operators to target failures of the updated policy. We evaluate I3L across three robot embodiments and eight simulated and real-world tasks, including four long-horizon whole-body mobile manipulation tasks. Controlled simulation experiments show that four-round HG-DAgger achieves the highest final success on both tasks. Under this protocol, I3L improves autonomous success over the base policies by an average of 68.3 percentage points and achieves at least 90% success on every task, outperforming demonstration-only training by 35.6 percentage points on average with the same number of training trajectories. Averaged across tasks, takeover duration per episode decreases by 72% over four rounds. Together, these results show that I3L helps close the last-mile gap between offline training and reliable deployment and provides practical insights into collecting and using corrections for long-horizon whole-body mobile manipulation. Videos, visualizations, and appendix are at https://i-3l.github.io/.

</details>


### [190] [Learning Robot Policies for Whole-Body Mobility Assistance via Dynamic Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17750)
*Shikun Ban,Shohei Nagai,Qingyang Song,Hanxi Wan,Eric Zhao,Thomas Wang,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于仿真的全身辅助机器人策略学习框架，用对抗运动先验训练的人类策略模拟被护理者反应，配合自适应脚本控制器大规模采集演示，训练3D扩散策略实现坐立与转移辅助，并初步实现仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助人类移动（如坐立STS与抬举转移L&T）可减轻护理负担，但真实人类-机器人交互数据采集成本高且涉及安全，需借助仿真；而现有工作多将人类视为静态或弱响应，无法体现移动辅助的协作本质。

Method: 构建仿真框架：核心是带对抗运动先验训练的人类策略，能对机器人动作产生真实的被护理者运动；由人类自适应的脚本控制器与该策略交互，规模化采集多样化机器人演示；再由演示训练3D扩散策略，输入为分割后的人体点云与机器人关节位置，输出移动机械臂的全身动作，部署时不依赖特权人体状态。

Result: 所得机器人策略在仿真中成功完成STS与L&T两类辅助任务；初步实验表明，在少量真实数据微调后，STS任务可实现仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: 通过引入具响应性的人类策略与大规模演示采集，仿真可有效学习全身辅助机器人策略，并具备向真实场景迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Robotic assistance for human mobility could benefit care recipients and reduce caregiver burden during physically demanding tasks such as Sit-to-Stand (STS) and Lift-and-Transfer (L&T). Learning robot policies for these whole-body assistive tasks is challenging: real-world data collection for human-robot interaction is costly and safety-critical, making simulation an attractive alternative. Prior work, however, typically treats the human as static or minimally responsive and thus fails to capture the cooperative nature of mobility assistance. We present a simulation-based framework for learning whole-body assistive robot policies. At its core is a human policy trained with an adversarial motion prior that produces realistic care-recipient motion in response to the robot's actions. A human-adaptive scripted controller interacts with this policy to collect diverse robot demonstrations at scale. From these demonstrations, we train 3D diffusion policies that map segmented human point clouds and robot joint positions to whole-body actions for a mobile manipulator, without relying on privileged human state at deployment. The resulting robot policies successfully perform both STS and L&T assistance in simulation. Preliminary experiments further demonstrate sim-to-real transfer for STS after fine-tuning on a small amount of real-world data. Videos are available on our project page: https://rchi-lab.github.io/human-gen-mobility-assistance/

</details>


### [191] [DexStitch: Learning and Composing Dexterous Skills from Human Videos via Contact-Aware Robotic Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17789)
*Byeonggyeol Choi,Seongmin Jung,Unghui Nam,Youngbo Song,Sunhong Min,MinHyung Lee,Byonghyo Shim,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexStitch 将长时程人类视频切分为技能与过渡段，转换为对齐的机器人观测-动作-技能数据，并通过技能条件策略实现技能拼接与闭环切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作任务由基本技能及其过渡组成，学习可顺序执行的技能需要同时监督技能与过渡，而机器人在真实采集此类监督成本高昂；人类视频虽可扩展，但迁移到机器人需同时提取机器人兼容的观测、动作及技能结构。

Method: 1) 技能分类器将长时程人类视频分割为操作技能与过渡；2) 转换为机器人观测与动作，同时保留技能标签；转换中利用逐指人-物接触引导灵巧手重定向与机器人图像修补，保持接触几何与手指-物体遮挡顺序；3) 共享技能条件策略先在合成数据上预训练，再在合成数据与少量单技能遥操作片段混合数据上微调；以当前与下一技能为条件引导过渡，预测技能进展实现闭环切换。

Result: 在留出会话上，分类器与人工标注在 92.4% 的帧上一致；在四项家务技能上，DexStitch 达到 80.0% 单技能成功率与 77.5% 序列成功率。

Conclusion: 通过从人类视频中提取并缝合技能与过渡，DexStitch 以低成本监督实现灵巧长时程操作，验证了技能分割、接触感知重定向与技能条件闭环策略的有效性。

Abstract: A long-horizon manipulation task consists of primitive skills and the transitions connecting them. Learning skills that can be executed in sequence requires supervision for both, which is costly to collect on a robot. Human videos offer a scalable source of demonstrations, but transferring them to robots requires extracting robot-compatible observations and actions together with their skill structure. We present DexStitch, a framework that cuts long-horizon human videos into skills and transitions, converts them into aligned robot observation-action-skill data, and stitches the learned skills into a requested sequence. A skill classifier first segments the videos into manipulation skills and transitions. DexStitch then converts the human demonstrations into robot observations and actions while retaining their corresponding skill labels. During conversion, per-finger human-object contacts guide dexterous hand retargeting and robotic inpainting, preserving contact geometry and finger-object occlusion ordering. To stitch the learned skills into a sequence, a shared skill-conditioned policy is pretrained on the resulting synthetic data and finetuned on a mixture of synthetic data and a small set of single-skill teleoperation episodes. Conditioning on the current and next skills guides each transition toward the following skill, while predicted skill progression enables closed-loop switching. On a held-out session, the classifier agrees with manual annotations on 92.4% of frames. Across four household skills, DexStitch achieves 80.0% single-skill success and 77.5% sequence success.

</details>


### [192] [Beyond Safe Execution: Safe Online Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving with Value Calibration and Adaptive Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17799)
*Zeqiao Li,Yijing Wang,Haoyu Wang,Zheng Li,Zhiqiang Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SOAR-Flow框架，用于离线初始化的Flow-based驾驶策略的安全稳定在线微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习可使驾驶策略适应部署条件，但训练全程需安全探索；仅靠安全过滤无法保证策略稳定改进或减少对修正的依赖。

Method: 采用CBF-MPC过滤器在执行前纠正不安全动作；基于Cal-QL的高斯回报感知价值校准，利用行为回报与预测回报波动平滑保守critic更新；配对纠正学习教授可行动作调整；干预-to-go预测器鼓励减少未来修正，并据此调节引导强度；配合逐渐减弱的校准正则化。

Result: 分析了控制步安全执行及辅助学习影响递减的条件；在仿真和无人地面车辆(UGV)上评估，显示驾驶性能提升且对安全修正依赖降低。

Conclusion: SOAR-Flow实现了受保护的在线微调下性能改进与安全修正依赖减少，同时保持硬性执行约束。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) enables driving policies to adapt to changing deployment conditions, but requires safe exploration throughout training. Safety filtering alone does not ensure stable policy improvement or reduced dependence on corrections. We propose Safe Online Adaptive Refinement (SOAR-Flow), a framework for safe and stable online fine-tuning of offline-initialized Flow-based driving policies. A control barrier function (CBF)--model predictive control (MPC) filter corrects potentially unsafe actions before execution. To support stable policy updates, we introduce Gaussian-return-aware value calibration based on calibrated Q-learning (Cal-QL), using recorded behavior returns and predicted return variability to smooth conservative critic updates and mitigate policy degradation. Paired corrective learning teaches feasible action adjustments, while an intervention-to-go predictor encourages decisions requiring fewer future corrections. Its predictions also regulate guidance strength, allowing recovery supervision to weaken as predicted dependence decreases. Together with gradually reduced calibration regularization, this supports continued task improvement while preserving hard execution constraints. We analyze conditions for safe execution at control steps and diminishing auxiliary learning influences. We evaluate SOAR-Flow in simulation and on an uncrewed ground vehicle (UGV). The results demonstrate improved driving performance and reduced reliance on safety corrections during protected online fine-tuning. The demonstration video and project overview are available at https://lzqw.github.io/SOAR-Flow/.

</details>


### [193] [Beyond the End-Effector: Learning Whole-Arm Contact Manipulation from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17802)
*Bo-Ruei Huang,Daniel Seita,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WACL分层框架，用三种机器人无关表示描述全臂接触任务，交由准静态表面接触优化器落地到机械臂，实现少样本学习与非预抓取式接触操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人操作多依赖末端执行器，而人类常用整条手臂接触来提升效率与适应性。让机器人具备类似能力面临三大难点：需自主选择手臂接触部位、生成有效的非抓取式交互、并满足末端位姿或安全约束。

Method: WACL采用分层框架，将全臂接触任务表示为三种机器人无关量：总结场景的语义关键点、规定按压位置与方式的参考接触轨迹、以及由视觉语言模型根据任务描述生成的安全感知路径约束。这些表示输入准静态表面接触优化器，优化器联合搜索接触的连杆、连杆上的接触位置及实现该接触的关节构型，从而将表示落到具体运动学链上。该分解使端执行器约束仅改变优化器输入，无需重训练。接触轨迹从每任务10个无遥操作的裸手人类演示中学习。

Result: 在7自由度机械臂的四个全臂任务真实实验中，WACL在端执行器约束下保持接触的表现显著优于强连杆位姿基线（成功率78% vs 33%），并能泛化到新约束而无需重训练。

Conclusion: WACL通过机器人无关表示与优化器解耦，实现了高效、可泛化的全臂接触操作，降低了对遥操作数据和重训练的依赖。

Abstract: Robot manipulation has traditionally relied on end-effector-centric interactions. In contrast, humans routinely exploit whole-arm contacts to improve efficiency, robustness, and adaptability, especially when the hands are occupied or when precise grasping is unnecessary. Enabling similar capabilities in robots remains challenging: the robot must select where on its arm to make contact, generate effective non-prehensile interactions, and satisfy task-specific pose or safety constraints on the end-effector. We propose Whole-Arm Contact Learning (WACL), a hierarchical framework that describes each whole-arm contact task with three robot-agnostic representations and hands them to a quasi-static surface-contact optimizer: semantic keypoints that summarize the scene, a reference contact trajectory that specifies where and how the arm presses on the object, and safety-aware path constraints that a vision-language model generates from the task description to protect the end-effector's task. The robot's kinematic chain enters only in this optimizer, which grounds these representations onto the arm by jointly searching over which link makes contact, where on that link, and the joint configuration that realizes it. This decomposition lets WACL learn the contact trajectory from 10 bare-hand human demonstrations per task collected without teleoperation, while new end-effector constraints change only the optimizer's input rather than any trained component. Real-world experiments on a 7-DoF arm across four whole-arm tasks show that WACL sustains contact under end-effector constraints where a strong link-pose baseline fails (78% vs. 33% success) and generalizes to new constraints without retraining. Website: https://wacl-robot.github.io

</details>


### [194] [Which Trajectory Is Safer? Combining Rule and Learning for Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17806)
*Xuewen Luo,Linlian Jiang,Bo Yu,Fan Ding,Xinxin Zuo,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FutureRisk连续安全度量，用于自动驾驶轨迹安全评估，可区分无碰撞轨迹间的风险差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞率是主流的轨迹安全评价指标，但其二元性掩盖了无碰撞轨迹之间巨大的安全差异。现有替代安全指标虽更精细，却往往在物理可解释性与对交互情境的自适应性之间难以兼顾。

Method: 提出FutureRisk，通过轨迹实际交互接近不安全状态的程度与持续性来量化潜在未来风险。核心为情境自适应风险模型（CARM），将情境相关的安全时间分布与独立监督的关键事件时间分布相结合；其安全判据以闭式物理先验为中心并受其约束，同时用有界残差实现额外自适应，保留物理锚点。

Result: 在DeepAccident与Bench2Drive上，FutureRisk与原生碰撞标签的一致性优于现有替代安全指标，能区分无碰撞轨迹间的风险，并实现跨数据集零样本迁移；对四种规划器的比较显示其能揭示二元碰撞结果所掩盖的安全差异。

Conclusion: FutureRisk是一种细粒度、可解释且情境自适应的轨迹安全评价指标。

Abstract: Collision rate remains a dominant metric for evaluating autonomous driving trajectory safety, but its binary nature obscures substantial safety differences among collision-free trajectories. Existing surrogate-safety metrics provide finer-grained assessments, yet often trade off physical interpretability against adaptivity to interaction context. We introduce FutureRisk, a continuous metric that quantifies the latent future risk of a trajectory by how closely and persistently its realized interactions approach unsafe states. At its core, our Context-Adaptive Risk Model (CARM) combines a context-dependent safety-time distribution with an independently supervised critical-event-time distribution. Its safety criterion is bounded around a closed-form physical prior, while a bounded residual provides additional adaptation without abandoning this physical anchor. Experiments on DeepAccident and Bench2Drive show that FutureRisk achieves stronger agreement with native collision labels than existing surrogate-safety measures, discriminates risk among collision-free trajectories, and transfers zero-shot across datasets. Comparisons across four planners further show that FutureRisk reveals safety differences hidden by binary collision outcomes. Together, these results establish FutureRisk as a fine-grained, interpretable, and context-adaptive metric for trajectory safety evaluation. Code is available at https://github.com/Xuewen2025/safety-evaluation.

</details>


### [195] [Physics-Aware Object-Centric SE(3) Forecasting for Real-Time Proactive Dynamic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17843)
*Yirui Zhou,Hongli Xu,Ying Zheng,Yi Wang,Lap-Pui Chau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以SE(3)接触相关刚体状态为紧凑任务充分抽象，构建"预测-选择"框架实现实时动态抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动式动态抓取要求机器人预测未来接触发生时的状态，而非仅对当前观测到的物体位姿作出反应。核心问题在于：机器人应预测何种未来、在何种抽象层次上进行预测。本文认为视觉完整的未来场景并非必要。

Method: 提出物体中心的轻量级"先预测再选择"（forecast-then-select）框架：由短时位姿历史预测未来刚体轨迹，并用可达性感知的拦截选择器在运动学与时间约束下挑选可行接触状态。基于图元形状的随机化物理仿真训练，当物体几何度量与SE(3)状态历史被恢复后即可迁移至真实物体。

Result: 仿真动态抓取平均成功率由24.5%提升至62.0%，真实世界接取、传送带与转台拦截任务由15.3%提升至67.3%；SE(3)预测器运行仅需15 ms，比稠密视觉未来预测快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 有效的主动式抓取无需设想完整未来场景；预测决定接触的紧凑物理未来，是实时动态抓取的一种有效且实用的抽象。

Abstract: Proactive dynamic grasping requires robots to predict the future state at which physical contact will occur, rather than reacting to the currently observed object pose. But what future must the robot predict, and at what level of abstraction? We argue that contact-relevant rigid-body state in SE(3) is a compact task-sufficient abstraction for this problem: it directly determines where and when the robot should establish contact, without requiring visually complete future scenes. This insight leads to a lightweight object-centric forecast-then-select framework that predicts future rigid-body trajectories from short pose histories and uses a reachability-aware interception selector to choose feasible contact states under kinematic and timing constraints. Trained with randomized physics simulation over primitive shapes, the forecaster transfers to real objects once their metric geometry and SE(3) state histories are recovered. Experiments across simulation and real-world dynamic grasping tasks show that our framework improves the average success rate from \(24.5\%\) to \(62.0\%\) in simulation, and from \(15.3\%\) to \(67.3\%\) in real-world catching, conveyor, and turntable interception. The SE(3) forecaster runs in \(15\,\mathrm{ms}\), orders of magnitude faster than dense visual future prediction, enabling real-time contact decision making. Our results suggest that effective proactive grasping does not require imagining the entire future scene. Instead, predicting the compact physical future that determines contact provides an effective and practical abstraction for real-time dynamic grasping.

</details>


### [196] [LAG-IO: Multi-IMU State Estimation for Legged Robots Using Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17870)
*Yicheng Zhou,Longwen Chen,Qinghua Yu,Junhao Xiao,Huimin Lu,Xieyuanli Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LAG-IO：基于因子图、紧耦合主体IMU/腿部多IMU/关节编码器的滑窗优化状态估计框架，结合KDE连续概率接触检测自适应调节ZUPT协方差，实现四足机器人鲁棒本体状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人本体状态估计在外部感知传感器不可靠的复杂环境中至关重要。传统腿部里程计依赖单一机体IMU与腿运动学经EKF融合，存在线性化误差与硬接触假设问题，在打滑或动态运动时产生严重漂移。

Method: LAG-IO采用因子图滑窗优化，紧耦合主体IMU、多个腿部安装IMU与关节编码器测量；引入基于核密度估计（KDE）的连续概率接触检测模块，自适应缩放零速更新（ZUPT）因子协方差；并构建运动学相对位置因子，将各腿独立惯性状态结构性地锚定到机器人主体。

Result: 在四足机器人多种非结构化地形实验中，相较现有基线，LAG-IO取得更高的平面轨迹精度，减小不可观测的竖直方向发散，在不稳定接触时轨迹过渡更平滑，同时保持有竞争力的姿态可观测性。

Conclusion: 多IMU因子图紧耦合结合连续概率接触建模，可有效提升足式机器人在打滑与动态运动等挑战场景下的本体状态估计鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: Robust proprioceptive state estimation is crucial for legged robots, particularly in challenging environments where exteroceptive sensors are unreliable. While traditional leg odometry typically relies on a single body-mounted inertial measurement unit (IMU) fused with leg kinematics via an extended Kalman filter (EKF), such methods are vulnerable to linearization errors and hard-contact assumptions, leading to severe drift during slippage or dynamic locomotion.In this paper, we propose LAG-IO, a factor graph-based multi-IMU state estimation framework that tightly couples measurements from a main body IMU, multiple leg-mounted IMUs, and joint encoders within a sliding-window optimization. To handle non-stationary foot-ground interactions, we introduce a continuous probabilistic contact detection module based on kernel density estimation (KDE), which adaptively scales the covariance of zero-velocity update (ZUPT) factors. Furthermore, kinematic relative position factors are formulated to structurally anchor the independent leg inertial states to the robot's main body. Experimental results on a quadruped robot across various unstructured terrains demonstrate that, compared to existing baselines, LAG-IO achieves higher planar trajectory accuracy, reduces unobservable vertical divergence, and provides smoother trajectory transitions during unstable contacts, while maintaining competitive attitude observability.

</details>


### [197] [Information-based Unified Design Framework for NMR Magnetic Tracking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17873)
*Seçkin Eren Yetim,Mehmet Efe Tiryaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息论的统一框架，用局部协方差分析评估NMR磁跟踪中接收器几何、标记间距与外部源配置，揭示位置与姿态跟踪精度的影响规律，并建立点偶极子源—接收器对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NMR磁跟踪是医学机器人引导中替代MRI定位的有前景方案，但标量场测量与场对齐标记力矩限制了位置和姿态跟踪，需要谨慎的构型设计，缺乏统一的分析工具。

Method: 构建基于信息（局部协方差）的统一分析框架，评估on/off-board NMR跟踪中的接收器几何、标记间距与外部源配置；通过数值评估比较不同平面阵列布局（含三角晶格阵列），并推导点偶极子源—接收器对偶性。

Result: 平面阵列精度联合依赖于接收器数量与几何；在9–20个接收器评估布局中，三角晶格阵列取得最低的最优位置不确定度；两个空间分离的场对齐标记可跟踪器件轴向，在合适标记间距下工作区中心预测角不确定度低于2°。

Conclusion: 该信息论框架统一了on/off-board NMR跟踪分析，通过源—接收器对偶性为接收器几何、标记分离与源配置提供定量设计指导，可提升磁跟踪位置与姿态精度。

Abstract: Nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) magnetic tracking offers a promising alternative to magnetic resonance imaging (MRI)-based localization for medical robotic guidance. However, scalar field measurements and field-aligned marker moments constrain position and orientation tracking, requiring careful configuration design. We present a unified information-based framework that uses local covariance analysis to evaluate receiver geometry, marker separation, and external source configuration in on/off-board NMR tracking. Numerical evaluation shows that planar-array precision depends jointly on receiver count and geometry, with triangular lattice arrays achieving the lowest best-case position uncertainty among the evaluated layouts for 9--20 receivers. Two spatially separated, field-aligned markers enable device-axis tracking, with predicted angular uncertainty below $2^\circ$ at the evaluated workspace center for suitable marker separations. We further establish point-dipole source--receiver duality, unifying the analysis of on/off-board NMR tracking. These

</details>


### [198] [Agentic Robot Creation through Generation, Verification, and Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17881)
*Xuebing Li,Yibo He,Sitong Liu,Ran Ji,Jiayuan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究AI智能体能否从高层用户意图生成可运行的机器人并教会其行为，从embodiment creation与behavior creation两方面展开：提出生成设计的计算评估指标，并提出Roboforge智能体框架，通过运动生成、策略学习与执行反馈迭代将任务规格转化为可执行策略，并在多机器人上验证及实物部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI智能体是否能端到端地将高层用户意图转化为可运行机器人并教会其行为。当前多模态模型在设计生成时对机械需求捕捉能力不明，且行为创建缺乏执行反馈闭环，因此需从embodiment creation与behavior creation两方面系统研究。

Method: 1) Embodiment creation：评估现有多模态大模型对机械需求的捕捉能力，并提出可制造前评估生成设计的计算指标；2) Behavior creation：提出Roboforge智能体框架，将任务规格经运动生成、策略学习与执行grounded修正转化为可执行策略。给定机器人embodiment与任务，智能体生成参考运动、配置学习目标并训练策略；关键是将运动保留在闭环中，用rollout反馈修正参考运动、奖励或两者。评估涉及多种MLLM生成embodiment，及四种不同机器人上的行为创建，对比reward-refinement与one-shot motion基线。

Result: 计算指标揭示了生成设计中反复出现的机械失效问题；行为实验表明基于执行的修正能提升任务级行为与执行质量。最终成功制造出一个智能体生成的Gorilla机器人，并将学习到的行为直接部署到实物机器人上。

Conclusion: AI智能体可在一定程度上去完成从高层意图到机器人embodiment生成与行为学习，但生成设计仍存在机械失效风险。保留运动闭环、基于执行反馈的迭代修正能有效提升行为性能，并实现从仿真学习到实物部署的贯通。

Abstract: Can an AI agent turn high-level user intent into a functioning robot and teach it how to behave? We study this question from two complementary aspects: embodiment and behavior creation. For embodiment creation, we investigate how well current multimodal models capture mechanical requirements, and introduce computational metrics for evaluating generated designs before fabrication. For behavior creation, we present Roboforge, an agentic framework that turns task specifications into executable policies through motion generation, policy learning, and execution-grounded revision. Given a robot embodiment and task, the agent generates a reference motion, configures the learning objective, and trains a policy. Crucially, motion remains in the loop: rollout feedback can revise the reference motion, reward, or both. We evaluate a generated embodiment across multiple MLLMs and behavior creation across four diverse robots against reward-refinement and one-shot motion baselines. Our computational metrics expose recurring mechanical failures in generated designs, while behavior experiments show that execution-grounded refinement improves task-level behavior and execution quality. Finally, we fabricate an agent-generated Gorilla and directly deploy learned behaviors on the physical robot.

</details>


### [199] [WoVR+: World Model as Reliable Simulator for Versatile VLA Policy Optimization with RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17922)
*Shangqing Zhou,Zhennan Jiang,Ning Ma,Xin Fu,Yupeng Zheng,Shuai Tian,Huangrui Li,Jiahao Liu,Chao Yu,Zhongpu Xia,Haoran Li,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WoVR+ 通过重构学习环境与想象交互协议，缓解 VLA-RL 中世界模型的幻觉与长程误差累积，在仿真和真机上显著提升成功率和长程任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型可减少 VLA-RL 中昂贵的真机交互，但其幻觉会误导策略优化。将此类方法扩展到更丰富的 VLA 接口和长程任务时，面临时空与跨模态一致性、RL 中探索-可靠性平衡以及想象 rollout 误差累积等挑战。

Method: WoVR+ 在 WoVR 的幻觉感知 WM-RL 框架基础上重新设计学习环境与想象交互协议：1）采用与 VLA 接口对齐的世界模型，提供多视角预测与本体感知更新并缓解幻觉；2）引入 chunk-wise 噪声，在保持基于流策略探索的同时限制损害预测的动作抖动；3）针对长程任务提出阶段局部想象训练，融合阶段入口重置、阶段特定奖励、局部时域与可控采样，以缩短生成前缀并在各阶段间平衡训练。

Result: WoVR+ 相比 SFT 在 RoboTwin 和 6 个真机任务上成功率分别提升 21.09% 和 16.7%；长程手机包装任务成功率从 30% 提升至 75%。

Conclusion: WoVR+ 验证了幻觉感知世界模型与阶段局部想象训练在 VLA-RL 中的有效性，可显著提升仿真及真机任务的成功率，并大幅改善长程任务表现。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models can reduce costly real-robot interaction in reinforcement learning for Vision-Language-Action models (VLA-RL), but hallucinations can mislead policy optimization. Extending them to richer VLA interfaces and long-horizon tasks raises challenges in spatiotemporal and cross-modal consistency, exploration-reliability balance in RL, and compounding rollout errors. We present WoVR+, extending WoVR's hallucination-aware WM-RL framework by redesigning the learned environment and imagined interaction protocol: A VLA-interface-aligned world model provides multi-view prediction and proprioceptive updates while mitigating hallucinations. Chunk-wise noise preserves flow-based policy exploration while limiting prediction-degrading action jitter. For long-horizon tasks, stage-local imagined training combines stage-entry resets, stage-specific rewards, local horizons, and controllable sampling to shorten generated prefixes and balance training across stages. WoVR+ improves success over SFT by 21.09% and 16.7% on RoboTwin and 6 real-robot tasks. Long-horizon phone-packaging success rises from 30% to 75%. Videos are available at https://youmo445.github.io/WoVR_Plus.github.io/.

</details>


### [200] [GLST: Global-Local Spatio-Temporal Memory Decomposition Framework for Online Chained-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17927)
*Yousef Liang,Ryan Ardalan,Yarden Goraly,Chenhao Zhu,Yue Yin,Jonas Frey,Claire Tomlin,Marco Pavone,Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在线链式目标导航问题，提出全局-局部时空信念学习（GLST）框架，通过记忆分解与目标条件奖励提升长时程导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无地图导航方法大多假设目标在单个回合内固定不变，无法适应目标在执行过程中在线变化的实际场景。在线链式目标导航中，目标可能通过已有目标重定向或新目标出现而演变，且没有显式的转换信号，此时导航可能分别需要持续探索或策略性重访，产生相互冲突的记忆需求。

Method: 提出全局-局部时空信念学习（GLST）框架，将潜在信念分解为全局-局部空间表示与长-短时间表示，以支持长时程推理；并引入目标条件奖励，在目标演变下平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在多种地形的仿真实验中，链式目标导航性能优于基线方法；使用Spot四足机器人的真实机器人实验进一步验证了记忆分解框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过记忆分解与目标条件奖励，GLST能有效应对目标在线演变下的长时程无地图导航，并在仿真与真实机器人上得到验证。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in real-world environments often requires long-horizon operation under partial observability and evolving goals. Existing mapless navigation approaches predominantly assume fixed goals throughout an episode, limiting applicability to practical scenarios where goals change online during execution. We study online chained-goal navigation, where goals evolve through existing-goal redirection or new-goal emergence without explicit transition signals. Under this setting, we find navigation may require either continued exploration or strategic revisitation, creating conflicting memory requirements. We propose Global-Local Spatio-Temporal Belief Learning (GLST), a memory-decomposition framework that separates latent beliefs into global-local spatial and long-short temporal representations for long-horizon reasoning. We further introduce goal-conditioned rewards to balance exploration and exploitation under evolving goals. Extensive experiments in simulation across diverse terrains demonstrate improved chained-goal navigation performance over baselines. Real-robot experiments using the Spot quadruped further validate our memory decomposition framework.

</details>


### [201] [Locomotion with Online Awareness of Dynamics for Precision Footholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17930)
*Kunzhao Ren,Zhishuo Zhu,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOAD 框架结合显式惯性估计与隐式动力学特征，实现负载未知条件下机器人窄梁精确行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知负载会改变机器人动力学，在落脚裕度较小时难以实现精确接触执行。为此需要一种能感知负载并适应动力学的运动控制框架。

Method: LOAD 是一个感知式运动框架：使用独立的循环模块从本体感受中推断躯干惯性变化与任务相关历史；并通过将基于高度图的教师策略蒸馏为基于深度图的学生的特权学习方式实现感知。

Result: 在仿真与硬件上跨梁宽和负载配置进行评估。隐式动力学特征可加速训练，显式惯性估计进一步提升通过成功率；二者结合使 LOAD 接近使用真值惯性参数的特权策略性能。硬件实验实现单侧 6 kg 负载通过 10 cm 窄梁、9 kg 负载通过 15 cm 窄梁，以及 15 cm 窄梁上均匀分布 11 kg 负载的通过。

Conclusion: 结果支持将物理估计与历史特征相结合，用于未知负载下的精确运动控制。

Abstract: Unknown payloads alter robot dynamics and make precise contact execution difficult when foothold margins are small. We present LOAD, a perceptive locomotion framework that combines explicit inertial estimates with implicit dynamics features for narrow-beam traversal. Separate recurrent modules infer torso inertial changes and task-relevant history from proprioception, while a height-map teacher is distilled into a depth-based student. We extensively evaluate LOAD and its variants in simulation and on hardware across beam widths and payload configurations. The results show that implicit dynamics features accelerate training, while explicit inertial estimation further improves traversal success; combining both enables LOAD to approach the performance of a privileged policy supplied with ground-truth inertial parameters. Hardware experiments demonstrate traversal with unilateral payloads of 6 kg on a 10 cm beam and 9 kg on a 15 cm beam, and an evenly distributed 11 kg payload on a 15 cm beam. These results support combining physical estimates and history features for precision locomotion under unknown loads.

</details>


### [202] [ReEnvision: Future-Guided Selective Action Repair for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17977)
*Xiyin Zeng,Xuewei Huang,Yi Lu,Weihao Yuan,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReEnvision框架，通过未来引导的选择性动作修复，仅修复受偏差影响的动作，保留有效动作，在多个仿真与真实任务上取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需要跨多个相互依赖动作的稳健执行。现有方法在出现执行偏差时，要么重新规划剩余的整个动作块（可能在不熟悉状态下引入错误、破坏原本有效动作），要么逐步纠正（反应过晚，无法显式预判偏差对后续动作的影响）。

Method: ReEnvision是一个未来引导的选择性动作修复框架：利用执行反馈更新联合的未来-动作信念，并学习该信念修正如何影响每个未执行动作，从而实现对缓存动作块中仅受影响动作的选择性修复，保留有效动作，仅在严重偏差时才进行完全重规划。

Result: 在CALVIN、L-CALVIN和RLBench上分别达到86.6%的5步成功率、将10步成功率从56%提升到63%、平均成功率88.8%；在五个真实世界操作任务上平均成功率为82.8%。受控修复实验进一步验证了在执行中定位受影响动作并保留有效动作的收益。

Conclusion: 通过基于信念修正的选择性动作修复，可在偏差发生时局部化受影响动作、保留有效动作，从而提升长时程机器人操作的执行稳健性与成功率。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation requires robust execution over sequences of interdependent actions. Existing methods typically handle execution deviations by replanning the remaining action chunk or correcting actions step by step as new observations arrive. However, a local deviation may affect only a few actions under distribution shift, yet replanning regenerates the entire chunk from an unfamiliar state, potentially introducing errors into otherwise valid actions. Step-wise correction may react too late, as it does not explicitly anticipate how the deviation affects subsequent actions. We propose ReEnvision, a future-guided selective action repair framework that updates a joint future-action belief using execution feedback. It then learns how the resulting belief revision affects each unexecuted action. The resulting belief revision enables selective repair of only the affected actions within a cached action chunk, preserving valid actions and reserving full replanning for severe deviations. On CALVIN, L-CALVIN, and RLBench, ReEnvision achieves 86.6% 5-step success, improves 10-step success from 56% to 63%, and reaches an average success rate of 88.8%, respectively. On five real-world manipulation tasks, it achieves an average success rate of 82.8%. Controlled repair experiments further demonstrate the benefits of localizing affected actions and preserving valid ones during execution.

</details>


### [203] [Breaking the Average: Overcoming Convergent Defaults in Robotic Manipulation via Diagnostic Chain-of-Thought and Structured Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17983)
*Mohammed Daba,Waleed Abdulrahman Saleh AlAkwa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需参数更新的三阶段框架，用于识别并纠正VLA模型因分布收敛产生的次优"默认行为"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型（如π_0.5）通过大规模行为克隆获得泛化能力，但受"分布收敛"影响：由于训练数据的模态平均，策略退化到"安全"但次优的均值行为，产生通用抓取策略（Convergent Defaults），在受限或长尾场景中失效。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1) Probing for Defaults，识别任务特定的模态塌缩；(2) Diagnostic Root Cause Analysis，利用VLA的潜在推理能力，以语言形式表达次优动作背后的逻辑；(3) Structured Guidance Injection，将具体且互异的替代方案编码为可复用的"Lens Prompts"，以偏移采样分布。全程不更新参数。

Result: 在ManiSkill3仿真环境中使用π_0.5进行验证；作者假设该方法优于原始π_0.5与标准Chain-of-Thought提示，能有效打破复杂操作任务中的收敛默认。

Conclusion: 该工作为VLA的分布收敛问题提供了一种免训练的参数外纠正思路，通过提示层干预改善受限与长尾操作场景中的行为多样性，其有效性尚待实验结果验证。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, such as $π_{0.5}$, have demonstrated impressive generalization capabilities by scaling behavior cloning across massive datasets. However, these models frequently suffer from distributional convergence, a phenomenon where the policy regresses to "safe" but suboptimal mean behaviors due to mode averaging in the training data. This often results in generic grasp strategies, Convergent Defaults, that fail in constrained or long-tail scenarios. In this work, we propose a novel framework to identify and correct these behaviors without parameter updates. We introduce a three-stage methodology: (1) Probing for Defaults, identifying task-specific mode collapse; (2) Diagnostic Root Cause Analysis, utilizing the VLA's latent reasoning capabilities to verbalize the logic behind suboptimal actions; and (3) Structured Guidance Injection, where concrete, distinct alternatives are encoded into reusable "Lens Prompts" that shift the sampling distribution. We validate this approach using $π_{0.5}$ in ManiSkill3 simulation environments. We hypothesize that our method outperforms vanilla $π_{0.5}$ and standard Chain-of-Thought prompting by effectively breaking convergent defaults in complex manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [204] [TRACE: Efficient Transfer Learning for Neural Whole-Body Control Across Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17999)
*Qingyu Lan,Zhihao Tian,Zifan Wang,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE 是一个高效跨本体适配框架，在保留预训练运动表示的同时将反馈控制专门化到目标机器人，以极低算力将 SONIC 控制器从 Unitree G1 迁移到 Unitree H2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形行为基础模型（BFM）支持多样化全身控制，但由于运动学与动力学失配，将预训练控制器适配到新本体仍具挑战。核心动机是在保留预训练运动表示的前提下，以低成本实现跨本体（不同体型尺度与动力学）的稳定迁移。

Method: TRACE 分两步：1）在输入/输出投影层注入运动学先验，并对齐标称关节动力学，以减少本体失配、支撑稳定微调；2）结合编码器蒸馏与低秩强化学习，在紧凑且经质量筛选的运动集上训练，采用两阶段课程——先建立稳定跟踪，再精修高难度动作。实验中用 1000 万运动帧、单张 RTX PRO 6000 GPU 约 8 小时，将 SONIC 从 Unitree G1 迁移到 Unitree H2。

Result: 在高度缩放的跟踪准则下，适配后控制器在 67,953 条评估动作上取得 96.08% 成功率；训练算力成本不足从零训练 SONIC 的 0.1% GPU 小时。真机演示涵盖动态动作跟踪与全身遥操作，所采集演示数据可支持下游视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略后训练。

Conclusion: 结果表明可跨不同体型尺度与动力学的机器人实际复用预训练人形控制器，验证了 TRACE 在跨本体适配上的高效性与实用性。

Abstract: Humanoid behavior foundation models (BFMs) enable diverse whole-body control, but adapting a pretrained controller to a new embodiment remains challenging because of kinematic and dynamic mismatch. We present TRACE, a framework for efficient cross-embodiment adaptation that preserves pretrained motion representations while specializing feedback control to the target robot.TRACE first injects kinematic priors into input and output projection layers and aligns nominal joint dynamics to reduce embodiment mismatch and support stable fine-tuning. It then combines encoder distillation with low-rank reinforcement learning on a compact, quality-screened motion set, using a two-phase curriculum to establish stable tracking before refining difficult motions. We apply TRACE to transfer SONIC from Unitree G1 to Unitree H2 using 10 million motion frames and approximately eight hours of training on a single RTX PRO 6000 GPU, at less than 0.1% of the GPU-hour cost of training SONIC from scratch. The adapted controller achieves 96.08\% success on 67,953 evaluation motions under a height-scaled tracking criterion. Real-robot demonstrations span dynamic motion tracking and whole-body teleoperation, with collected demonstrations supporting downstream vision-language-action policy post-training. These results demonstrate practical reuse of a pretrained humanoid controller across robots with different body scales and dynamics.

</details>


### [205] [Data-Free Continual Learning for World Action Model with Rectified Generative Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18038)
*Kaipeng Fang,Hangxu Liu,Chaojian Shi,Yunsong Zhou,Hongzi Zhu,Minyi Guo,Jingkuan Song,Lianli Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出R²-WAM，让世界动作模型通过自回归再生成过去任务轨迹并经过矫正式过滤实现无数据持续学习，在LIBERO与真实任务上把遗忘降低90%以上，接近需要真实数据的经验回放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于预训练视频生成模型的世界动作模型（WAM）在新机器人任务上适配快，但顺序微调时会遗忘旧任务。常规经验回放需要旧任务数据，而机器人策略通常只以权重形式部署，训练数据不随之共享，因此需要无数据的持续学习方案。

Method: 核心思路是让WAM自回归再生成（"重放自身过去"）已学任务的完整rollout，用来回放以缓解遗忘。针对两个问题——模型无法识别任务完成、完成后仍生成无目的动作，以及开环生成中动作逐渐偏离观测——作者提出矫正式生成重放R²-WAM：用agentic evaluator在任务完成处截断生成的rollout，并过滤掉动作与观测物理不一致的rollout。

Result: 在四个LIBERO套件和五个真实世界任务上，R²-WAM相对顺序微调将遗忘降低90%以上，且在不访问任何旧任务数据的情况下几乎追平经验回放。分析进一步表明，WAM的遗忘集中在逆动力学建模上：被遗忘的WAM仍能生成视觉上合理的旧任务观测，但其生成的动作失效。

Conclusion: WAM具备通过再生成自身过去实现无数据持续学习的能力，配合截断与物理一致性过滤可显著抑制遗忘；遗忘的主要瓶颈在于逆动力学（动作生成）而非观测生成，这为后续持续学习研究指明了方向。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) built on pretrained video generation models adapt quickly to new robotic tasks, but they forget previously learned tasks under sequential fine-tuning. Experience replay (ER) mitigates such forgetting by rehearsing data of previous tasks, yet this data is often unavailable in robotics, where a policy is deployed as weights only and its training data is not shared along with it. In this work, we ask whether a WAM can achieve data-free continual learning by regenerating its own past. Our key observation is that a WAM can indeed autoregressively regenerate complete rollouts of previously learned tasks and replay them to mitigate forgetting. However, replaying such raw rollouts still lags far behind replaying real data, which we attribute to two issues. First, the WAM is unable to recognize task completion and keeps generating purposeless motion after the task is done. Second, under open-loop generation, the generated actions gradually drift away from the generated observations. To address both issues, we present rectified generative replay (R$^2$-WAM), which truncates generated rollouts at task completion with an agentic evaluator and filters out rollouts whose actions are physically inconsistent with the observations. On the four LIBERO suites and five real-world tasks, R$^2$-WAM reduces forgetting by over 90% relative to sequential fine-tuning and nearly matches ER without accessing any data from previous tasks. Our analysis further reveals that forgetting in WAMs concentrates in inverse dynamics modeling: a forgotten WAM still produces visually plausible observations of previous tasks while its generated actions fail.

</details>


### [206] [Contractor Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18040)
*Simon Rohou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Contractor Filters（CFs），一种面向非线性连续时间状态估计的集合隶属架构，用 contractor 组合符号化表示可行状态，在统一代数框架下处理预测、校正、模糊性与离群值，无需预设状态几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对有界不确定性与偶发离群值下的非线性连续时间状态估计，传统集合论滤波每次更新后需重新物化几何包围集，缺乏灵活性；同时粒子滤波在持续偏差与空间混叠下可靠性下降。

Method: 以 contractor 的组合符号化表示可行状态，构建集合论滤波方程与数值实现之间的抽象层，将预测、校正、模糊性与离群值处理统一到同一代数中而不指定状态几何；推导精确标称递推与保证鲁棒包围，采用验证流包含保持可靠性；可选子铺砌压缩给出有限外表示。

Result: 在仅有距离与地形定位任务中，针对离群值、概率模型失配、关联模糊及多模态情形，与鲁棒粒子滤波对比：CFs 在所有测试配置中均保留仿真真值，而粒子滤波在持续偏差与空间混叠下可靠性退化；contractor 强度与压缩预算控制铺砌紧致度与计算成本的权衡，并保持包围保证。

Conclusion: CFs 提供了一种无需预设状态几何、具备严格包围保证的集合隶属估计框架，在鲁棒性与离群值处理上优于粒子滤波，其紧致度与计算成本可通过 contractor 强度与压缩预算调节，且不牺牲可靠性保证。

Abstract: We introduce Contractor Filters (CFs), a set-membership architecture for nonlinear continuous-time state estimation with bounded uncertainties and sporadic outliers. CFs represent feasible states symbolically by compositions of contractors rather than rematerializing a geometric enclosure after each update. This provides an abstraction layer between the set-theoretic filtering equations and their numerical realization, so prediction, correction, ambiguity, and outlier handling can be expressed within one algebra without prescribing a state geometry. We derive an exact nominal recursion and a guaranteed robust enclosure, with validated flow inclusions preserving soundness. Optional sub-paving compression then provides finite outer representations. CFs are evaluated against a robust particle filter on range-only and terrain-based localization under outliers, probabilistic model mismatch, association ambiguity, and multimodality. CFs retain the simulated truth in all tested configurations, while particle-filter reliability degrades under persistent bias and spatial aliasing. Contractor strength and compression budget control the trade-off between paving tightness and computational cost, while preserving the enclosure guarantee.

</details>


### [207] [LADDER: A Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18053)
*Petko Petkov,Giuliano Albanese,Nikolay Nikolov,Luc Van Gool,Jonathan Tremblay,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出长时序机器人操作基准LADDER，含3套件145任务（1→10子目标），评估编排范式，揭示底层策略在长时序下崩溃、编排可缓解但受执行误差限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作策略擅长短时序技能，但在长时序任务上性能骤降。现有基准因任务时序过短，或将时序与场景/物体变化耦合，无法隔离失败原因。

Method: 构建LADDER基准：在3个套件（SimplerEnv-Long、RobotArena-∞-Long、RoboLab-Long）中由1扩展至10个子目标，含145任务、8类、2种本体、每系统2024仿真回合。评估两类编排范式：面向学习型底层策略的文本子目标（VoLo）与工具选择编排（CaP-X、GPT-Policy、PSP-Agent、TiPToP）；并在两种本体上开展真机实验。

Result: 直接给定完整指令时，底层策略在单子目标任务达85.4%，扩展任务近乎归零；编排可缓解但受底层策略物理执行误差约束，仅约25%。GPT-Policy配合GPT-6 Astra的工具选择编排显著优于学习型策略方法，但时间与成本开销大，成功率约62%。真机实验证实底层策略崩溃与编排增益。

Conclusion: LADDER可有效隔离长时序失败；长时序性能瓶颈在于底层执行而非高层规划，编排能带来提升但存在上限，工具选择编排更强却代价高昂。

Abstract: General-purpose robot manipulation policies learn short-horizon skills effectively, but degrade rapidly on long-horizon tasks. Existing benchmarks cannot isolate failures because task horizon is either too short or entangled with scene and object variations. We propose LADDER, a long-horizon robot manipulation benchmark scaling from 1 to 10 subgoals within the same environment over three suites (SimplerEnv-Long, RobotArena-$\infty$-Long, and RoboLab-Long), 145 tasks, eight categories, two embodiments, and 2,024 simulated episodes per system. We evaluate two orchestration paradigms: text subgoals for learned low-level policies (VoLo) and tool selection orchestration (CaP-X, GPT-Policy, PSP-Agent, TiPToP). Given the full instruction directly, low-level policies achieve 85.4% on single-subgoal tasks, but drop to near zero on extended tasks. While orchestration remedies this, it remains bounded by physical execution errors of the low-level policy rather than high-level planning and achieves only ~25%. Tool selection orchestration with GPT-Policy on GPT-6 Astra significantly outperforms learned-policy methods, but incurs significant time and cost budget and succeeds only ~62%. Real-world experiments across two embodiments confirm the collapse of the low-level policy and the gain from orchestration. Project website: https://ladder.insait.ai

</details>


### [208] [LynxScenes: A Large-Scale Real-World Benchmark for Outdoor Wheel-Legged Robot Perception, Navigation, Imagination and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18093)
*Jun Tian,Hao Wu,Ruo Chen,Yili Liao,Hongyi Lin,Jincheng Yu,Chao Li,Yingjie Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LynxScenes是一个基于轮腿机器人采集的大规模真实世界数据集与基准，含约10万段导航片段（833.6小时、3294.7公里、300万标注帧），支持轨迹预测、BEV感知、未来场景预测与多视图重建四类任务，并提出了伪闭环评估器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外人机共享空间中运行的轮腿机器人需要精确感知、社会合规导航、未来场景推理与场景重建能力。然而现有数据集通常孤立地支持其中某一项能力，且很少在真实轮腿平台上提供密集的空间与行为级监督。

Method: 使用LYNX M20轮腿机器人采集数据，同步记录四个鱼眼相机、双LiDAR、IMU、里程计与机器人状态。标注包括27个类别共2082万个有向3D框、1389万个道路结构标注，以及分三层组织的20条导航指令并与连续未来轨迹对齐。数据集支持四项任务：指令条件下的轨迹预测、基于BEV的3D物体与道路结构感知、未来场景预测、多视图场景重建；针对轨迹预测额外引入基于日志回放的伪闭环评估器，度量安全性、进度与指令合规性。

Result: 对代表性导航、感知、世界模型与重建方法进行了广泛评估，结果显示近距离户外机器人导航仍存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: LynxScenes提供了大规模真实轮腿机器人数据集与基准，并计划公开数据集和评测工具包，以推动户外共享空间机器人导航研究。

Abstract: Wheel-legged robots operating in outdoor spaces shared with humans require accurate perception, socially compliant navigation, future-scene reasoning, and scene reconstruction. However, existing datasets typically support these capabilities in isolation and rarely provide dense spatial and behavior-level supervision on real wheel-legged platforms. We introduce LynxScenes, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark collected with a LYNX M20 wheel-legged robot. It contains approximately 100K 30-s navigation clips spanning 833.6 h and 3,294.7 km, with 3.0M annotated frames. The synchronized recordings include four fisheye cameras, dual LiDARs, IMU, odometry, and robot states. Annotations comprise 20.82M oriented 3D boxes across 27 categories, 13.89M road-structure annotations, and 20 navigation instructions organized into three layers and aligned with continuous future trajectories.
